### [1] [Exact Dynamics of Topological Order Across a CDW--SPT Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11303)
*Pradip Kattel,Yicheng Tang,Natan Andrei*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维相互作用系统从电荷密度波相到对称保护拓扑相的动力学过程，发现慢速 ramp 能建立拓扑序，而突然 quench 则因激发过多无法形成长程拓扑序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非平衡动力学中拓扑序能否在跨越相变时出现，以及涌现条件是什么。

Method: 通过酉映射到二次费米子哈密顿量解析求解，研究突然淬火和慢速 ramp 两种协议，计算 Loschmidt 回波、关联函数和 string 关联子。

Result: 突然淬火后拓扑序无法建立，慢速 ramp 则可以；Loschmidt 速率函数出现尖点标志着动力学量子相变。

Conclusion: 进入拓扑相区域本身不足以产生拓扑序，抑制演化过程中的激发产生才是关键。

Abstract: We investigate the nonequilibrium dynamics of a one-dimensional interacting system across a transition from a charge-density-wave (CDW) phase to a symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phase. Starting from a CDW initial state, we study both sudden quenches and slow ramps into the SPT regime. While the CDW order melts under both protocols, the fate of topological order is sharply different. Following a sudden quench, long-range SPT order does not emerge because the post-quench state contains a finite density of excitations above the topological ground state. In contrast, slow ramps allow the system to follow the instantaneous ground state away from the critical region, enabling the buildup of SPT order with deviations governed by Kibble-Zurek defect production. The dynamics is solvable via a unitary mapping to a quadratic fermionic Hamiltonian, allowing us to compute the Loschmidt echo, correlation functions, and string correlator. The Loschmidt rate function exhibits cusps signaling dynamical quantum phase transitions, while the correlation dynamics reveal the contrasting mechanisms governing quenches and ramps across the transition. These results demonstrate that entering the topological regime is not sufficient for the emergence of topological order; the decisive factor is the suppression of excitation production during the evolution.

</details>


### [2] [Invariants of Sequential Circuits and Generalized Non-Abelian Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11527)
*Shintaro Sato,Yoshimasa Hidaka,Ryohei Kobayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了非可逆对称性下由顺序量子电路定义的贝里相位不变量，该不变量可用于探测非可逆对称性的't Hooft反常，并应用于(3+1)D拓扑序中识别出一种新的非阿贝尔费米子环激发，其特征统计量由该不变量刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统中的非可逆对称性通常产生移动对称缺陷的顺序幺正电路，作者希望定义这些电路序列的不变量，并利用该不变量探测非可逆对称性的't Hooft反常以及识别新的拓扑激发。

Method: 定义由顺序幺正电路序列产生的贝里相位，该相位在局部变形下保持不变，从而构成不变量；利用该不变量排除短程纠缠态存在的可能性，探测非可逆对称性的反常；将该框架应用于(3+1)D拓扑序，分析环激发的统计性质。

Result: 证明贝里相位在局部变形下构成不变量；发现(3+1)D $\mathbb{D}_4$拓扑序中存在一种新的非阿贝尔费米子环激发；找到一种新的(3+1)D混合拓扑序，其长程纠缠由顺序电路不变量保护。

Conclusion: 顺序电路不变量为纯幺正算符层面的非可逆对称性反常提供了一种诊断工具，并有助于发现和刻画新的拓扑激发类型。

Abstract: Non-invertible symmetries in quantum many-body systems generally give rise to sequential unitary circuits that move symmetry defects. In this paper, we investigate invariants defined by sequences of such circuits, which move non-invertible defects and generate a Berry phase evaluated on quantum states with defects. We show that this Berry phase generally defines an invariant under local deformations, provided that the sequential circuits preserve the locality of those deformations. This invariant also rules out a short-range-entangled state that preserves the non-invertible symmetry, thereby signaling the 't Hooft anomaly of a non-invertible symmetry purely in terms of unitary operators acting on a state. We then apply this framework to loop excitations in three spatial dimensions and identify a new loop excitation in the (3+1)D $\mathbb{D}_4$ topological order, which we dub a non-Abelian fermionic loop. Using the invariant of sequential circuits, we characterize the statistics of non-Abelian fermionic loops. In addition, we find a new (3+1)D mixed topological order with a single non-Abelian fermionic loop, whose long-range entanglement is protected by an invariant of sequential circuits.

</details>


### [3] [The Kondo effect in ferromagnetic quantum critical CeRh$_6$Ge$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11978)
*Martin Sundermann,Joe D. Thompson,Eric D. Bauer,Chun Fu Chang,Sheng-Huai Chen,Chang-Yang Kuo,Liu Hao Tjeng,Getrud Zwicknagl,Andrea Severing*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过光电子能谱和X射线吸收谱研究了CeRh₆Ge₄中4f电子的电子结构，发现Kondo效应混合了高位晶场态，形成多轨道基态（4f占据数~0.9），并观测到轨道依赖的Kondo效应，为理解其压力诱导铁磁量子临界点提供了关键微观基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CeRh₆Ge₄在压力下展现出罕见的铁磁量子临界点，而干净的巡游Ce化合物通常会避免铁磁量子临界性。理解4f电子的局域与否是解释这一行为的关键，因此需要深入探究其电子结构。

Method: 采用芯能级光电子能谱和X射线吸收谱实验技术，结合线性偏振X射线吸收谱测量温度依赖的线性二色性，并利用离子全多重态模型和单杂质安德森模型（非交叉近似，SIAM/NCA）进行理论计算对比。

Result: 实验观测到与静态磁化率推断的晶场能级序列一致的温度依赖线性二色性；离子模型无法描述该二色性，而SIAM/NCA模型可复现；Kondo效应混合了高位晶场态，形成多轨道基态，4f占据数~0.9；低温下实验与计算的偏差暗示存在轨道依赖的Kondo效应。

Conclusion: 多轨道基态和轨道依赖的Kondo杂化是理解CeRh₆Ge₄中压力诱导量子临界性的重要出发点。

Abstract: The mechanism of a pressure-induced quantum critical point in the heavy fermion ferromagnet CeRh$_6$Ge$_4$ has attracted interest, as ferromagnetic quantum criticality in a clean itinerant Ce compound is typically avoided. The localized versus itinerant character of the 4\textit{f} electrons is a key aspect for understanding this behavior. We investigated the electronic structure of the 4\textit{f} shell in CeRh$_6$Ge$_4$ using core-level photoelectron and x-ray absorption spectroscopy, demonstrating the hybridization of Ce 4\textit{f} with the conduction electrons. Linearly polarized x-ray absorption reveals a temperature-dependent linear dichroism consistent with the crystal-electric-field (CEF) sequence as inferred from the static susceptibility. This dichroism cannot be described by an ionic full-multiplet model alone, but is reproduced by including the Kondo effect within a single-impurity Anderson model in the non crossing approximation (SIAM/NCA). The Kondo effect mixes higher lying crystal-field states into a resulting multiorbital ground state with 4\textit{f} occupancy \textit{n}$_f$\,$\sim$\,0.9. Deviations at low temperatures between the measured linear dichroism and calculated dichroism suggest an orbital-dependent Kondo effect. A scenario in which there is a multiorbital ground state and orbital-dependent Kondo hybridization should be a starting point for a model of pressure-induced criticality in CeRh$_6$Ge$_4$.

</details>


### [4] [Phonons and magnetism of kagome FeGe probed by nuclear resonant scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12038)
*Yu Tang,Saizheng Cao,Sijie Xu,Xiaokun Teng,Sven Velten,Ilya Sergeev,Pengcheng Dai,Yilin Wang,Yu Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过核共振散射技术研究了kagome FeGe在电荷密度波（CDW）和非公度磁有序转变中的晶格动力学和超精细相互作用，发现CDW显著硬化了声子模并影响磁性，揭示了自旋、电荷和晶格自由度之间的强耦合交织本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kagome FeGe具有2×2×2电荷密度波（CDW）且与反铁磁序存在强相互作用，为理解其中自旋、电荷和晶格自由度的耦合机制，需对CDW和磁有序转变前后的晶格动力学和磁性进行实验探测。

Method: 采用<sup>57</sup>Fe核共振散射（NRS）测量，结合第一性原理计算，获取Fe偏态声子态密度及时间域穆斯堡尔谱。

Result: 进入CDW态后，声学声子和22、27、31 meV附近光学声子发生硬化（Fe偏态声子态密度），第一性原理计算定性复现该行为；进入非公度磁有序相后，声子态密度和超精细相互作用参数无明显变化，但时域穆斯堡尔谱检测到与非公度磁有序或慢涨落相关的细微特征。

Conclusion: CDW显著改变了FeGe的晶格动力学和磁性，证明自旋、电荷和晶格自由度之间存在强烈的交织耦合。

Abstract: Kagome FeGe hosts a $2\times2\times2$ charge-density wave (CDW) that strongly interplays with antiferromagnetic order. Here, we report $^{57}$Fe nuclear resonant scattering measurement to study FeGe across its long-range CDW and incommensurate magnetic transitions. Upon entering the CDW state, hardening of acoustic phonons and optical phonons around 22~meV, 27~meV, and 31~meV are observed in the Fe partial phonon density of states, which can be qualitatively captured by first-principle calculations. Upon entering the incommensurate magnetic phase, neither the phonon density of states nor the hyperfine interaction parameters change significantly, although a subtle feature associated with the incommensurate magnetic order or slow fluctuations is detected in the time-domain Mössbauer spectra. These findings show that the CDW in kagome FeGe significantly modifies its lattice dynamics and magnetism, evidencing an intertwined nature of the spin, charge, and lattice degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [5] [From Topological Order to Mixed-State Phases: A Ground-State Probe of Fractionalized Excitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12132)
*Yunlong Zang,Yu-Bin Li,Shenghan Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出从单个基态的约化密度矩阵（RDM）中检测拓扑序的新方法：2D拓扑有序系统在纠缠切割处的RDM实现了一个1D混合态相。对于Z₂ toric code相，它是一个Z₂强到弱自发对称破缺（SW-SSB）相；扩展到Z₂自旋液体时，自旋-1/2自旋子会在θ=π处的自旋旋转无序参数上产生尖点，提供对称分数化的直接基态特征。该方法仅需单个基态波函数，适用于量子模拟平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上探测拓扑相依赖纠缠熵和纠缠谱，但约化密度矩阵本身包含更丰富的信息。本文旨在探索如何从单个基态的RDM中直接检测拓扑序和对称分数化，为实验可观测量的设计提供新途径。

Method: 将2D拓扑有序系统在纠缠切割处的RDM视为1D混合态，分析其对称性结构。利用矩阵乘积密度算符形式解析验证，并在kagome晶格格点共振价键态上进行数值验证。

Result: Z₂ toric code相的RDM对应1D Z₂ SW-SSB相，任意子的退禁闭表现为RDM中Z₂电荷和Z₂畴壁的短程关联；体态e-m对偶性转化为SW-SSB相的Kramers-Wannier自对偶性。对于Z₂自旋液体，自旋-1/2自旋子在θ=π处产生无序参数尖点，可作为对称分数化的直接基态特征。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种从单个基态波函数探测拓扑序和对称分数化的新框架，无需依赖激发态信息，易于在量子模拟平台上实现，为拓扑相的实验检测开辟了新路径。

Abstract: How do we detect topological phases from a single ground state? Entanglement entropy and spectrum have long been the standard tools -- but the reduced density matrix (RDM) itself contains far more information. We show that the RDM of a 2D topologically ordered system, expressed at the entanglement cut, realizes a 1D mixed-state phase. For the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ toric code phase, it is a 1D $\mathbb{Z}_2$ strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SW-SSB) phase, where deconfinement of anyons manifests as the short-range correlation of both $\mathbb{Z}_2$ charge and $\mathbb{Z}_2$ domain-wall in the RDM. The bulk $e$-$m$ duality translates into a Kramers--Wannier self-duality of the SW-SSB phase. Extending the framework to gapped $\mathbb{Z}_2$ spin liquids, the global spin-rotation symmetry manifests as an additional weak symmetry for the 1D RDM. Spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ spinons result in a cusp on the disorder parameter of spin-rotation at $θ=π$, providing a direct, ground-state signature of symmetry fractionalization. We verify this prediction analytically using the matrix product density operator formalism and numerically for the kagome-lattice resonating valence bond state. The proposed observable requires only a single ground-state wavefunction, making it amenable to quantum simulation platforms.

</details>


### [6] [Kinetic kagome magnetism: from self-trapping RVB polarons to semiclassical correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12204)
*Yufei Pei,Shuai A. Chen,Claudio Castelnovo,Roderich Moessner*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过在kagome晶格上研究单空穴反Nagaoka问题，揭示了空穴动力学在高度受挫掺杂Mott绝缘体中对磁性的作用。在接近完全极化时，掺杂的空穴通过结合反转自旋形成共振价键（RVB）极化子而离域化，这些极化子具有极小有效带宽并表现自陷现象。通过调节自旋极化，跟踪这些态向非极化区域的演化，观察到$\sqrt{3}\times\sqrt{3}$反铁磁关联的涌现，为理解短尺度RVB物理如何演变为长尺度常规磁关联提供了框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解空穴动力学在高度受挫掺杂Mott绝缘体中决定磁性的作用，特别关注从完全极化到非极化状态的演化过程。

Method: 在kagome晶格上研究单空穴反Nagaoka问题，以磁化作为调控参数，通过系统调节自旋极化跟踪量子态的演化。

Result: 1) 发现RVB极化子态，具有极小的有效带宽和自陷行为；2) 在非极化区域观察到$\sqrt{3}\times\sqrt{3}$反铁磁关联，类似于经典Potts和Heisenberg模型。

Conclusion: 研究结果为理解短尺度RVB物理如何演变为长尺度常规磁关联提供了理论框架，揭示了高度受挫系统中空穴动力学与磁性之间的深层联系。

Abstract: To gain deeper insight into the role of hole kinetics in determining magnetism in highly frustrated doped Mott insulators, we consider the single-hole counter-Nagaoka problem on the kagome lattice, using magnetization as a tuning parameter. Near full polarization, a doped hole delocalizes upon binding reversed spins in a pattern of singlet bonds which we term resonating-valence-bond (RVB) polaron. These RVB polarons can have extremely small effective bandwidths, and hence exhibit self-trapping. By tuning the spin polarization, we track the evolution of these states toward the unpolarized sector, where we observe the emergence of $\sqrt{3}\times\sqrt{3}$ antiferromagnetic correlation reminiscent of the classical Potts and Heisenberg models on the kagome lattice. These results provide a framework to understand how RVB physics at short scales evolves into conventional magnetic correlations at long scales.

</details>


### [7] [Structural responses incipient to pressure-driven antiferromagnetic quantum critical point of van der Waals heavy-fermion metal CeSiI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12222)
*Hanming Ma,Tong Shi,Wenhao Li,Qingxin Dong,Xiaoli Ma,Shaoheng Ruan,Zhongjin Wu,Pengtao Yang,Zhaoming Tian,Jianping Sun,Yoshiya Uwatoko,Xiaohui Yu,Hechang Lei,Bosen Wang,Jinguang Cheng*

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: CeSiI is a van der Waals heavy-fermion metal recently found to exhibit unconventional superconductivity near a pressure-induced antiferromagnetic quantum critical point (QCP) at Pc =6 GPa. Here, we report a comprehensive single-crystal X-ray diffraction study of CeSiI under high pressures up to 8.3 GPa at room temperature, revealing subtle structural responses that precede pressure-driven QCP. We find that the unit-cell volume decreases smoothly upon compression without showing any structural phase transition in the investigated pressure range. Intriguingly, we observe abrupt and concurrent anisotropic responses of the lattice parameters around Pc =6 GPa, i.e., the a-axis contracts while the c-axis enlongated suddenly, with the unit-cell volume smoothily varies with pressure. Structural refinements further show that these lattice anomalies primarily originate from changes of Ce-Ce and Ce-Si bond lengths, as well as a flattening of the inner honeycomb Si layer within the CeSiI monolayer around Pc. Our findings establish an interesting case linking pressure-driven electronic transition of QCP at low temperatures to incipient structural responses at room temperature, thereby providing fresh insight into the pressure-temperature phase diagram of CeSiI.

</details>

### [8] [Rankin--Selberg Subconvexity via Spectral Reciprocity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11451)
*Peter Humphries,Liyang Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了GL₂上酉尖自守表示对Rankin-Selberg L函数中心值L(1/2,π×π')的显式次凸界，通过发展Michel-Venkatesh谱互反框架的精细化版本，获得了比以往所有结果（包括F=ℚ情形）更优的显式界，并将其应用于CM子轨道有效等分布、全测地子流形定量等分布、数域上二面体量子唯一遍历性及尖自守表示区分等算术问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Rankin-Selberg L函数在中心点的值建立显式且更优的次凸界，以改进去的全部分数结果，并为多个算术应用提供关键工具。

Method: 基于Michel-Venkatesh的谱互反框架，发展了一种精细化的、完全显式的谱互反形式，能够精确控制导体和局部测试向量。

Result: 获得了显式次凸界，即使在F=ℚ上也优于所有已知结果；并将这些界应用于CM子轨道有效等分布、全测地子流形定量等分布、数域上二面体量子唯一遍历性的定量形式以及区分尖自守表示等算术问题。

Conclusion: 通过精细化谱互反方法，成功获得了超越以往结果的显式次凸界，并为多个重要算术问题提供了有效应用。

Abstract: We establish explicit subconvex bounds for central values of Rankin--Selberg $L$-functions $L(1/2,π\timesπ')$ associated with pairs of unitary cuspidal automorphic representations of $\mathrm{GL}_2$ over a number field. Building on the spectral reciprocity framework of Michel and Venkatesh, we develop a refined, fully explicit form of spectral reciprocity that allows for precise control of conductors and local test vectors. As a consequence, we obtain an explicit subconvex bound, which, even over $F=\mathbb{Q}$, improves all previously known results.
  We further apply these bounds to several arithmetic problems. These include effective equidistribution of CM suborbits on quaternionic Shimura varieties, quantitative equidistribution of totally geodesic submanifolds, a uniform quantitative form of dihedral quantum unique ergodicity over number fields, and an application to distinguishing cuspidal automorphic representations.

</details>


### [9] [Multivariate Period Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11706)
*Rohit Pokhrel*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对多变量周期环的研究与经典理论不够一致，对其结构的描述不够清晰，且缺少对B-可容许表示范畴的Tannakian性质的刻画，这阻碍了对多变量p进Hodge理论中表示分类的进一步理解。

Method: 提出了一种与经典理论更一致的多变量周期环研究新方法；定义了(F,G)-正则环的类似物，用于刻画B-可容许表示。

Result: 新方法更清晰地描述了多变量周期环的结构；证明了B-可容许表示范畴是G_{K,Δ}表示范畴的一个Tannakian子范畴。

Conclusion: 该工作为多变量p进Hodge理论提供了更坚实的理论基础，通过Tannakian子范畴的建立，为表示分类提供了核心工具。

Abstract: In this article, we present a new approach to studying multivariate period rings that is more consistent with classical theory and provides a clearer description of their structure. We also prove that the category of $B$-admissible representations forms a Tannakian subcategory of the category of representations of $G_{K,Δ}$ by defining an analogue of $(F,G)$-regular rings, which is central to the classification of representations in multivariate $p$-adic Hodge theory.

</details>


### [10] [The p-adic Cauchy Theorem and Overconvergent Period Sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11707)
*Finn Wiersig*

TL;DR: 本文建立了p-adic Cauchy定理在任意光滑刚性解析簇上的几何类比，证明了使用超收敛de Rham周期结构层定义的水平截面函子与Scholze使用OBdR定义的水平截面函子一致，并给出了Scholze的水平截面函子与向量丛平坦连接上D-cap-模的de Rham函子的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的p-adic Cauchy定理断言p-adic微分方程的形式解是收敛的，本文旨在将该结果推广到任意光滑刚性解析簇的几何设定中。

Method: 通过比较两种水平截面函子（基于超收敛de Rham周期结构层的函子与Scholze基于OBdR的函子），证明它们的一致性，进而将形式解的超收敛性质与Scholze构造联系起来。

Result: 证明了超收敛de Rham周期结构层定义的水平截面函子与Scholze的OBdR水平截面函子一致，即每个由Scholze构造产生的形式解已经是超收敛的。进一步将Scholze的水平截面函子等同于向量丛平坦连接上D-cap-模的de Rham函子。

Conclusion: 建立了p-adic Cauchy定理的几何类比，为p-adic微分方程在刚性解析几何中的收敛性理论提供了统一的函子性框架，并揭示了Scholze构造与经典de Rham理论之间的深层联系。

Abstract: The classical p-adic Cauchy theorem asserts that formal solutions of ordinary p-adic differential equations are convergent. In this article we establish a geometric analogue of this result for arbitrary smooth rigid-analytic varieties. More precisely, we show that the horizontal sections functor defined using the overconvergent de Rham period structure sheaf agrees with Scholze's horizontal sections functor defined using OBdR. Equivalently, every formal solution arising from Scholze's construction is already overconvergent. As an application, we identify Scholze's horizontal sections functor with the de Rham functor for D-cap-modules on vector bundles with flat connection.

</details>


### [11] [Arithmetic Symmetry in Ideal Prouhet-Tarry-Escott Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07735)
*Yu-Dai Tsai,Junseok Lee,Fuminobu Takahashi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究三次理想Prouhet-Tarry-Escott问题的对称解集，发现对称整数解可约化为双平方和方程$x^2+y^2=u^2+v^2$，并证明了对称解的数量渐近为$N_{\mathrm{sym}}(H) = \frac{4\log 2}{3π^2}H^3\log H+O(H^3)$，对数增强来自双平方和表示函数的二阶矩，表明对称解集比朴素标度预期更大，与手征规范理论中反常抵消的整数电荷谱相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受手征规范理论中整数电荷谱的反常抵消问题启发，研究三次理想Prouhet-Tarry-Escott问题的对称解集，旨在揭示其数论结构及其与物理中反常抵消的潜在联系。

Method: 利用对称性将问题约化为双平方和方程$x^2+y^2=u^2+v^2$，通过分析整数变量中双平方和表示函数的性质，采用解析数论方法（包括该表示函数的二阶矩）对对称解进行计数。

Result: 对称解的数量渐近为$N_{\mathrm{sym}}(H) = \frac{4\log 2}{3π^2}H^3\log H+O(H^3)$，其中对数因子来自双平方和表示函数的二阶矩。

Conclusion: 对称解集比朴素度加权盒计数标度$H^3$更大，识别出三次理想解空间中一个大的算术结构化子族，并暗示配对反常自由的整数电荷谱反映了基本数论结构。

Abstract: Motivated in part by anomaly cancellation for integral charge spectra in chiral gauge theory, we study the symmetric locus in the ideal degree-three Prouhet-Tarry-Escott problem. A symmetric integer solution is one whose entries are paired about a common center $c\in \frac12\mathbb Z$. This symmetry reduces the problem to a sum-of-two-squares equation, $x^2+y^2=u^2+v^2$, in integer variables, subject to the appropriate parity conditions. Thus the problem is governed by representations as sums of two squares. For the full symmetric locus, let $N_{\mathrm{sym}}(H)$ denote the number of nontrivial symmetric integer solutions of height at most $H$, counted with unordered multiset conventions and summed over the admissible centers. Then \begin{align*} N_{\mathrm{sym}}(H) = \frac{4\log 2}{3π^2}H^3\log H+O(H^3). \end{align*} The logarithmic enhancement comes from the second moment of the sum-of-two-squares representation function. In particular, the symmetric locus is larger than one would expect from the naive $H^3$ degree-weighted box-counting scale alone. This asymptotic identifies a large arithmetically structured subfamily of the ideal degree-three solution space, and suggests that paired anomaly-free integral charge spectra reflect a fundamental number-theoretic structure.

</details>

*Ji-Cai Liu,Qing-Yuan Tao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Guo提出的两个q-同余猜想：第一个关于截断Ramanujan型和的平方分圆同余（n≡1 mod 4时成立），第二个关于带参数s的截断基本超几何和模[n]的可整除性。证明分别基于Guo的Watson变换同余和周期分解与消去技巧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明Guo提出的两个q-同余猜想，完善q-模拟超同余理论，特别是在Ramanujan型发散级数超同余和Van Hamme (A.2)超同余推广方向上的未解问题。

Method: 第一个猜想的证明利用Guo已有的Watson变换同余；第二个猜想的证明基于在单位根处进行周期分解，以及在剩余块内实现反射消去。

Result: 成功证明了Guo猜想7.2（平方分圆同余，条件n≡1 mod 4）和猜想4.1（模[n]可整除性，带参数s的截断基本超几何和族）。

Conclusion: 两个q-同余猜想得证，进一步丰富了q-模拟超同余的理论工具，其中周期分解与反射消去方法可能适用于其他类似猜想。

Abstract: We prove two conjectural $q$-congruences proposed by Guo. The first is Conjecture 7.2 in Guo's work on $q$-analogues of two ``divergent'' Ramanujan-type supercongruences; it asserts a square-cyclotomic congruence for a truncated $q$-analogue of a Ramanujan-type sum when $n\equiv1\pmod4$. The second is Conjecture 4.1 in Guo's extension of Van Hamme's $(A.2)$ supercongruence; it gives divisibility modulo $[n]$ for a family of truncated basic hypergeometric sums with a parameter $s$. The proof of the first result relies on a known Watson-transformation congruence obtained by Guo. The proof of the second result is based on period decomposition at primitive roots of unity and a reflection cancellation inside residue blocks.

</details>

Conclusion: Farey三角形与p-adic Hodge理论中的基本引理之间存在结构性的对应关系，且该关系具有一般性。

Abstract: In this article, we relate any Farey triangle in the extended upper half-plane to a variant of Colmez--Fontaine's fundamental lemma in $p$-adic Hodge theory. In particular, their original fundamental lemma corresponds to the fundamental Farey triangle $(\frac{1}{0},\frac{1}{1},\frac{0}{1})$.

</details>


### [14] [Capparelli's partition theorem as part of an infinite hierarchy: Combinatorial and Weighted Words extensions of recent work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12359)
*Yazan Alamoudi,Krishnaswami Alladi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文从Capparelli定理出发，构建了一个四重无限层次的分拆定理体系。作者证明了在所有偶数阶上存在分拆定理并给出了双射证明，同时展示了四个生成函数中两个在所有阶（奇偶）上相等，并利用加权词方法为剩余两个函数构建了涵盖所有阶的通用框架，生成了具有不同扩张和平移的多个无限层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于前人提出的$q$-超几何恒等式无限层次（阶0、1、2分别对应Euler、Lebesgue、Capparelli分拆定理），作者希望探索阶4及以上偶数阶的分拆定理，揭示Capparelli定理背后更深层、更广泛的分拆结构。

Method: 采用双射证明方法验证偶数阶分拆定理；通过生成函数分析比较四个函数的相等关系；利用加权词方法（method of weighted words）构建剩余两个生成函数的通用框架，涵盖所有阶数并产生不同扩张与平移的无限层次。

Result: 在所有偶数阶上建立了分拆定理并给出双射证明；发现了一个以Capparelli定理为基的四重无限层次分拆定理体系；证明了四个生成函数中有两个在所有阶（奇偶）上相等；利用加权词方法为剩余两个函数构建了通用框架。

Conclusion: Capparelli定理不仅是单一的分拆定理，而是一个四重无限层次分拆定理体系的基例。该体系在偶数阶上具有明确的分拆定理和双射证明，且通过加权词方法可将所有阶统一纳入一个更广泛的框架中。

Abstract: In a recent paper, the authors introduced an infinite hierarchy of $q$-hypergeometric identities, of which the first three orders, $0$, $1$, and $2$, relate to the partition theorems of Euler, Lebesgue, and Capparelli, and stated a partition theorem at order 4 which lies beyond Capparelli's theorem. Here, we first state certain partition theorems that hold at all even orders beyond Capparelli and provide bijective proofs for these theorems. In doing so, we show that there is a fourfold infinite hierarchy of partition theorems that emanates from Capparelli's theorem, which is the base case. It is also shown that the equality of two of the four generating functions holds for all orders, odd and even. Lastly, a very general framework for the remaining two functions is constructed via the method of weighted words, encompassing all possible orders and yielding several infinite hierarchies with different dilations and translations.

</details>

Abstract: In this note we give a lower bound for the second moment of a ratio of zeta functions summed over the non-trivial zeros of the Riemann zeta function that is half the size of the conjectured value. Our result is conditional on the assumption of the Riemann Hypothesis and that all the non-trivial zeros of the zeta function are simple.

</details>

### [16] [MASK: Multi-Agent Semantic K-Scheduling for Risk-Sensitive 6G Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11249)
*Ahmet Gunhan Aydin,Elif Tugce Ceran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MASK架构，通过语义重要性评分调度Top-K智能体传输，在严格带宽限制下实现鲁棒的风险感知多智能体协作，性能接近无通信约束的理想基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G互联机器人需要高性能协作控制，但物理无线信道的频谱资源有限且量化为资源块/子载波，所有智能体同时传输不可行，亟需在严格瞬时带宽约束下维持鲁棒协作的通信机制。

Method: 提出Multi-Agent Semantic K-Scheduling (MASK)架构，包含Arbiter-Assisted Semantic Information Gating (A-SIG)轻量级协调机制，通过局部语义重要性评分仅调度Top-K智能体传输；聚合优先级观测为紧凑隐状态后，自监督全局编码器驱动分布性策略以缓解尾部风险。

Result: 在多种基准测试中，即使信道访问限制为群规模的一小部分，MASK仍能与无通信约束基线性能相当；且框架对数据包擦除具有内在鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 语义调度是资源受限6G系统的关键使能技术，验证了在严格带宽约束下实现高性能多智能体协作的可行性。

Abstract: Realizing the vision of 6G connected robotics requires reconciling high-performance collaborative control with the rigid spectral limitations of physical wireless channels. In realistic collaborative sensing scenarios, spectral resources are quantized into finite physical resource blocks or orthogonal subcarriers, rendering simultaneous transmission by all agents infeasible. To address this, we propose Multi-Agent Semantic K-Scheduling (MASK), a control architecture designed to sustain robust, risk-aware coordination under strict instantaneous bandwidth caps. We introduce Arbiter-Assisted Semantic Information Gating (A-SIG), a lightweight coordination mechanism that enforces hard access constraints by scheduling only the top-K agents based on locally computed semantic importance scores. By aggregating these prioritized observations into a compact latent state, a self-supervised global encoder enables a distributional policy to mitigate tail risks despite data sparsity. We evaluate MASK across diverse benchmarks, demonstrating that it matches the performance of communication-unconstrained baselines even when channel access is restricted to a small fraction of the swarm size. Furthermore, the framework exhibits inherent resilience to packet erasures, validating semantic scheduling as a critical enabler for resource-constrained 6G systems.

</details>


### [17] [Model-based Optimization of Anguilliform Swimming Gaits for Soft Robotic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11278)
*Brian Van Stratum,James Gallentine,Caleb Rucker,Eric Barth,Jonathan E. Clark,Kourosh Shoele*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种仿海鳐软体双环境机器人（SLIDER），通过Lighthill大振幅细长体理论建立流体动力学模型，结合非线性结构模型与高效隐式求解方法，经遗传算法协同优化游泳控制模式与尾鳍设计，实现了0.59 Bl/s的系留游泳速度，并探讨了水陆两栖多模态优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为软体双环境（水/陆）机器人提供一套完整的建模与优化流程，以解决流体-结构耦合作用下设计参数复杂、多模态运动难以协同优化的问题。

Method: 利用Lighthill大振幅细长体理论描述惯性效应、涡力与粘性耗散；开发快速几何与材料非线性结构模型；采用隐式二阶box方法求解流固耦合方程；结合遗传算法对游泳控制模式与尾鳍尺寸进行协同优化。

Result: 低频游泳以环境阻力主导，高频游泳受惯性流体力主导；优化后实现21.7±0.4 cm/s（0.59 Bl/s）的系留游泳速度；验证了计算与实验结果的一致性。

Conclusion: 提出了一套有效的软体双环境机器人建模与优化框架，成功实现了游泳性能的提升，并为水陆两栖多模态机器人的优化提供了参考。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the Soft Lamprey-Inspired Dual Environment Robot (SLIDER) and a proper modeling and optimization procedure employed to design the robot. We represent the primary fluid environment actions - inertial effects, vortex forces, and viscous dissipation - using Lighthill's theory for large-amplitude elongated bodies. For structural design parameters such as internal pressure, tail size, and body stiffness, a fast, geometrically and materially nonlinear model is developed and validated. The fluid-structure interaction equations are solved implicitly with an efficient second-order box method. A pneumatic manifold robotic system is employed to actuate SLIDER in a quiescent water tank environment, allowing cross-comparison of computational and experimental results. We find that low-frequency swimming is dominated by resistant environmental forces, whereas higher-frequency swimming is primarily affected by inertial fluid forces. Using our efficient model alongside a genetic algorithm, we co-optimize a swimming control pattern and caudal fin design (subject to SLIDER's climbing morphology) to achieve a tethered swimming speed of 21.7 +/- 0.4 cm/s (0.59 Bl/s). Furthermore, we investigate the optimization procedure for a multimodal robot performing both swimming and climbing tasks.

</details>


### [18] [Embodied-R1.5: Evolving Physical Intelligence via Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11324)
*Yifu Yuan,Yaoting Huang,Xianze Yao,Yutong Li,Shuoheng Zhang,Linqi Han,Pengyi Li,Jiangeng Sun,Wenting Jia,Zhao Zhang,Yuhao Liu,Ruihao Liao,Yucheng Hu,Qiyu Wu,Yuxiao Li,Zibin Dong,Fei Ni,Yan Zheng,Shuyang Gu,Yi Ma,Hongyao Tang,Han Hu,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Embodied-R1.5 是一个仅8B参数的统一具身基础模型，通过自动化数据构建（15B tokens）和多任务平衡RL训练，在24项具身VLM基准中16项达到SOTA，并可通过少量微调成为VLA模型，在4个操作基准上超越π₀.₅等领先模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身基础模型难以在单一架构内统一整合具身认知、任务规划、纠错和指向等多种推理能力，且面临异构任务冲突和数据覆盖不足的问题。

Method: 1) 三条自动化数据构建管线，生成超过15B tokens的大规模数据集；2) 多任务平衡强化学习（RL）配方缓解异构任务冲突；3) Planner-Grounder-Corrector (PGC) 闭环框架，使单一模型能够自主执行和自纠错完成长时序任务。

Result: 8B参数下，在24项具身VLM基准中16项达到SOTA，超越Gemini-Robotics-ER-1.5和GPT-5.4；微调为VLA后在4个操作基准上超越π₀.₅；零样本真实机器人实验在指令跟随、可操作性接地、铰接物体操作和长时序复杂任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: Embodied-R1.5展示了通过自动化数据构建和自纠错闭环框架实现通用物理智能的可行性，开源了模型权重、数据集、训练代码和具身评估框架EmbodiedEvalKit以推动后续研究。

Abstract: We introduce Embodied-R1.5, a unified Embodied Foundation Model (EFM) that integrates comprehensive embodied reasoning capabilities, spanning embodied cognition, task planning, correction, and pointing, within a single architecture toward general physical intelligence. Leveraging three automated data construction pipelines to significantly expand the data coverage of critical capabilities, we build a large-scale data system of over 15B tokens, and design a multi-task balanced RL recipe to alleviate heterogeneous task conflicts. We further introduce a Planner-Grounder-Corrector (PGC) closed-loop framework that enables a single model to autonomously execute and self-correct over long-horizon tasks. With only 8B parameters, Embodied-R1.5 achieves SOTA on 16 out of 24 embodied VLM benchmarks, surpassing leading models like Gemini-Robotics-ER-1.5 and GPT-5.4. Benefiting from the internalized embodied capabilities, Embodied-R1.5 can be fine-tuned into a VLA with only a small amount of data, outperforming leading VLA models like $π_{0.5}$ across 4 popular manipulation benchmark suites. We further conduct extensive zero-shot real-robot experiments, validating performance in instruction following, affordance grounding, articulated object manipulation, and long-horizon complex tasks, demonstrating strong generalization to the physical world. We open-source model weights, datasets, training code, and EmbodiedEvalKit, an evaluation framework tailored for embodied tasks, to facilitate future research in EFMs.

</details>


### [19] [HiPi: Reproducible High-Fidelity Piezoresistive Sensors for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11372)
*Changyi Lin,Raihan Haque,Hui-Ping Wang,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiPi 是一种可复现的高保真压阻式触觉传感系统，通过优化硬件堆栈（包括兼容商业制造的紧凑型PCB、低成本STM32模块、220Hz通信管道和FPCB导电层），在可复现制造与高保真读数之间架起桥梁，显著提升了接触几何重建精度（IoU从0.428提升至0.797，Dice从0.539提升至0.886），适用于双臂操作和多指机器人系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压阻式触觉传感器存在实用权衡：可复现设计强调易复制性但保真度低，高保真读数架构则制造和部署困难。作者旨在设计一个兼具可复现性、可部署性和多传感器可扩展性的高保真传感系统。

Method: 基于低串扰读数原理，HiPi重新设计了完整硬件堆栈：1) 紧凑型读取PCB，兼容商业PCB制造和组装服务，免除手动焊接；2) 更小、更低成本的STM32 MCU模块；3) 优化通信管线，在双臂四阵列（2048 taxels）下实现220 Hz读取速率；4) 基于FPCB的导电层，简化传感器制造和堆叠。

Result: 使用3D打印接触图案实验，HiPi相比可复现基线显著提升了接触几何重建精度：平均IoU从0.428提升至0.797，平均Dice得分从0.539提升至0.886。

Conclusion: HiPi有效弥合了可复现制造与高保真读数之间的差距，使密集压阻式触觉传感在双臂操作和多指机器人系统中更加实用。

Abstract: Piezoresistive tactile sensors are attractive for robotic manipulation because they are thin, lightweight, low-cost, and scalable to dense large-area sensing. However, existing systems still face a practical trade-off: recent reproducible designs emphasize accessibility and ease of reproduction, whereas high-fidelity readout architectures remain more difficult to fabricate, assemble, and deploy. We present HiPi, a reproducible high-fidelity piezoresistive sensing system for robotic manipulation. Building on a low-crosstalk readout principle, HiPi redesigns the complete hardware stack around reproducibility, deployability, and multi-sensor scalability. The system includes a compact readout PCB compatible with commercial PCB fabrication and assembly services, eliminating manual soldering; a smaller and lower-cost STM32-based MCU module; an optimized communication pipeline that achieves 220 Hz readout in a bimanual setup with four dense tactile arrays (2048 taxels in total); and FPCB-based conductive layers that simplify sensor fabrication and stacking. Experiments with structured 3D-printed contact patterns show that HiPi preserves contact geometry substantially better than a reproducible baseline, improving the average IoU from 0.428 to 0.797 and the average Dice score from 0.539 to 0.886. These results suggest that HiPi bridges an important gap between reproducible fabrication and high-fidelity readout, making dense piezoresistive tactile sensing more practical for bimanual manipulation and multi-fingered robotic systems.

</details>


### [20] [PLUME: Probabilistic Latent Unified World Modeling and Parameter Estimation for Multi-Finger Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11396)
*Abhinav Kumar,Soshi Iba,Rana Soltani Zarrin,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PLUME是一种概率潜变量世界模型，能够在线推断物理参数并联合学习系统动力学，实现模拟训练策略的零样本迁移到真实环境，在精密操作任务（如拧螺丝）中优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧手操作对物理参数（如物体形状、位姿、摩擦系数）高度敏感，而模拟训练的策略在部署时面临真实参数未知的挑战。标准域随机化策略无法满足需要根据参数值调整策略的精密操作任务（如拧螺丝）。

Method: PLUME（Probabilistic Latent Unified world Modeling and parameter Estimation）构建了一个潜空间世界模型，联合学习物理参数的后验信念演化以及基于这些参数的系统动力学，同时将奖励函数纳入潜空间以支持规划。通过在线参数推断实现世界模型与真实动力学的对齐，无需重新训练或微调。

Result: 在模拟的拧螺丝、转阀门、提桶和拨盘任务以及真实机器人拧螺丝任务上，PLUME实现了模拟训练策略的零样本迁移，性能超越了离线强化学习和世界模型增强的行为克隆基线方法。

Conclusion: PLUME通过联合学习参数信念与动力学，能高效在线对齐真实环境，为精密灵巧操作中参数不确定性下的零样本迁移提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation with multi-finger hands can be sensitive to physical parameters such as object shape, pose, and friction coefficients. While simulation enables large-scale data collection with known parameter values, simulation-trained policies must still handle uncertainty at deployment, where the true parameters and therefore the true dynamics are unknown. Standard domain randomization strategies may be insufficient for precise tasks like screwdriver turning, as manipulation strategies may need to change depending on specific parameter values. To address this, we propose Probabilistic Latent Unified world Modeling and parameter Estimation (PLUME), a world model that jointly learns to evolve a belief over parameter values as well as the system dynamics conditioned on those parameters. We learn a latent space to jointly represent multiple qualitatively different physical parameters along with rewards, themselves functions of partially-observable variables, to inform planning. Our novel learning framework leads to efficient alignment of the world model to true dynamics through online parameter inference as opposed to re-training or fine-tuning. We evaluate our method on simulated screwdriver turning, valve turning, bucket lifting, and disk flicking tasks, as well as a hardware screwdriver turning task, where we achieve successful zero-shot transfer of our simulation-trained policy and outperform state-of-the-art offline reinforcement learning and world-model-augmented behavior cloning baselines. Please see our website at https://plume-world-model.github.io for videos.

</details>


### [21] [Dynamic Execution Horizon Prediction for Chunk-based Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11408)
*Yuchi Zhao,Miroslav Bogdanovic,Arjun Sohal,Liyu Tao,Kourosh Darvish,Alán Aspuru-Guzik,Florian Shkurti,Animesh Garg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DEHP方法，通过在线强化学习为预训练的固定长度动作块策略动态预测执行步数，在保持策略冻结的同时平衡开环执行效率与闭环反应能力，显著提升高精度和长时域操作任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略中固定执行步长的开环特性在细粒度操作任务中缺乏重新规划能力，且执行步长高度依赖任务需要通过经验调参确定。

Method: 在预训练的块策略基础上附加轻量级执行步长预测分支，使用在线强化学习训练该分支，保持原策略完全冻结，实现与黑盒策略的兼容。

Result: 在多种高精度和长时域操作任务中显著提升成功率；定性分析显示细粒度阶段预测较短步长，自由空间运动预测较长步长。

Conclusion: DEHP通过动态调整执行步长，有效平衡了开环块执行效率与闭环单步控制反应性，且兼容现有黑盒块策略。

Abstract: Action chunking has become a standard design in modern robot policies, from diffusion/flow policies to vision-language-action models, where the policy predicts a sequence of actions and executes a fixed number of them instead of acting one step at a time. However, this paradigm relies on a key assumption: a fixed execution horizon. During chunk execution, the policy operates open-loop, which is particularly problematic for fine-grained manipulation tasks that require frequent replanning. In practice, the execution horizon is typically chosen through empirical tuning and is highly task-dependent. To this end, we propose Dynamic Execution Horizon Prediction (DEHP), an effective method that trains a lightweight execution-horizon prediction branch using online reinforcement learning while keeping the pretrained chunk policy completely frozen. This makes the method compatible with black-box chunk policies and isolates the effect of adapting the execution horizon from changes to the underlying action generator. Across our evaluations, DEHP improves the success rate of different high-precision and long-horizon manipulation tasks by a large margin. Our qualitative analysis further shows that DEHP predicts shorter execution horizons during fine-grained stages of the task and longer horizons during free-space motion. In this way, DEHP balances the efficiency of open-loop chunk execution with the reactivity of closed-loop single-step control. Project page: https://dehp-chunking.github.io/

</details>


### [22] [A Modular Dual-Camera Pipeline for Micro-Inspection Using Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11419)
*S. H. Mirtajadini,N. Rublein,R. M. Ramakrishnan,G. ter Maat,M. Aldibaja,A. Y. Mersha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 aerial_micro_inspection 的通用无人机微距检测管道，利用变焦云台相机和宽视场立体导航相机，通过视觉反馈回路补偿无人机运动，实现对目标表面的分区精细检测。该管道在树木害虫检测和温室粘虫板检测两个场景中验证了其鲁棒性和有效性，代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机检测系统需要无人机靠近目标或沿复杂路径飞行以获取细节，且易受扰动和定位误差影响导致丢失目标；此外，非结构化目标（如树木、车辆、人）的几何、位置和姿态信息难以预先获取，使得轨迹规划困难。本文旨在解决上述问题，提出一个无需先验信息的通用微距检测方案。

Method: 提出一个通用管道，包含：(i) 变焦云台相机用于远距离获取精细细节；(ii) 宽视场立体导航相机用于实时获取目标表面、估计距离并将表面分区；(iii) 基于视觉的反馈回路补偿无人机运动，使检测相机依次访问小型分区。采用 PX4 飞控，基于 ROS 2 开发，支持通过替换表面分割和微目标检测模块适配新应用。

Result: 仿真实验表明管道在无人机扰动下具有改进的覆盖鲁棒性；真实实验中成功检测到橡树上的栎列队蛾及其卵，以及温室粘虫板上的粉虱，成像细节丰富。

Conclusion: 提出的管道有效解决了现有无人机检测系统在近距离飞行、扰动鲁棒性和非结构化目标适配方面的局限性，具有通用性和可扩展性，开源代码便于社区复用和二次开发。

Abstract: Most existing drone-based inspection systems require the drone to fly dangerously close to the target or follow complex flight paths to capture small details. In addition, drone flight is affected by disturbances and localization inaccuracies, which can cause the drone to lose sight of its supposed target when it has a narrow view. Furthermore, trajectory planning often requires prior information about the target's geometry, position, and orientation, which is not always available for non-structural targets such as trees, vehicles, or people. To address these challenges, this paper presents aerial_micro_inspection, a generic pipeline for aerial micro-inspection across different use cases. The pipeline assumes a PX4-powered drone equipped with two cameras: (i) a zoomed, gimbal-mounted inspection camera that captures fine details without requiring the drone to fly very close to the target, and (ii) a wide-field-of-view stereo navigation camera that acquires the target surface on site, estimates its range, and partitions it into smaller inspection regions. In addition, a vision-based feedback loop compensates for drone motion while the inspection camera visits small partitions of a larger surface. We evaluate the pipeline in simulation and real-world experiments, mainly in two use-case scenarios: tree inspection for detecting oak processionary caterpillars and their eggs, and greenhouse inspection of sticky traps for detecting whiteflies. The results show improved coverage robustness under drone disturbances in simulation, as well as effective detection of caterpillars and eggs and high-detail imaging of insects in real-world experiments. The pipeline is open-source, developed in ROS 2, and can be adapted to new applications by replacing the surface-segmentation and micro-target detection checkpoints. The code is available at: https://github.com/SaxionMechatronics/aerial_micro_inspection

</details>


### [23] [Bridging the sim2real gap in the table tennis robot with a transformer-based ball states predictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11464)
*Yin Bi,Christian Conti,Bilan Yang,Alexander Sigrist,Peter Dürr,Naoya Takahashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Transformer的乒乓球状态预测框架，利用注意力机制建模长时间依赖关系，结合大规模真实世界数据集实现精准预测，并提出SPAD策略以改善仿真到真实的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乒乓球机器人需要在高速动态环境中进行闭环控制，球状态的准确快速预测至关重要。物理方法依赖精确参数辨识和初始状态，学习方法难以捕获长时间依赖且常受限于有限或仿真数据。

Method: 提出基于Transformer的框架，利用注意力机制直接从历史观测建模长时间时序相关性，无需显式飞行或弹跳模型；收集不同水平玩家和多种球炮配置的大规模真实数据集；提出Swap Predictor at Deployment (SPAD)策略，在部署时用真实世界训练的预测器替换训练时使用的物理仿真器。

Result: 高容量Transformer架构与大规模真实数据结合实现了准确的长时间预测；SPAD策略有效缩小了仿真到真实的差距，同时保持了基于仿真训练的效率和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了基于Transformer的预测框架和SPAD迁移策略在高速动态机器人控制中的有效性，无需重新训练即可提升策略的仿真到真实迁移能力。

Abstract: Robotic table tennis is a representative benchmark for high-speed, closed-loop robotic control in dynamic environments, where accurate and fast prediction of ball states is critical for reliable planning and control. Physics-based approaches rely heavily on accurate parameter identification and precise initial state, while learning-based methods often struggle to capture long-range temporal dependencies and are typically trained on limited or simulated data. We propose a transformer-based framework for table tennis ball state prediction that leverages attention mechanisms to model long-range temporal correlations directly from historical observations, without relying on explicit flight or bounce models. To support robust learning and generalization, we collected a large-scale real-world dataset from players of varying skill levels and diverse ball cannon configurations. The combination of a high-capacity transformer architecture and extensive real-world data enables accurate long-horizon forecasting. Building on this capability, we introduce a plug-and-play sim-to-real transfer strategy, Swap Predictor at Deployment (SPAD), which replaces the physics-based simulator used during training with the proposed real-world-trained predictor at deployment, improving the sim-to-real transferability of the policy without requiring retraining. We demonstrate that this simple substitution effectively narrows the sim-to-real gap while preserving the efficiency and scalability of simulation-based training.

</details>


### [24] [Steering Multirobot Behavior via Closed-Loop Affine Activation Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11489)
*Satyajeet Das,Darren Chiu,Shashank Hegde,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLAE是一种推理时框架，通过编辑冻结策略的中间激活来引导机器人行为，无需微调或重训练，避免了灾难性遗忘。在四旋翼导航任务上验证了其能控制速度、协调编队和产生新行为（如躲避监控）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实机器人需要超越预训练策略的包络适应新行为，但微调或重训练存在灾难性遗忘风险，会退化原有性能。因此需要一种不修改基础策略权重即可引导行为的方法。

Method: CLAE是一个闭环推理时框架，核心步骤包括：1) 在冻结策略的激活上训练稀疏自编码器；2) 通过事后探测选择与行为相关的潜在特征；3) 学习一个轻量级基于RL的引导策略，在推理时对选中的潜在特征施加状态依赖的仿射编辑。

Result: 在四旋翼导航任务上验证：1) 控制单个机器人速度；2) 协调多机器人保持编队；3) 产生躲避监控摄像头的新行为。均通过仿真和实物实验验证。

Conclusion: CLAE通过编辑冻结策略的中间激活实现行为引导，在不降低基策略性能的前提下实现了行为控制、协调和新行为生成，为机器人策略的灵活适应提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Real-world robots need to adapt their behavior beyond the envelope of their pre-trained policy. Policy finetuning or retraining are options, but they risk catastrophic forgetting, degrading the pretrained policy's base performance. To combat this, we introduce CLAE: Closed-Loop Affine Activation Editing, an inference-time framework for steering the behavior of a frozen policy by editing intermediate activations while keeping the base policy weights and downstream action head untouched. CLAE approaches behavior steering as a closed-loop problem whose outputs edit policy activations that adapt online to the robot state, environment, target behavior, and multi-robot context. It trains a sparse autoencoder over frozen-policy activations, selects behavior-relevant latent features via post-hoc probing, and learns a lightweight RL-based steering policy that applies state-dependent affine edits to selected latents during inference. We validate CLAE on a frozen multi-quadrotor navigation policy trained to perform a single task: navigating robots to a set of goal locations while avoiding obstacles. Through extensive simulations and physical tests, we show that while navigating to their goal positions, CLAE can 1. steer individual robot behavior by controlling each robot's velocity profile; 2. coordinate multirobot behavior by preserving a desired formation; and 3. produce entirely new behavior wherein robots are required to reduce their exposure to surveillance cameras in the environment.

</details>


### [25] [Learning Object Manipulation from Scratch via Contrastive Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11525)
*Tongle Shen,Caleb Chuck,Fan Feng,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Interaction-weighted Resampling (IWR) 方法，通过交互感知重采样技术改进对比强化学习在机器人操控任务中的表现，在模拟环境中平均提升19.8%，并在真实机器人空气曲棍球任务中将成功率从25%提升至60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比强化学习在交互密集的操控任务中表现不佳，主要原因在于物体交互（如接触、抓取）会引起动态模式的突变为分段非线性结构，标准CRL的能量函数难以表示和规划这类可达性结构。

Method: 将操控动态建模为分段平滑马尔可夫过程，提出 Interaction-weighted Resampling (IWR) 方法：在交互发生的前、中、后阶段进行交互感知重采样，促使学到的表示保留决定未来可达性的模态边界，从而捕获多模态和分段非线性可达性结构。

Result: 在2D动态控制、机器人操控和机器人空气曲棍球等交互密集环境中，IWR相比先前CRL方法在样本效率和整体性能上均有提升，模拟环境平均改进19.8%；在真实机器人空气曲棍球任务中，成功率从25%提升至60%。

Conclusion: IWR通过显式建模交互引起的动态模态变化，有效弥补了CRL在操控任务中的不足，首次实现了真实世界基于目标条件的机器人空气曲棍球智能体。

Abstract: Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (CRL) has seen recent success in a wide variety of goal-conditioned robotics tasks by learning structured representations of the dynamics. However, despite its success in locomotion and simpler control domains, CRL often struggles in interaction-rich manipulation. We argue that a key source of this difficulty is object-centric interaction, such as contact or grasping, that induces distinct changes in the underlying dynamic modes. In this work, we formulate manipulation dynamics as a piecewise-smooth Markov process and show that interaction-induced mode changes create piecewise nonlinear reachability structures that are difficult for standard CRL energy functions to represent and plan over. Based on this analysis, we introduce Interaction-weighted Resampling (IWR). IWR performs interaction-aware resampling around phases before, during, and after interactions, encouraging the learned representation to preserve the mode boundaries that determine future reachability to capture multi-modal and piecewise nonlinear reachability. Across interaction-centric environments, including 2D dynamic control, robotic manipulation, and robot air hockey, IWR improves both sample efficiency and overall performance over prior CRL methods, with 19.8% average improvement in simulation. Finally, using a sim-to-real pipeline with policies trained by IWR, we demonstrate the first real-world goal-conditioned robot air hockey agent capable of hitting goals, improving success from 25% to 60%. Project Page: IWR-arxiv.github.io.

</details>


### [26] [Adversarial Attacks on Learned Policies for Surgical Robotic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11535)
*Shutong Jin,Ziyang Chen,Preethi Satish,Paavan Gupta,Florian T. Pokorny,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究手术机器人学习策略的对抗攻击威胁，发现通过视觉扰动可使手术子任务成功率平均下降61%。提出了包括新型光度对抗攻击在内的三种攻击方法，并在清创和缝合两个子任务、三种策略架构（ACT、Diffusion Policy、Pi0）上进行了560次物理实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的策略正被用于增强手术机器人的灵巧性，但端到端的视觉-动作映射可能遭受对抗攻击，导致患者受伤。现有研究缺乏对手术机器人学习策略对抗威胁的系统性分析。

Method: 定义了两种威胁模式（破坏性攻击和引导性攻击），提出了三种访问策略信息程度递增的对抗攻击方法，并引入了一类模拟自然光照变化的光度对抗攻击。在ACT、Diffusion Policy、Pi0三种架构上，针对清创和缝合两个手术子任务进行攻击评估。

Result: 在560次物理实验中，最先进的策略受到显著干扰，手术子任务成功率平均降低61%。

Conclusion: 手术机器人学习策略对对抗攻击高度脆弱，视觉层面的微小扰动即可严重影响任务执行，这对手术机器人安全部署构成了实质性威胁。

Abstract: Learning-based policies are being considered to augment the dexterity of human surgeons in robot-assisted surgery. Can the end-to-end mapping from visual observations to robot actions be vulnerable to adversarial attacks, potentially leading to patient injury? In this paper, we present the first study of adversarial threats to learning-based policies in surgical robotics. We investigate two threat modes: (a) disruptive attacks, where imperceptible visual perturbations interrupt policy execution, and (b) steering attacks, where such perturbations steer policy actions toward attacker-specified directions. We formulate three adversarial attack methods, each with increasing access to policy information, and evaluate their impact on two surgical subtasks: debridement and suturing. Our evaluation covers three end-to-end policy architectures: ACT, Diffusion Policy, and Pi0. In addition, we introduce a new class of photometric adversarial attacks that mimic natural visual changes, such as lighting variations, to generate effective yet visually plausible perturbations. Results from 560 physical experiments using phantoms for debridement and suturing suggest that state-of-the-art policies can be significantly disrupted, resulting in an average 61% reduction in surgical subtask success rates. Project page: https://sites.google.com/view/adversary-surgery

</details>


### [27] [ConsistencyPlanner: Real-time Planning with Fast-Sampling Consistency Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11569)
*Qichao Zhang,Xing Fang,Jiaqi Fang,Zhenwen Cai,Jie Ling,Qiankun Yu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Consistency Planner 提出了一个基于快速采样一致性模型的实时闭环规划框架，通过高效多模态采样和异构特征融合，在Waymax仿真器中实现了优于现有方法的安全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶规划方法中，传统规则方法缺乏适应性，而学习方法虽具潜力但在建模多样化多模态驾驶行为与实时规划之间难以平衡，常导致决策不果断或产生不安全行为。

Method: 1) 高效多模态采样：采用快速采样一致性模型生成多样化的未来轨迹集合，实现实时多模态动作探索；2) 异构特征融合：引入注意力增强解码器，动态整合场景特征和动作令牌等异构输入特征为统一表征。

Result: 在Waymax仿真器上的广泛评估表明，该方法在安全指标上优于现有方法，尤其在具有挑战性的动态场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: Consistency Planner通过快速采样一致性模型有效解决了实时规划中多模态行为建模与计算效率的权衡问题，为复杂动态驾驶场景下的安全规划提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Closed-loop planning in complex, real-world driving scenarios presents a critical challenge for autonomous driving systems. While traditional rule-based methods are interpretable, their predefined heuristics lack the adaptability for dynamic traffic environments. Learning-based approaches have shown considerable promise. Conversely, learning-based approaches, despite their promise, struggle to balance the modeling diverse and multimodal driving behaviors and real-time planning, often leading to indecisive or unsafe actions. To address this limitation, we propose Consistency Planner, a real-time planning framework with fast-sampling consistency models. Our approach is built upon two key technical contributions. Efficient Multimodal Sampling: We employ fast-sampling consistency models to generate a diverse set of plausible future trajectories. This enables efficient, real-time exploration of multimodal actions, overcoming the computational bottlenecks of previous iterative generative methods. Heterogeneous Feature Fusion: We introduce an attention-enhanced decoder that dynamically integrates heterogeneous input features (including scene feature and action token) into a cohesive representation for robust planning. Extensive evaluation in the Waymax simulator demonstrates superior performance in safety metrics compared to existing methods, with particularly strong results in challenging dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [28] [Distortion-Resilient Robotic Imitation Learning for Autonomous Cable Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11577)
*Hao Wang,Fu-Zhao Ou,Shiqi Wang,Zhaolin Wan,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对工业中线缆布线任务中图像观测失真的问题，提出了一种包含图像质量评估模块、基于置信度的学习机制和决策模块的机器人模仿学习框架，能够在失真图像观测下保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能控制方法缺乏针对图像信号失真场景的专门解决方案，且图像质量信息未被充分利用来提升控制性能，这影响了机器人线缆布线等任务的可靠性和准确性。

Method: 提出一个新颖的机器人模仿学习框架，包含：(1) 图像质量评估模块，从图像观测中提取质量信息；(2) 基于置信度的学习机制，自适应地优先处理困难样本；(3) 决策模块，决定离散技能或连续动作。三者协同工作以应对失真图像观测。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的框架能够提升决策模块的整体性能，在图像观测失真场景下保持高水准表现。

Conclusion: 该框架通过引入图像质量评估与置信度学习机制，有效解决了智能控制中图像失真问题，为机器人灵巧操作和序列决策提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid development of intelligent control methodologies has endowed robots with powerful autonomous intelligence. Cable routing, a ubiquitous foundational task in industry, provides a rigorous benchmark for robotic dexterity and sequential decision-making. In these practical scenarios, image observation distortion frequently occurs. Samples characterized by low-quality image observations often hinder accurate model training, posing challenges to the reliability and accuracy of intelligent control systems. Nevertheless, no dedicated intelligent control solution has been proposed for scenarios of image signal distortion. Meanwhile, image quality information has not been sufficiently exploited to further enhance the performance of intelligent control methodologies. To this end, we propose a novel robotic imitation learning framework that comprises an image quality assessment module, a confidence-based learning mechanism, and a decision-making module, which is designed to maintain high performance even under distorted image observations. In the proposed framework, the image quality assessment module synergizes with the confidence-based learning mechanism to enhance the efficacy of the decision-making module. Specifically, the image quality assessment module is incorporated to extract image quality information from image observations, while the confidence-based learning mechanism adaptively prioritizes challenging samples to improve learning effectiveness. The decision-making module determines appropriate discrete skills or continuous actions. Experimental results demonstrate that our formulated framework enhances the overall performance of the decision-making module.

</details>


### [29] [LUCID: Learning Embodiment-Agnostic Intent Models from Unstructured Human Videos for Scalable Dexterous Robot Skill Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11628)
*Harsh Gupta,Guanya Shi,Wenzhen Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LUCID是一个两阶段框架，从互联网规模的非结构化人类视频中学习任务意图，然后在大规模并行仿真中学习机器人控制，通过共享的意图接口实现跨不同机器人本体（如灵巧手和平行爪夹爪）的零样本迁移，在五个真实世界操作任务上展示了仅需互联网视频监督即可完成训练的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习流水线依赖昂贵的机器人演示或结构化人类数据，且与特定本体绑定。非结构化人类视频虽然规模大且多样化，但无法直接映射到机器人动作。因此需要一种能从互联网视频中提取可迁移的任务意图，并连接机器人控制的框架。

Method: 提出LUCID两阶段框架：第一阶段从互联网规模的非结构化人类视频中学习意图模型（预测场景中短期应发生什么），该模型在闭环中根据当前观测预测短时域意图；第二阶段在大规模并行仿真中学习本体特定的感觉运动策略，将意图转换为机器人动作。意图接口作为共享中间表示，使同一意图模型可适配不同本体（灵巧手、平行爪夹爪）。

Result: 在五个真实世界操作任务上评估：搅拌、擦拭、分拣任务仅需互联网视频监督即可零样本迁移到新场景和新物体实例；推T形件和电缆布线任务各需1小时自拍手机视频监督。所有任务均成功完成。

Conclusion: LUCID证明了从互联网规模的非结构化人类视频中学习可迁移的任务意图，并结合仿真训练的机器人控制器是可行的，实现了跨本体的零样本迁移，显著降低了对昂贵机器人演示数据的依赖。

Abstract: The most widely-adopted robot learning pipelines today learn skills from robot demonstrations or structured human data, which are expensive to collect and tied to specific embodiments. In contrast, unstructured human videos provide a scalable alternative. They contain diverse manipulation demonstrations across objects, scenes, and strategies, but are not directly connected to robot action. We propose LUCID, a two-stage framework that learns task intent from unstructured human videos drawn from internet-scale datasets and learns robot control in massively-parallel simulation. The intent model predicts short-horizon intent (what should happen next in the scene) from the current observation in closed loop. An embodiment-specific sensorimotor policy converts this intent into robot actions. The intent interface is shared across controllers, so the same intent model can be applied to different embodiments, from our primary dexterous hand to a parallel-jaw gripper. We evaluate LUCID on five real-world manipulation tasks: stirring, wiping, and binning supervised by only internet video, with zero-shot transfer to novel scenes and object instances; and push-T and cable routing supervised by 1 hr each of self-collected smartphone video. Project page: https://lucid-robot.github.io/.

</details>


### [30] [SAFER-Nav: Enhancing Safety for Visual Robot Navigation via Segmentation-Aware Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11636)
*Geonyeong Ko,Giung Lee,Changjoo Nam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种导航模型，通过微调将障碍物边界和可通行自由空间结构直接融入策略中，兼容多种RGB骨干网络。在多种机器人平台和环境下，相比ViNT、NoMaD及其CARE增强变体，该方法在保持目标到达性能的同时降低了碰撞频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的导航模型（特别是基础模型）在陌生环境中泛化能力差，生成的轨迹虽能指向目标但不够安全。现有改进方法（外部轨迹校正或内部几何先验）未让策略显式学习障碍物边界或可通行空间结构。

Method: 提出一种导航模型，通过微调将障碍物边界和可通行自由空间结构直接编码到策略中，设计上与多种RGB骨干网络兼容。

Result: 在多种机器人平台、室内环境、静态和动态障碍物场景下，相比ViNT、NoMaD及其CARE增强变体，该方法在维持目标到达性能的同时有效降低了碰撞频率。

Conclusion: 显式地将障碍物边界和可通行空间结构纳入策略学习，能够在不牺牲导航目标达成能力的前提下，显著提升导航安全性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-based navigation models, particularly foundation models, generate viable trajectories from RGB observations alone. However, even state-of-the-art transformer- and diffusion-based policies struggle to generalize in unfamiliar deployment environments containing unseen obstacles or shifted conditions. The resulting trajectories often remain goal-directed but unsafe. Existing efforts improve safety through external trajectory correction or internal geometric priors, yet the resulting policies are not trained to explicitly represent obstacle boundaries or traversable free-space structure. To address this, we propose a navigation model that incorporates these structures directly into the policy via fine-tuning and is designed to be compatible with diverse RGB-based backbones. Across multiple robot platforms, indoor environments, and static and dynamic obstacle scenarios, our method reduces collision frequency relative to ViNT, NoMaD, and their CARE-augmented variants while maintaining goal-reaching performance.

</details>


### [31] [Improving Human Diving Endurance with a Field-Deployable, Untethered Exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11704)
*Zhihao Zhou,Zhenmeng Ju,Rui Yang,Chenxi Zhang,Zhihao Zhou,Ming Xu,Enhao Zheng,Dongjie Jiang,Lecheng Ruan,Jingeng Mai,Qining Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiveMate是一款可在真实水下环境中部署的无绳外骨骼，通过自适应踢腿辅助将潜水员的呼吸气体消耗效率提升42.9%，潜水时长延长54.9%，肌肉激活显著降低，证明外骨骼辅助可有效提升人类水下运动耐力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下运动中人类耐力受限于高能量消耗（克服阻力）和自持呼吸气体的有限供应，而外骨骼技术在陆地运动中被证明能降低代谢成本，但其在水下环境中增强人类耐力的潜力尚未被探索。

Method: 开发了DiveMate——一种可在真实水下环境中部署的无绳外骨骼，通过自适应踢腿辅助技术，在自然潜水条件下进行实验验证。

Result: 给定能量（呼吸气体）下旅行距离增加42.9%，潜水时长延长54.9%，气体消耗率降低47.0%，肌肉激活显著减少，运动学特征和规律性得到改善。

Conclusion: 外骨骼辅助有益于提升人类潜水耐力并增强探索水下世界的能力，该研究拓展了外骨骼的应用前沿，为未来水下辅助设备的设计与评估提供了参考。

Abstract: Human endurance in underwater locomotion is fundamentally restricted by high energetic demands to overcome drag and the finite supply of self-contained breathing gas. While exoskeleton technology can reduce the metabolic cost of humans in terrestrial locomotion, its potential to enhance human endurance during underwater diving remains entirely unexplored. Here, we present DiveMate, a field-deployable, untethered exoskeleton designed to improve human diving endurance via adaptive kick assistance in real-world underwater environments. During naturalistic diving, DiveMate increases the travel distance using a given energy (breathing gas) by 42.9% and extends dive duration by 54.9% through reducing gas consumption rate. Marked reductions in muscle activation indicate a decrease in physiological exertion, with the net gas consumption rate decreasing by 47.0%. Kinematic characteristics and regularity improvements further underpin efficient energy economy. These results suggest that applying exoskeleton assistance is beneficial for improving human diving endurance and augmenting their ability to explore the aquatic world. This study extends the application frontier of exoskeletons and provides a potential reference for the design and assessment of future underwater assistive devices.

</details>


### [32] [Explore From Sketch: Accelerating UAV Exploration in Large-scale Environments with Prior Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11708)
*Tiancheng Lai,Yuman Gao,Xiangyu Li,Ruitian Pang,Xingpeng Wang,Siqi Shen,Mengke Zhang,Yin He,Fei Gao,Chao Xu,Yanjun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用不精确的2D先验地图引导无人机进行LiDAR自主探索的框架，通过鲁棒的2D-3D点云配准和层级视点规划策略，在复杂大尺度环境中实现了高达34.2%的效率提升和37.9%的飞行距离缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模、拓扑复杂的环境中，无人机自主探索常因调度不佳和绕路导致效率低下；而先验地图（如施工图纸）虽不精确但普遍可用，具有提供全局结构引导的潜力。

Method: 提出一个两阶段框架：(1) 鲁棒的2D-3D点云配准流水线，包含GeoContext描述符用于单帧候选检索、多帧验证机制用于粗变换估计与离群点剔除、Scale-ICP算法用于精化，能处理地图偏差并提供多假设；(2) 层级视点规划策略，在定位不确定性下将局部视点附于先验引导点，使用蒙特卡洛树搜索确定遍历顺序，并通过风险感知选择器和固定端点TSP生成局部覆盖路径。

Result: 基准测试表明，相比最先进方法，探索效率提升高达34.2%，飞行距离减少37.9%；仿真与现场实验验证了对先验地图不完整和变形的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了利用稀疏、未对齐甚至不一致的2D先验地图可显著提升无人机在复杂环境中的自主探索效率，且所提框架对地图误差具有良好鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration with UAVs in large-scale, topologically complex environments often suffers from low efficiency due to suboptimal scheduling and detours. Prior maps (e.g., construction drawings), although usually imprecise and flawed, are readily available in many scenarios and have the potential to provide global structural guidance. This paper presents a novel exploration framework that leverages sparse, unaligned, and even discrepant 2D prior maps for LiDAR-based UAV exploration. First, a robust 2D-3D point cloud registration pipeline is proposed to align LiDAR observations with prior maps. The registration pipeline combines a GeoContext descriptor for single-frame candidate retrieval, a multi-frame verification mechanism for coarse transformation estimation with outlier rejection, and a Scale-ICP algorithm for refinement. The registration module can handle map discrepancies and provide multiple hypotheses when geometric ambiguities arise. To effectively utilize the registration results for exploration planning, we further develop a hierarchical viewpoint planning strategy under localization uncertainties. The hierarchical strategy first spatially attaches local viewpoints to prior guidepoints and adopts a Monte Carlo Tree Search solver to determine their traversal sequence under each registration hypothesis. To mitigate registration uncertainty, a risk-aware selector evaluates prior sequences using confidence-weighted travel risk, and a fixed-endpoint traveling salesman problem is formulated to generate an efficient local coverage path under the selected prior guidance. Benchmark evaluations reveal up to 34.2% improvement in exploration efficiency and 37.9% reduction in flight distance compared to state-of-the-art methods, while extensive simulations and field experiments further demonstrate robustness to prior map incompleteness and deformations.

</details>


### [33] [TacCoRL: Integrating Tactile Feedback into VLA via Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11743)
*Siyu Ma,Yuqi Liang,Chang Yu,Yunuo Chen,Hao Su,Yixin Zhu,Yin Yang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacCoRL通过仿真-真实协同训练和强化学习，将触觉反馈注入VLA策略，在无需大规模触觉预训练或大量真实接触探索的情况下，将双手机器人接触密集型任务的成功率从50.0%提升至72.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖视觉观察，在接触密集型任务中无法捕捉局部接触状态，导致策略在近失败状态下的动作响应不足，而收集真实接触数据成本高、风险大。

Method: 1) 使用真实对齐的仿真器作为接触交互的闭环训练环境；2) 混合仿真和真实轨迹对策略进行触觉条件动作的热启动；3) 利用可验证任务奖励的强化学习优化策略；4) 结合真实轨迹的监督目标保持策略分布锚定。

Result: 在四项双手机器人接触密集型任务中，最终视觉-触觉策略平均成功率达72.5%，基线为50.0%。

Conclusion: TacCoRL框架证明，通过仿真-真实协同训练和强化学习，可以高效地将触觉反馈注入VLA策略，显著提升接触密集型机器人操作任务的性能，且无需大规模触觉预训练或真实世界在线RL。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide strong visual, language, and action priors for robot manipulation, but visual observations alone often miss the local contact state required for contact-rich tasks. We present TacCoRL, a scalable framework that injects Tactile feedback into VLA policies and improves them through sim-real Co-training and simulation-based reinforcement learning (RL), without requiring large-scale tactile pretraining or extensive real-world contact exploration. The key idea is not only adding touch as an input, but learning how contact readings should modulate action responses in near-failure states that are rare in demonstrations and risky to collect on hardware. We use a real-aligned simulator as a closed-loop training environment for contact interaction. Mixed simulated and real trajectories first warm-start tactile-conditioned actions in the pretrained policy. Reinforcement learning with verifiable task rewards then optimizes the policy using simulated contact rollouts. It reinforces tactile-conditioned actions that lead to task completion, while a supervised objective on real trajectories keeps the refined policy anchored to deployment visual, tactile, and action distributions. The resulting policy transfers directly to the real robot without privileged simulation state or online real-world RL. Across four bimanual contact-rich tasks, the final visuo-tactile policy achieves an average success rate of 72.5%, compared to baseline of 50.0%. Result videos and more details are available at https://tac-corl.github.io/

</details>


### [34] [Blind Dexterous Grasping via Real2Sim2Real Tactile Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11767)
*Shengcheng Luo,Xiyan Huang,Zhe Xu,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合Real2Sim触觉校准、布局感知触觉编码器和触觉条件扩散策略的框架，实现了在真实多指机器人手上仅依赖触觉信号的盲抓取，在10个已知和10个未知物体上达到27%的真实世界抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手盲抓取是重要的操作能力，但真实机器人上的触觉策略学习面临触觉仿真到真实的差距以及稀疏触觉信号表达能力有限的挑战。

Method: 1) Real2Sim触觉校准管道，构建接触校准的数字孪生仿真器；2) 布局感知触觉编码器，通过自监督预训练融入传感器几何先验；3) 在校准仿真器中训练物体特定的强化学习专家，聚合成功轨迹形成触觉条件扩散策略。

Result: 在配备分布式触觉传感的LEAP Hand上，对20个物体（10个已知+10个未知）真实抓取成功率为27%。消融实验表明布局感知预训练提升性能，Real2Sim校准提高触觉接触事件的一致性。

Conclusion: 接触事件校准、几何感知触觉表示学习和扩散策略聚合为真实灵巧手触觉盲抓取提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Blind grasping with a dexterous hand is a crucial manipulation capability. Nevertheless, learning such tactile-only policies for real robots remains challenging due to the tactile sim-to-real gap and the limited expressiveness of sparse tactile signals. To bridge this gap, we propose a framework for tactile-only blind grasping that is deployable on a physical multi-fingered robotic hand. Our approach combines three key components. First, we introduce a Real2Sim tactile calibration pipeline that constructs a contact-calibrated digital-twin simulator capable of reproducing real tactile signals. Second, we improve the expressiveness of sparse tactile observations using a layout-aware tactile encoder, which incorporates sensor-geometry priors through self-supervised pretraining. Third, to improve generalization to unseen objects, we train object-specific reinforcement-learning experts in the calibrated simulator and aggregate their successful grasp trajectories into a tactile-conditioned Diffusion Policy. We evaluate our method on a physical LEAP Hand equipped with distributed tactile sensing across 10 seen and 10 unseen objects. The deployed policy achieves a 27\% real-world grasp success rate across all 20 objects, without real-world grasping demonstrations or visual input. Simulation ablations show that layout-aware tactile pretraining improves grasping performance, while sensing-level evaluations confirm that Real2Sim calibration increases the consistency of tactile contact events between simulation and hardware. Together, these results suggest that contact-event calibration, geometry-aware tactile representation learning, and diffusion-based policy aggregation provide an effective path toward tactile-only blind grasping on real dexterous robotic hands. Project page:Dex-Blind-Grasp.github.io.

</details>


### [35] [Human-Guided Co-Manipulation of Carbon Fiber Plies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11818)
*Rami Ojanen,James Fant-Male,Roel Pieters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了碳纤维铺层人机协作操控中的不同控制方法，提出结合语音指令、视觉手腕跟踪和力控柔顺控制的多模态方案，以实现完整直观的任务控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性材料因可变形性难以实现全自动化，而纯人工操作存在人体工学挑战和效率低下问题，因此需要人机协作以提高生产效率和灵活性。

Method: 在受控环境下评估多种协作控制方法，比较其优缺点，并提出语音指令、视觉手腕跟踪与力/柔顺控制相结合的多模态方案。

Result: 多模态组合方法（语音+视觉跟踪+力控柔顺控制）被认为是完成碳纤维铺层任务最完整且直观的控制方案。

Conclusion: 多模态融合控制策略能有效平衡自动化与人工参与的优点，为柔性材料的人机协作操控提供最佳解决方案。

Abstract: The handling of flexible materials is a difficult task to fully automate due to the challenges caused by the deformability of these types of objects. Meanwhile, a fully manual process can be ergonomically challenging, tedious and inefficient. Thus, human-robot collaboration (HRC) and cooperative manipulation (co-manipulation) have received increasing interest in this field as they enable human involvement when needed while also improving productivity. To enable efficient co-manipulation and interaction between the human operator and the robot, different modalities and control methods are required. In this paper, we present and examine different control methods for co-manipulation of carbon fiber plies, evaluating the pros and cons of each method in a controlled setting. We propose that a multimodal combination of speech commands, wrist-tracking through vision, and force with compliant control would provide the best solution for complete and intuitive control of the task.

</details>


### [36] [Modular Anthropomorphic Hand Design via Multi-Parameter Finger Benchmarking and Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11826)
*Yu Zhang,Huijiang Wang,Josie Hughes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过模块化手指优化来提升拟人化灵巧手整体性能的框架，该框架使用机制导向和任务相关指标对候选手指设计（关节、骨骼、皮肤、传感器布局）进行定量评估，并通过多物体抓取和灯泡旋拧等任务验证了优化手指带来的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟人化灵巧手的设计空间涉及形态、驱动和传感等多个维度，现有优化方法往往缺乏结构或仅考虑单一性能指标，无法进行系统比较和针对性改进，而手指特性对灵巧性至关重要。

Method: 开发了一个手指可模块化集成到完整遥操作手的机器人手平台；通过多项定量基准快速筛选不同手指级原型；候选手指设计（关节、骨骼、皮肤、传感器布局变化）使用机制导向和任务相关指标进行评估，建立组件设计与全手具身之间的定量联系。

Result: 通过开发具有优化手指的拟人化机器人手进行验证，证明了这些手指在多物体抓取和灯泡旋拧等任务中实现了性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过模块化手指优化和定量基准评估，可以显著提升拟人化灵巧手的整体性能，该框架建立的设计-性能定量联系为系统性手部设计提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Designing anthropomorphic dexterous robotic hands remains challenging as the design space straddles morphology, actuation, and sensing properties, and performance metrics span both task-dependent and task-agnostic. Existing optimization methods are often unstructured or consider only a single performance metric, limiting systematic comparison and targeted refinement. While the design considerations of the entire hand are significant, the individual finger properties play a key role in dexterity. By developing a robotic hand platform where fingers can be modularly integrated into a full teleoperated hand, we propose that optimizing the fingers can significantly improve overall hand performance. This approach enables rapid screening of different finger-level prototypes through a number of quantitative benchmarks before their integration into the hand for task-level validation. Candidate finger designs (incorporating variations in joint, bone, skin, and sensor placement) are assessed using both mechanism-oriented and task-relevant metrics, which establish a quantitative link between component design and full hand embodiment. The framework is validated through the development of an anthropomorphic robotic hand with optimized fingers, demonstrating how these fingers enable performance improvements across tasks, including multi-object grasping and light bulb screwing.

</details>


### [37] [Critic Architecture Matters: Dual vs. Unified Critics for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11891)
*Mehmet Turan Yardımcı*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过Unitree G1人形机器人（23个主动自由度）在NVIDIA Isaac Lab中的对比实验，发现双评论家（dual-critic）架构在目标到达速度（3.5倍）、吞吐量（2倍）和到达率（65.2% vs 53.8%）上均显著优于单评论家（unified-critic）架构，且额外奖励机制无法超越架构本身带来的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标强化学习中人形机器人需要协调 locomotion 和 manipulation，但评论家架构（统一 vs 分离）这一设计选择对策略性能的影响尚未被系统研究。

Method: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上，使用13级序列课程（从静态到达行走变向目标）训练locomotion-manipulation策略，对比统一评论家与双评论家架构，并引入反博弈奖励机制进行对照。

Result: 双评论家策略目标到达速度快3.5倍（6.5 vs 22.6步），吞吐量高2倍（14.3 vs 7.0次/千步），到达率高（65.2% vs 53.8%）；反博弈奖励无额外提升（60.9% vs 65.2%）。

Conclusion: 评论家架构是多目标人形机器人RL中关键且常被忽视的设计选择，其影响超过奖励工程；在RL微调模仿学习策略时，统一评论家可能通过竞争梯度抑制已学行为。

Abstract: Multi-objective reinforcement learning for humanoid robots must coordinate locomotion and manipulation within a single policy. A natural design choice is whether to use a single (unified) critic that estimates the combined value of all objectives, or separate (dual) critics with disjoint reward signals. We present a controlled comparison on the Unitree G1 humanoid (23 active DoF) in NVIDIA Isaac Lab, training loco-manipulation policies through a sequential curriculum spanning 13 levels from stationary reaching to walking with variable-orientation targets. In standardized evaluation, dual-critic policies reach targets 3.5$\times$ faster (6.5 vs. 22.6 simulation steps), achieve 2$\times$ higher throughput (14.3 vs. 7.0 validated reaches per 1,000 steps), and attain higher validated reach rates (65.2% vs. 53.8%) compared to the unified-critic policy. Notably, additional anti-gaming reward mechanisms provide no further improvement beyond the architectural change alone (60.9% vs. 65.2%). These results have direct implications for the emerging paradigm of RL fine-tuning of imitation-learned policies: when refining a pre-trained manipulation policy with RL, a unified critic risks suppressing the learned behavior through competing locomotion gradients. These findings demonstrate that critic architecture is a primary - and often overlooked - design choice in multi-objective humanoid RL, with greater impact than reward engineering on reaching efficiency.

</details>


### [38] [DuoBench: A Reproducible Benchmark for Bimanual Manipulation in Simulation and the Real World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11901)
*Tobias Jülg,Seongjin Bien,Simon Hilber,Yannik Blei,Pierre Krack,Maximilian Li,Sven Parusel,Rudolf Lioutikov,Florian Walter,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DuoBench是一个针对双臂机器人操作策略的可扩展基准测试框架，包含11个任务（覆盖4种协调类别），提出分阶段评估方案支持细粒度语义故障分析，并提供人类遥操作数据集。实验表明当前策略在双臂操作中仍面临挑战，尤其在早期交互阶段、并行臂执行和仿真到现实迁移方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试未能充分捕捉双臂协调带来的控制复杂性和故障模式，缺乏系统性的双臂操作策略评估框架。

Method: 构建DuoBench框架，包含11个任务（覆盖4种协调类别）、分阶段评估方案、3D可打印资产的任务食谱、人类遥操作数据集，并在仿真和真实硬件上对多种双臂模仿学习与视觉-语言-动作策略进行基准测试。

Result: 当前策略在双臂操作中表现不佳，尤其在早期交互阶段、并行臂执行以及仿真到现实迁移方面存在显著挑战。

Conclusion: DuoBench为诊断双臂操作故障模式和研究未来双臂策略学习方法提供了可复现的测试平台。

Abstract: Bimanual robot systems substantially expand manipulation capabilities, but coordinating two arms introduces additional control complexity and failure modes that are not well captured by existing benchmarks. We introduce DuoBench, an extensible benchmarking framework for bimanual manipulation policies on the FR3 Duo platform. DuoBench comprises eleven tasks spanning four coordination categories, implemented in simulation and partially reproduced in the real world through reproducible task recipes with 3D-printable assets. In addition, we propose a stage-based evaluation scheme that supports fine-grained semantic failure analysis beyond binary success and provide human-teleoperated datasets for all benchmark tasks. We benchmark several dual-arm imitation-learning and vision-language-action policies in simulation and on real hardware. Our results show that current policies remain challenged by bimanual manipulation, particularly in early interaction stages, parallel arm execution, and transfer between simulation and real-world settings. DuoBench provides a reproducible testbed for diagnosing these failure modes and studying future methods for dual-arm policy learning. Code, datasets, and videos are available at https://duobench.github.io/

</details>


### [39] [Deformable In-Hand Slip-Aware Tactile Sensor with Integrated Velocity, Force/Torque, and Pressure Map Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11952)
*Gabriel Arslan Waltersson,Yiannis Karayiannidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型触觉传感器，将速度、力/力矩和压力图感知集成到单个可变形接触垫中，用于带滑移感知的抓取操作。这是首个在同一柔性结构中融合这些感知模态的传感器，支持快速低成本制造，并通过实验评估了其性能与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉传感器通常只能提供单一的感知模态（如仅压力或仅力/力矩），难以满足灵巧操作中对多模态感知融合的需求，尤其缺乏同时检测滑移和接触状态的能力。

Method: 采用标准PCB制造与快速原型技术相结合的方法，将速度、力/力矩和压力图三种感知模态集成到一个具有可变形接触垫的单一柔性结构中。

Result: 该传感器能够稳健地跟踪平面和曲面，适用于多种材料，并通过实验验证了其能力和局限性。

Conclusion: 该传感器是首个在同一柔性结构中融合速度、力/力矩和压力图三种感知模态的触觉传感器，具备低成本、快速制造的优势，为灵巧操作中的滑移感知控制提供了新方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel tactile sensor for in-hand manipulation with slip-aware control that integrates velocity, force/torque, and pressure map sensing into a single device with a deformable contact pad. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first sensor to combine these sensing modalities within a single compliant structure. The sensor features a deformable contact surface and can robustly track both flat and curved surfaces across a wide range of materials. Its performance is evaluated through a comprehensive set of experiments that highlight both its capabilities and limitations. The sensor is designed for rapid and low-cost fabrication using a combination of standard PCB manufacturing and rapid prototyping techniques.

</details>


### [40] [MPPI-based Informative Trajectory Planning for Search and Capture of Drifting Targets with ASVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12019)
*Sanjeev Ramkumar Sudha,Marija Popović,Erlend M. Coates*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合规划框架，利用基于模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制的时空信息规划方法，实现自主水面艇在动态环境中对多个漂浮目标的搜索与捕获，在长时域内优化连续轨迹以平衡搜索与跟踪目标，并在拦截阶段切换至纯追踪制导控制器，实验验证了该方法优于基线且通过了实地测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标跟踪与追击方法多采用简单制导行为和短时预测，难以有效平衡未知区域的探索和已知目标的跟踪，无法应对动态环境中连续漂移的多目标搜索与捕获任务。

Method: 提出混合规划框架：1）采用基于MPPI的时空信息规划方法，通过采样优化长时域连续轨迹，生成运动学级指令；2）多目标代价函数平衡搜索与跟踪目标，确保轨迹安全可行；3）拦截阶段切换至纯追踪制导控制器进行物理捕获。

Result: 实验表明所提规划方法优于所选规划基线，并在自主水面艇实地试验中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 基于MPPI的混合规划框架能有效解决动态环境中多漂移目标的搜索与捕获问题，为自主水面艇在环境清理和搜救等应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous surface vehicles offer an efficient solution for environmental cleanup as well as search and rescue operations in open waters. Targets in these settings drift continuously, so efficient search must balance exploration of unobserved regions with tracking of known targets. However, most target tracking and pursuit scenarios consider simple guidance behaviours and short-term predictions for decision-making. In this letter, we address the problem of search and capture of multiple drifting targets, such as litter, in dynamic environments, using a hybrid planning framework. A key aspect of our strategy is a spatiotemporal informative planning method based on model predictive path integral (MPPI) control, a sampling-based model predictive control approach. The planner directly generates kinematic-level commands by optimising continuous trajectories over long horizons. A multi-objective cost balances search and tracking objectives while ensuring safe, feasible trajectories. In the interception stage, we switch to a pure pursuit guidance controller for the physical capture of moving targets. Experiments show that our planner outperforms the chosen planning baselines. Finally, we validate our approach in field trials with an ASV.

</details>


### [41] [Learning Unions of Convex Sets via Invertible Latent Decomposition for Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12027)
*Taerim Yoon,Dongho Kang,Kisang Park,Junha Cha,Stelian Coros,Sungjoon Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ILD框架，通过学习可逆映射和隐空间中的显式凸多面体集合，结合显式表示的可优化性和隐式表示的可扩展性，实现高维复杂环境下的无碰撞路径规划，并在多种机器人平台上验证了其高成功率和实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有碰撞避免路径规划的显式表示（如凸集并集）可嵌入优化规划器作为硬约束，但参数随构型空间维度扩展性差；隐式表示扩展性好但缺乏保证。本文旨在弥合两者差距，兼顾可优化性与可扩展性。

Method: 提出ILD（可逆隐分解）框架，联合学习一个可逆映射和隐空间中显式凸多面体的并集。规划在隐空间凸集上进行，可逆映射将路径解码回原始构型空间。进一步提出可见性引导采样（VGS）保持凸集间连通性。

Result: 在2D导航、6-DoF和14-DoF操作环境中，ILD实现了比基线更广的覆盖、更好的集合间连通性和更高的路径规划成功率，测试时精炼后零假阳性。在14-DoF双臂机械臂上实现实时无碰撞规划，并在真实6-DoF单臂部署中适应场景几何变化。

Conclusion: ILD成功融合了显式表示与隐式表示的优势，为高维复杂环境下的实时无碰撞路径规划提供了有效框架，具有良好的可扩展性和实际部署能力。
