Abstract: Collision-free path planning in cluttered, real-world environments relies on a representation of the collision-free space, and existing representations broadly fall into two
  categories. Explicit representations, such as unions of convex sets, can be plugged into optimization-based planners as hard collision-free constraints, but their parameters scale
  poorly with configuration-space dimension. Implicit representations, by contrast, are flexible and scale well to complex geometries, yet typically lack such guarantees. We bridge this
  gap with ILD (Invertible Latent Decomposition), a framework that jointly learns an invertible mapping and a union of explicit convex polytopes in the resulting latent space. Planning
  is carried out over these latent convex sets, and the invertible mapping decodes the resulting paths back to the original configuration space while preserving feasibility with
  respect to the refined explicit safe regions. We further propose Visibility-Guided Sampling (VGS) to keep the convex sets connected for path planning. Across 2D navigation, 6-DoF, and
  14-DoF manipulation environments, ILD achieves broader coverage, better inter-set connectivity, and higher path-planning success rates than prior baselines, with zero observed false
  positives after test-time refinement. On a 14-DoF bimanual manipulator, we further demonstrate real-time collision-free planning, with test-time refinement adapting to scene-geometry
  changes during real-world deployment on a single 6-DoF arm.

</details>


### [42] [VICX: Generalizable Robot Manipulation via Video Generation and In-Context Operator Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12028)
*Song Chen,Linyan Xiang,Ying Zhou,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VICX提出了一种解耦的闭环操作框架，利用冻结的视频生成模型产生高层视觉计划，并通过V2T-ICON网络将其映射为可执行的机器人状态轨迹，实现跨任务、跨本体的双泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人操作需要同时具备对未知场景的任务级推理能力，以及将视觉计划可靠地映射到具体本体执行的能力，现有方法在这两方面存在脱节。

Method: 采用解耦架构：1）冻结的视频生成模型生成视觉语言条件化的高层视觉计划；2）V2T-ICON作为任务无关接口，基于分割提取的机械臂帧观测和检索的图像-状态对作为上下文提示，将视觉计划映射为可执行的机器人状态轨迹，推理时无需参数更新。

Result: 在Meta-World上的实验表明，VICX支持跨任务泛化、闭环自校正和跨本体迁移，实现了任务语义和机器人执行的双重泛化。

Conclusion: VICX通过解耦视觉规划与执行映射，有效弥合了高层任务推理与具体执行之间的鸿沟，为通用机器人操作提供了可行的泛化方案。

Abstract: Generalizable robot manipulation requires not only task-level reasoning over unseen scenes, but also reliable grounding of visual plans into embodiment-specific execution. To bridge this gap, we propose VICX (Video generation and In-Context eXecution), a decoupled closed-loop manipulation framework. In VICX, a frozen video generation model produces vision-language-conditioned high-level visual plans, while a Video-to-Trajectory In-Context Operator Network (V2T-ICON) serves as the task-agnostic interface that grounds these plans into executable robot-state trajectories. To improve execution generalization, V2T-ICON operates on segmentation-extracted arm-only frame observations and uses retrieved image-state pairs as in-context prompts, allowing a robust and generalizable visual-to-state mapping at inference time without parameter updates. Experiments on Meta-World show that VICX supports cross-task generalization, closed-loop self-correction, and cross-embodiment transfer, demonstrating dual generalization across both task semantics and robot execution. The project webpage can be found here: https://scaling-group.github.io/vicx/.

</details>


### [43] [KinematicRL: A Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning Framework For Social Navigation With Kinodynamic Feasibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12042)
*Zhiming Xu,Haodong Yang,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen,Chenpeng Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出KinematicRL框架，通过二阶控制、纯2D激光雷达人体追踪和残差门控模块，解决了深度强化学习在社会导航中从仿真到真实部署的动力学可行性和状态估计问题，并在真实差速驱动机器人上成功部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL社会导航方法存在两大局限：一是简化的一阶动力学导致仿真与实际之间存在追踪误差，阻碍真实部署；二是依赖相机-激光雷达融合的上下文人体状态估计管道增加了系统复杂度。本文旨在统一解决这些问题，实现可直接部署的真实世界导航策略。

Method: 1) 理论分析表明控制阶数越高追踪误差呈指数衰减，因此采用二阶控制作为动作空间，并设计适用于差速驱动机器人的二阶控制公式，结合随机iLQR通过散度最小化目标预训练策略；2) 提出基于2D激光雷达的聚类人体追踪管道，利用空间邻近性和速度相似性进行检测关联，通过时间聚合获得稳定的速度估计；3) 引入无偏残差门控模块，在处理时变人群规模的同时平衡基于反应和基于记忆的行为。

Result: KinematicRL策略在动力学性能和适应检测到的人数变化方面持续改进。在真实环境实验中，结合所提追踪管道后，KinematicRL可在真实差速驱动机器人上以最小修改直接部署。

Conclusion: 通过提升控制阶数、简化感知管道以及引入残差门控机制，有效弥合了社会导航中DRL的仿真到真实鸿沟，为实际部署提供了统一可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has shown promise for social navigation, yet its real-world deployment remains hindered by a persistent sim-to-real gap arising from simplified first-order dynamics and context-specific human state estimation pipelines. This work presents a unified framework that addresses these limitations to produce dynamically feasible navigation policies suitable for real-world deployment. First, theoretical analysis reveals that tracking error between simulated and actual robot position decays exponentially with increased control order, motivating the use of higher-order control inputs as DRL action space. A second-order control formulation tailored to differential drive robots is developed, complemented by a stochastic iterative Linear Quadratic Regulator (iLQR) that pretrains the policy via a divergence minimization objective. Second, to avoid the added system complexity of camera-LiDAR fusion, a cluster-based human tracking pipeline using only 2D LiDAR is introduced. Human detections are associated according to both spatial proximity and velocity similarity, enabling reliable differentiation of nearby pedestrians and yielding stable velocity estimates through temporal aggregation. Third, we introduce an unbiased residual gating block to balance reaction- and memory-based behaviors while handling time-varying crowd sizes, both critical for social navigation. The resulting policy, KinematicRL, consistently improves kinematic performance and adapts to varying number of detected humans. Experiments in real-world environments demonstrate that, when combined with the proposed tracking pipeline, KinematicRL can be deployed on a real differential drive robot with minimal modifications.

</details>


### [44] [Point Cloud Segmentation for Autonomous Clip Positioning in Laparoscopic Cholecystectomy on a Phantom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12048)
*Balázs Gyenes,Nikolai Franke,Paul Maria Scheikl,Pit Henrich,Rayan Younis,Gerhard Neumann,Martin Wagner,Franziska Mathis-Ullrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个实现腹腔镜手术中自主夹子定位的机器人系统，通过合成数据预训练和数据增强克服标注数据稀缺问题，达到亚毫米级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险机器人应用（如机器人辅助手术）需要同时具备高精度和可解释性，而现有系统在数据稀缺和低容错环境下难以满足这些要求。

Method: 采用单摄像头无色点云分割，结合样条插值提取夹子目标位置，支持人工调整；使用12.8万合成点云预训练+60个手工标注真实点云微调，提出两种新型数据增强技术；可视化末端执行器运动路径以满足微创手术约束并保证可解释性。

Result: 目标定位精度达0.75mm（95%成功率），自主夹子定位成功率100%。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了手术领域数据稀缺下的高精度定位问题，且方法可推广至其他需要精确定位与导航的手术及非手术任务。

Abstract: High-risk applications in robotics, such as robot-assisted surgery, present unique challenges. These systems must be both highly precise and interpretable in order to be deployed in environments with very low tolerance for error or unsafe exploration. We present the first robotic system to demonstrate autonomous clip positioning on a physical phantom in laparoscopic surgery, one of the most common interventions in general surgery. After segmentation of a colorless point cloud from a single camera, target positions for the clips are extracted using spline interpolation, and can then be adjusted by the human operator. The segmentation model is trained on only 60 hand-labeled real point clouds, reflecting data scarcity in the surgical domain. We overcome this with a combination of pre-training on 128,000 synthetic point clouds and two novel data augmentation techniques. The motion of the end-effector to each target is visualized for the operator, satisfying the unique motion constraints of minimally-invasive surgery while ensuring that the robot's actions are verifiable and interpretable. In real robot experiments, our system localizes targets with the required precision of 0.75mm at a 95% success rate and executes autonomous clip positioning with a 100% success rate. We provide insights that are applicable to many other surgical and non-surgical tasks that require identifying and navigating to a precise target. Source code and project page: https://github.com/balazsgyenes/kirurc

</details>


### [45] [Fibration Trees: A Unified Approach to Multi-Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12070)
*Andreas Orthey,Florian T. Pokorny,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出纤维树（fibration trees）作为统一框架，将状态空间投影（优先级/任务空间）与分解（并行/解耦子空间）纳入单一形式体系，并基于此开发了Fibration-RRT规划器，在多机器人（最高96自由度）的32个场景中验证了其概率完备性与高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏统一框架来无缝处理投影（优先级或任务空间）与分解（并行或解耦子空间）的组合，亟需一种能同时涵盖这些策略的形式化方法。

Method: 定义纤维树（以状态空间为节点、纤维映射为边）统一建模投影；提出Fibration-RRT采样规划器，泛化了商空间RRT（顺序优先级）和离散RRT（并行分解），并支持任务空间投影；算法被证明概率完备。

Result: 在32个多机器人场景（最高96自由度）中，Fibration-RRT能高效求解高维问题，验证了纤维树作为统一框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 纤维树为多机器人运动规划提供了强大的统一框架，Fibration-RRT通过利用用户定义的纤维树可高效解决高维问题。

Abstract: State space projections and decompositions have emerged as powerful tools to tackle the curse of dimensionality in high-dimensional, multi-robot motion planning problems. However, existing methods lack a unified framework which seamlessly handles combinations of projections (prioritization or task-space) and decompositions (parallel or decoupled subspaces). To fill this gap, we introduce fibration trees, which are trees consisting of state spaces as nodes and fibrations as edges, whereby a fibration models a projection from a higher-dimensional space to a lower-dimensional (or simplified) space. By modeling projections as fibrations, we unify sequential prioritization, parallel decomposition, and task-space projections under a single, coherent formalism. Building on this, we develop the rapidly-exploring random fibration trees (Fibration-RRT) planner, a sampling-based motion planner that generalizes strategies from quotient-space RRT (for sequential prioritizations) and discrete RRT (for parallel decompositions), while allowing the inclusion of task-space projections. Fibration-RRT operates on user-defined fibration trees and is proven to be probabilistically complete. To test the generality and efficiency of Fibration-RRT, we provide an open-source implementation and conduct experiments on 32 scenarios using multi robot teams with up to 96 degrees of freedom. Our results indicate that Fibration-RRT efficiently solves high-dimensional problems by exploiting user-defined fibration trees, thereby establishing fibration trees as a powerful, unified framework for multi-robot motion planning.

</details>


### [46] [DAM-VLA: Decoupled Asynchronous Multimodal Vision Language Action model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12105)
*Pankhuri Vanjani,Zhuoyue Li,Jakub Suliga,Moritz Reuss,Gianluca Geraci,Xinkai Jiang,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DAM-VLA通过解耦视觉-语言-动作模型中各模态的时序处理，让每个模态按自身传感器频率独立更新和保留信息，在7项真实世界灵巧操作任务中实现了95.2%的平均成功率（对比同步基线40.95%），并支持100Hz的平滑反应控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型从视觉-语言预训练继承了同步时钟，所有输入以同一频率处理。这与物理交互不匹配：高频模态（如触觉/力矩）变化达数百赫兹，视觉变化较慢，语言在整个回合中保持不变。同步机制导致慢模态过采样、快模态欠采样，动作生成被限制在最低有效频率。

Method: 提出DAM-VLA框架，为每个模态维护独立的潜在缓冲区（latent buffer），按传感器速率刷新，动作头（action head）连续读取。通过门控交叉注意力（gated cross-attention）整合新的高频模态，保持预训练骨干网络不变。

Result: 在7个接触丰富的真实世界操作任务中，DAM-VLA平均成功率95.2%，最强同步基线仅40.95%，提升超两倍。同时实现了100Hz的平滑、反应式控制。

Conclusion: 解耦各模态的时序处理能产生更强的表征和更鲁棒的控制，证明了异步感知-运动架构在机器人操作中的优越性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models inherit a shared synchronous clock from vision-language pretraining, processing every input at one rate. This is misaligned with physical interaction, where a high-frequency modality changes at hundreds of hertz, vision evolves more slowly, and language stays constant across an episode. A synchronous VLA oversamples slow modalities, undersamples fast ones, and caps action generation at the lowest effective frequency. We hypothesize that decoupling temporal processing per modality, letting each update and retain information at its own sensor rate, yields stronger representations and more robust control. We present DAM-VLA, which maintains per-modality latent buffers refreshed at sensor rates and read continuously by the action head, integrating new high-frequency modalities through gated cross-attention that leaves the pretrained backbone intact. Across seven contact-rich real-world manipulation tasks, DAM-VLA more than doubles the average success rate of the strongest synchronous baseline (95.2\% vs.\ 40.95\%) while sustaining smooth, reactive 100\,Hz control. Project website: \href{https://intuitive-robots.github.io/DAM-VLA/}{intuitive-robots.github.io/DAM-VLA/}

</details>


### [47] [Bridging the Morphology Gap: Adapting VLA Models to Dexterous Manipulation via Intent-Conditioned Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12109)
*Chuanke Pang,Junyi Huang,Zhijun Zhao,Yaobing Wang,Kun Xu,Xilun Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出InDex框架，通过将预训练的1-DoF平行夹爪输出重用作宏观虚拟抓取意图代理，采用两阶段解耦学习架构，实现从低自由度平行夹爪到高自由度灵巧手的跨形态适应，有效解决形态鸿沟和灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型大多局限于低自由度平行夹爪，将其适配到高自由度灵巧手存在严重的形态鸿沟，直接端到端联合微调会导致灾难性遗忘和动作流形坍塌。

Method: 提出InDex两阶段解耦学习架构：第一阶段参数高效地对齐VLA骨干网络，预测连续手臂轨迹和标量抓取意图；第二阶段冻结空间骨干网络，使用意图条件去噪扩散头解码多指末端执行器的细粒度关节运动。

Result: 在多阶段、高接触的灵巧操作任务仿真基准中，InDex以极少的演示数据有效掌握复杂技能，性能显著优于整体基线模型，同时保留了原始VLA先验的鲁棒空间泛化能力。

Conclusion: InDex通过跨形态语义继承实现高效的数据适应，为将VLA先验从平行夹爪迁移至灵巧手提供可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable zero-shot generalization in robotic manipulation, yet the vast majority of pre-trained pipelines remain strictly confined to low-DoF parallel grippers. Adapting these rich semantic priors to high-DoF dexterous hands introduces a severe morphology gap, direct end-to-end joint fine-tuning inherently causes catastrophic forgetting of spatial reasoning and acute action manifold collapse due to data scarcity. In this paper, we present InDex, a novel, data-efficient adaptation framework rooted in cross-morphology semantic inheritance. Rather than discarding the pre-trained 1-DoF parallel grasp output, we repurpose it as a continuous, macroscopic virtual grasp intent proxy to sequentialize the control topology. We implement a two-stage decoupled learning architecture: the first stage parameter-efficiently aligns the VLA backbone to predict continuous arm trajectories and the scalar grasp intent; the second stage freezes this spatial backbone and leverages an intent-conditioned denoising diffusion head to decode fine-grained joint articulations for multi-fingered end-effectors. Extensive simulation benchmarks across a suite of multi-stage, contact-rich dexterous manipulation tasks demonstrate that InDex effectively masters intricate skills with minimal demonstration data, substantially outperforming monolithic baselines while preserving the robust spatial generalizability of the original VLA prior.

</details>


### [48] [PEBRE: An Open-Hardware Compute and Perception Add-On for the Pepper Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12112)
*Malte Kuhlmann,Ignacio Bugueno-Cordova,Emil Alms,Javier Ruiz-del-Solar,Nicolás Navarro-Guerrero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了PEBRE，一个为Pepper机器人设计的开源硬件附加模块，通过集成Jetson Orin Nano等多种外部组件，增强了机器人的计算和感知能力，并延长了其使用寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pepper机器人计算和感知能力有限，且面临平台过时问题，需要一种模块化、开源的方式来增强其性能并延长其作为研究平台的使用寿命。

Method: 设计并开发了PEBRE开源硬件附加模块，集成了Jetson Orin Nano（计算）、Logitech BRIO和Intel RealSense D435i（视觉）、Samson UB1和RØDE VideoMicro II（音频）等外部组件。

Result: 实验验证显示，PEBRE显著提升了Pepper的感知能力和计算性能。

Conclusion: PEBRE作为一个开源、模块化的硬件附加方案，能够加速Pepper机器人的软件开发，促进外部组件的更高效集成，并使这一研究平台在预期寿命之后仍能保持功能性。

Abstract: This paper presents the design, development, and experimental verification of PEBRE, an open-hardware add-on for fast software development on the Pepper Robot. Our project enhances Pepper's computational and perception capabilities by integrating external components such as a Jetson Orin Nano, Logitech BRIO, Intel RealSense D435i, Samson UB1, and RØDE VideoMicro II. Our results show that the new hardware considerably improved Pepper's perception abilities and computational power. This development contributes to the community by implementing an open hardware and open-source modular add-on to the Pepper robot and keeping this relevant research platform functional beyond its expected lifespan. With PEBRE, we aim to facilitate faster software development and more efficient integration of external components, ultimately enhancing the capabilities of the Pepper robot.

</details>


### [49] [AerialClaw: An Open-Source Framework for LLM-Driven Autonomous Aerial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12142)
*Ke Li,Jianfei Yang,Luyao Zhang,Guo Yu,Chengwei Yan,Yuan Ding,Di Wang,Nan Luo,Gang Liu,Xiao Gao,Quan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AerialClaw是一个开源软件框架，使无人机能够作为基于LLM的决策智能体运行，通过自然语言理解任务、调用飞行技能、观察反馈并闭环迭代决策，采用模块化的脑-技能-运行时架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无人机应用大多依赖预定义指令序列或任务特定管线，开发者需手动连接感知、规划、飞行控制、仿真、日志和安全等模块，限制了自主空中系统的灵活性、可重复性和可扩展性。

Method: 提出AerialClaw框架，采用模块化的脑-技能-运行时（brain-skill-runtime）架构：结合硬技能（原子化无人机操作）、Markdown式软技能（可复用任务策略）、文档驱动智能体状态与能力边界、记忆驱动反思、面向安全的运行时验证，以及平台无关的执行适配器。支持多种仿真环境（轻量模拟、PX4 SITL+Gazebo、AirSim）并集成Web控制台、可插拔模型后端等工具。

Result: AerialClaw实现了无人机从指令执行平台向决策智能体的转变，能够理解自然语言任务、维护上下文、调用可执行空中技能、观察感知与运行时反馈，并闭环更新决策，提供了可重复且可扩展的开源框架。

Conclusion: 通过结合标准化空中技能、文档驱动的智能体状态、记忆机制和闭环LLM决策，AerialClaw为构建能够解释任务、做出决策、执行技能并从反馈中自适应行为的无人机系统提供了可重复且可扩展的开源框架。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly used in inspection, search and rescue, environmental monitoring, and emergency response. However, most UAV applications still rely on pre-defined command sequences or task-specific pipelines, where developers manually connect perception, planning, flight control, simulation, logging, and safety modules. This limits the flexibility, reproducibility, and extensibility of autonomous aerial systems. This paper presents AerialClaw, an open-source software framework that enables UAVs to operate as decision-making aerial agents rather than merely command-following platforms. Given a natural-language mission, AerialClaw allows an LLM-based agent to understand the task, maintain context, invoke executable aerial skills, observe perception and runtime feedback, and iteratively update its decisions in a closed loop. The framework adopts a modular brain-skill-runtime architecture, combining hard skills for atomic UAV operations, Markdown-based soft skills for reusable task strategies, document-driven agent state and capability boundaries, memory-driven reflection, safety-oriented runtime validation, and platform-agnostic execution adapters. AerialClaw supports lightweight mock execution, PX4 SITL with Gazebo, and AirSim-based simulation, together with a web console, pluggable model backends, example missions, simulation assets, and staged deployment scripts. By combining standardized aerial skills, document-driven agent state, memory, and closed-loop LLM decision-making, AerialClaw provides a reproducible and extensible open-source framework for building UAV systems that can interpret missions, make decisions, execute skills, and adapt their behavior from feedback.

</details>


### [50] [Intelligent Automation for Embodied Benchmark Construction: Pipelines, Embodiments, Simulators, and Trends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12207)
*Jinshan Lai,Jianwei Hu,Baoyang Jiang,Fengchun Zhang,Leyuan Wang,Haotian Li,Yida Wang,Tingxuan Huang,Xi Ren,Qiang Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述通过五阶段构建管线（需求与任务构建、数据获取、数据清洗与标注、基准套件生成与指标定义、评估执行与诊断反馈）系统回顾了具身智能基准测试的构建方法，分析了从人工到自动化、基础模型辅助到智能体闭环工作流的演进，并指出自动化并不简单降低基准成本，而是将成本转向验证、审计、版本控制与长期治理，因此具身评估的进步不仅依赖更大规模的基准套件，更需要可诊断、可审计、可负责任更新的构建管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能已涵盖导航、家务辅助、操作、自动驾驶、空中智能体和多模态大模型控制等多个领域，但基准构建成为可靠评估的核心瓶颈。现有静态数据集无法满足具身基准将任务规范、环境、机器人数据、演示、标注、指标、评估脚本和发布策略整合为统一评估系统的需求。

Method: 采用五阶段构建管线框架进行文献综述：1) 需求与任务构建；2) 数据获取；3) 数据清洗与标注；4) 基准套件生成与指标定义；5) 评估执行与诊断反馈。在每个阶段分析从人工到传统自动化、基础模型辅助和智能体闭环工作流的过渡，并比较人力、数据/资产获取、计算/仿真、验证/调试、治理/维护和返工风险等定性构建成本。

Result: 主要发现是自动化并不简单降低基准成本，而是将成本从某些环节转移到验证、审计、版本控制和长期治理等环节。随着自动化程度提高，对可诊断性、可审计性和可负责任更新的需求变得更加突出。

Conclusion: 具身评估的进步不仅取决于更大规模的基准套件，更取决于构建管线的可诊断性、可审计性和可负责任更新能力。自动化带来的成本转移而非简单降低成本，意味着未来需要系统性地考虑基准测试的全生命周期治理。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence now spans navigation, household assistance, manipulation, autonomous driving, aerial agents, and multimodal large-model control. This expansion has made benchmark construction a central bottleneck for reliable evaluation. Unlike static datasets, embodied benchmarks combine task specifications, environments, robot data, demonstrations, annotations, metrics, evaluation scripts, and release policies into a single evaluation system. This survey reviews the literature through a five-stage construction pipeline: requirement and task construction, data acquisition, data cleaning and annotation, benchmark suite generation and metric definition, and evaluation execution with diagnostic feedback. For each stage, the survey analyzes the transition from manual curation to traditional automation, foundation-model assistance, and agentic closed-loop workflows. It also compares qualitative construction costs across human labor, data and asset acquisition, compute and simulation, validation and debugging, governance and maintenance, and rework risk. The main conclusion is that automation does not simply reduce benchmark cost. Instead, it often shifts cost toward validation, auditability, version control, and long-term governance. Progress in embodied evaluation will therefore depend not only on larger benchmark suites, but also on construction pipelines that are diagnosable, auditable, and responsibly refreshable.

</details>


### [51] [DrivingAgent: Design and Scheduling Agents for Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12236)
*Zhongyu Xia,Wenhao Chen,Yongtao Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DrivingAgent，一种面向自动驾驶系统设计与实时调度的智能体框架，通过自动化模块开发和强化学习驱动的轻量级LLM调度，在nuScenes和Bench2Drive基准上实现了更优的速度-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶系统引入基础模型时面临两个关键挑战：(i) 人工设计和集成新模型的过程耗时费力；(ii) 缺乏智能的动态调度机制以满足严格的实时约束。现有LLM智能体框架不适用于自动驾驶，未能区分系统设计与实时调度的不同需求，将模块视为黑盒，且不支持持续运行。

Method: 提出DrivingAgent框架，分两个阶段：(1) 设计阶段：通过解释系统架构、生成代码、通过超网络训练验证模块，自动化模块开发；(2) 调度阶段：使用强化学习训练轻量级LLM，支持结构化记忆（长期存储+时间戳短期上下文），动态编排系统模块以满足实时约束。

Result: 在nuScenes和Bench2Drive两个基准数据集上，DrivingAgent实现了更优的速度-精度权衡。

Conclusion: DrivingAgent有效解决了自动驾驶系统中基础模型集成带来的设计与调度双重挑战，通过自动化开发和动态调度实现了性能提升。

Abstract: Many autonomous driving systems are increasingly incorporating foundation models to improve generalization and handle long-tail scenarios. However, this trend introduces two key challenges: (i) the manual and labor-intensive process of designing and integrating new models, and (ii) the lack of intelligent, dynamic scheduling mechanisms to meet strict real-time constraints. While Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents offer a promising avenue for automation, existing frameworks are ill-suited for autonomous driving. Specifically, they fail to distinguish between the fundamentally different requirements of system design and real-time scheduling, treat modules as opaque black boxes, and are not designed for continuous operation. To address these limitations, we propose DrivingAgent, a novel agent framework tailored to the dual challenges of autonomous driving system design and scheduling. In the design phase, DrivingAgent automates module development by interpreting system architecture, generating code, and validating modules via super-network training. In the scheduling phase, it employs a lightweight LLM trained with reinforcement learning to dynamically orchestrate system modules in real time, supported by a structured memory that integrates long-term storage with timestamped short-term context. Experimental results demonstrate that DrivingAgent achieves a superior speed--accuracy trade-off on both the nuScenes and Bench2Drive benchmarks.

</details>


### [52] [Learning What to Say to Your VLA: Mostly Harmless Vision Language Action Model Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12299)
*Hyun Joe Jeong,Gokul Swamy,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个交互式搜索语言序列、蒸馏为测试时语言反馈策略（LFP）并学习改进头以预测何时语言引导能提升性能的框架，通过共形化防止有害干预，在冻结的预训练VLA模型上运行，无需微调或访问原始训练数据。在仿真中提升24.7%，硬件中提升65.0%，并具有强无害性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型将语言映射到行为的过程脆弱且不直观：语义相似的指令可能导致截然不同的行为，某些能力无法通过单纯提示唤起，导致人类指令和零样本语言模型都难以可靠地引导VLA完成任务。

Method: 1) 交互式搜索语言序列以改进闭环VLA任务性能；2) 将搜索到的序列蒸馏为测试时语言反馈策略（LFP）；3) 学习改进头（improvement head）预测何时语言引导能提升性能；4) 对改进头进行共形化（conformalize）以防止在分布外场景中LFP降低任务性能的有害干预。该方法适用于任意冻结的预训练VLA模型。

Result: 在已见环境中，共形化LFP在仿真中提升基础VLA性能24.7%，在硬件中提升65.0%。在视觉和语义扰动下，该方法具有强无害性保证，并能产生开环提示无法观察到的恢复行为。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需微调或访问原始训练数据即可提升冻结预训练VLA模型性能的框架，通过共形化保证无害性，在仿真和硬件实验中均取得了显著提升，并在分布外场景中具备安全性保障。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide a natural language interface to robot control, but the mapping from language to behavior is often brittle and unintuitive: semantically similar instructions can induce drastically different behaviors, while some capabilities may not be elicitable through prompting alone. As a result, both human instructions and zero-shot language models can fail to reliably steer VLAs toward successful task execution. In this work, we propose a framework that interactively searches for language sequences that improve closed-loop VLA task performance, distills these sequences into a test-time language feedback policy (LFP), and learns an improvement head that predicts when language steering will improve performance. We conformalize this improvement head to prevent harmful steering interventions, where the LFP decreases task performance relative to the original instruction on out-of-distribution scenarios. Crucially, our approach operates on arbitrary frozen pre-trained VLAs, requiring neither access to the original training distribution nor fine-tuning of the underlying model. On seen environments, our conformalized LFP improves base VLA performance by 24.7% in simulation and 65.0% in hardware. On visual and semantic perturbations, our conformalized LFP has strong harmlessness guarantees, and produces recovery behaviors not observed with open-loop prompting.

</details>

*Lars Oerlemans,Moji Shi,Marija Popovic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多无人机协同系统，通过共享威胁信念地图，引导无人机对地面车辆路径相关区域进行针对性侦察，从而降低地面车辆在未知威胁环境中的累计暴露风险。实验表明，该方法相比基线减少了38%的暴露量，并将无人机冗余覆盖率从38.8%降至3.7%。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中侦察系统侧重于环境映射或全覆盖，未能将感知资源导向对地面车辆安全最关键的路径相关区域，导致地面车辆在穿越未知威胁区域时面临不必要的风险。

Method: 1) 建立共享暴露信念模型，通过空中观测在线更新威胁概率；2) 地面车辆利用该信念进行路径重规划以避开新发现的威胁；3) 无人机团队通过空间区域分配进行协调，避免冗余感知，将感知资源集中于路径相关区域。

Result: 在仿真实验中，该方法将地面车辆累计暴露量降低38%（相比不考虑威胁水平的系统），并将多无人机冗余覆盖率从38.8%降至3.7%。

Conclusion: 通过无人机-地面车辆协同的共享信念更新与区域分配策略，可以显著提升地面车辆在威胁环境中的导航安全性，同时大幅减少无人机感知冗余。

Abstract: Safe ground navigation in large, threat-augmented environments requires aerial support that actively reduces the risks that a ground vehicle faces along its route. Existing aerial reconnaissance systems focus on mapping or covering the environment, but do not direct sensing toward regions that are most relevant for ground vehicle safety. In this paper, we address the problem of coordinating a team of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to improve the safety of an unmanned ground vehicle (UGV) navigating through unknown threat zones. A key aspect of our approach is a shared exposure belief that is updated online from aerial observations and used jointly by the UAV team and the ground vehicle. This enables us to direct aerial sensing towards route-relevant regions while allowing the UGV to replan around newly revealed threats. We coordinate the UAV team through spatial region assignment to avoid redundant sensing. Simulation experiments show that our approach reduces cumulative UGV exposure by 38% compared to a system that does not account for hazard levels, and reduces redundant aerial coverage from 38.8% to 3.7% under our multi-UAV coordination scheme.

</details>

*Paria Rezayan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了MARUS仿真器，建立了一个标准化的虚拟海上试验框架，用于自动执行TC/ZZ机动、数据采集与后处理，生成符合IMO/ITTC标准的可重复数据集，支持USV水动力导数辨识与数字孪生校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人水面艇（USV）的水动力导数精确辨识对控制与导航至关重要，但物理实船试验成本高、风险大，且现有仿真工具缺乏标准化、可审计的虚拟试验流程。

Method: 在MARUS仿真器中集成虚拟海试框架，实现TC/ZZ机动自动执行与数据生成；引入可追溯的命令-执行日志记录、面向系统辨识的数据调理、IMO/ITTC机动指标自动提取；提出差动推力转向的显式命令-执行分离机制。

Result: TC试验中，左右舷归一化进距差异约3.9%，战术直径差异约4.6–4.7%；ZZ试验中，±10°和±20°机动首摇超调量均低于1°，满足IMO标准，峰值首摇角速度约4.1–5.8°/s。

Conclusion: 该虚拟海试框架能够生成可重复、可审计、符合IMO/ITTC标准的数据集，可有效支撑系统辨识、水动力导数估计及数字孪生校准工作。

Abstract: Accurate identification of hydrodynamic derivatives is essential for control and navigation of Unmanned Surface Vehicles (USVs), but high-fidelity manoeuvring data from physical sea trials are constrained by cost and safety. Turning Circle (TC) and Zig-Zag (ZZ) trials remain fundamental to IMO and ITTC assessment procedures. This paper extends the Marine Robotics Unity Simulator (MARUS) by introducing a standardised Virtual Sea Trial framework for automated execution and data generation of TC/ZZ manoeuvres, with traceable command-actuation logging, system-identification (SI)-focused data conditioning, and automated extraction of IMO/ITTC-aligned manoeuvring metrics. A key contribution is a dedicated TC/ZZ data acquisition and post-processing pipeline, improving the repeatability and auditability of simulator-based manoeuvres while producing SI-ready datasets for hydrodynamic-derivative identification and digital-twin workflows. Another feature is explicit command-execution separation for differential-thrust steering, where inputs are recorded as ordered rudder-equivalent commands and realised actuation is logged as an execution-level proxy derived from applied thrust. Case-study results demonstrate repeatable and compliant manoeuvre behaviour. For TC tests, the normalised advance differs by approximately 3.9 percent between port and starboard sides, while the tactical diameter differs by approximately 4.6 to 4.7 percent. For ZZ tests, first and second overshoot excesses remain below 1 degree for both +/- 10 degree and +/- 20 degree manoeuvres, satisfying IMO criteria, while peak yaw rates range from approximately 4.1 to 5.8 deg/s. Overall, the framework provides a repeatable and auditable virtual sea-trial workflow for generating IMO/ITTC-aligned datasets and supporting system identification, hydrodynamic-derivative estimation, and digital-twin calibration.

</details>


### [55] [CHORUS: Decentralized Multi-Embodiment Collaboration with One VLA Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12352)
*Ria Doshi,Tian Gao,Annie Chen,Chelsea Finn,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CHORUS框架，利用预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的视觉运动先验，实现基于局部观测的去中心化多机器人协作，无需推理时的显式对齐或信息共享。在真实实验中，CHORUS在移动测距、图书交接和洗衣篮搬运等任务中，相比从头训练的去中心化模型提升64%的性能，反应速度提升40个百分点，并优于集中式基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作在移动场景中面临挑战：集中式方法随团队规模扩展性差，去中心化方法通常需要显式对齐或推理时的信息共享来克服部分可观测性。作者希望利用预训练VLA模型的视觉运动先验，实现仅基于每个机器人局部观测的反应式去中心化协作，消除推理时的额外假设。

Method: 提出CHORUS框架，将单一VLA骨干网络适配到多样化的多机器人团队中。推理时，每个机器人运行独立的CHORUS副本，仅依赖于自身观测和机器人身份提示词进行决策。

Result: 在真实世界实验中（移动测距、图书馆图书交接、洗衣篮搬运），CHORUS相比从头训练的去中心化模型取得64%的绝对性能提升，对队友行为的反应性提升40个百分点，并优于集中式基线方法。

Conclusion: 共享的VLA骨干网络能够在无需每个机器人独立策略或推理时机器人间通信的条件下，实现去中心化的多机器人协作。

Abstract: Multi-robot collaboration allows robots to efficiently take on a wide range of tasks, from moving a couch through a doorway to assembling structures on a construction site. However, achieving such coordination in mobile multi-robot settings remains challenging: centralized methods conditioned on the combined observations of a team scale poorly with team size, and decentralized methods that train one policy per robot often require explicit alignment procedures or information sharing at inference time to overcome partial observability. Our key insight is that the visuomotor priors of pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models should enable reactive, decentralized collaboration from each robot's local observations alone, without these inference-time assumptions. We propose CHORUS, a framework that adapts a single VLA backbone to control diverse, multi-robot teams. At inference time, each robot runs an independent copy of CHORUS, conditioned only on its own observations and a robot-identifying prompt. In real-world experiments including mobile tape measurement, library book handovers, and laundry basket lifting, CHORUS achieves a 64% point improvement over decentralized, from-scratch models, improves reactivity to teammate behavior by 40% points, and outperforms centralized baselines. Together, these results show that a shared VLA backbone is capable of achieving decentralized multi-robot collaboration, without per-robot policies or inter-robot communication at inference.

</details>


### [56] [Ambient Diffusion Policy: Imitation Learning from Suboptimal Data in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12365)
*Adam Wei,Nicholas Pfaff,Thomas Cohn,Arif Kerem Dayı,Constantinos Daskalakis,Giannis Daras,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Ambient Diffusion Policy，一种利用噪声依赖的数据使用方式，从次优机器人数据中提取有用特征进行模仿学习的简单有效方法，在六项任务上优于现有基线，最高提升33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量机器人数据昂贵且耗时，而次优数据集（低质量或分布外演示）大量存在。现有方法无法有效区分次优样本中的有用特征和有害特征，因此需要一种能够从任意来源的次优数据中提取有用信息的方法。

Method: 引入噪声依赖的数据使用机制，限制次优数据仅在高扩散时间和低扩散时间对训练做出贡献。基于机器人动作数据呈现谱幂律分布这一观察，利用全局到局部的层次性和局部性两个性质，从理论上形式化论证了该方法的合理性。

Result: 在四种次优动作数据（噪声轨迹、仿真到真实差距、任务不匹配、大规模数据混合）和六项任务上进行了验证。在扩展到Open X-Embodiment大规模数据集时，相比现有共训练基线方法性能提升高达33%。

Conclusion: Ambient Diffusion Policy有效提高了次优演示数据的利用率，扩展了机器人学中可用数据源的范畴，为利用丰富多样的次优数据提供了新范式。

Abstract: We propose Ambient Diffusion Policy, a simple and principled method for imitation learning from suboptimal data in robotics. High-quality, task-specific robot data is expensive and time-consuming to collect, while suboptimal datasets with lower-quality or out-of-distribution demonstrations are abundant. Existing methods that co-train on both data sources in robotics often fail to separate the meaningful and the harmful features in the suboptimal samples. In contrast, our method extracts only the useful features by introducing a new axis to co-training in robotics: noise-dependent data usage. Ambient Diffusion Policy restricts the contribution of suboptimal data during training to only the high and low diffusion times. To rigorously justify our approach, we first observe that robot action data exhibits a spectral power law. This induces two important properties on the optimal Diffusion Policy that we exploit: a global-to-local hierarchy and locality. We theoretically formalize this discussion using a simplified model. Our experiments validate Ambient Diffusion Policy on four types of suboptimal action data (noisy trajectories, sim-to-real gap, task mismatch, and large-scale data mixtures) across six tasks. The results show that it effectively learns from arbitrary sources of suboptimal data. Notably, it outperforms existing co-training baselines by up to 33% when scaled to Open X-Embodiment - a large dataset with heterogeneous data quality and unstructured distribution shifts. Overall, Ambient Diffusion Policy increases the utility of suboptimal demonstrations and expands the set of usable data sources in robotics.

</details>


### [57] [APT: Action Expert Pretraining Improves Instruction Generalization of Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12366)
*Kechun Xu,Zhenjie Zhu,Anzhe Chen,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型在OOD语言指令上泛化差的问题，提出APT两阶段训练方法：先冻结VLM预训练动作专家作为视觉-动作先验，再通过门控融合注入语言特征，有效缓解了语言-视觉-动作数据的结构性不平衡，在主流VLA架构上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型中语言多样性远低于视觉和动作数据，导致结构性不平衡（structural imbalance）。连续动作专家从随机初始化开始，在不平衡数据上学习产生噪声梯度，污染了VLM表征，无法充分利用其语言能力，导致对分布外语言指令泛化性能差。

Method: 从贝叶斯视角将策略分解为语言无关的VA先验和语言条件的VLA似然，提出APT两阶段训练方法：(1) Stage 1：冻结VLM，仅在视觉-动作对上预训练动作专家作为VA先验，绕过语言不平衡；(2) Stage 2：通过门控融合机制注入语言token，在保留已学习的视觉运动先验的同时融合VLM特征。该方法适用于π和GR00T等主流VLA架构。

Result: 在未见过的指令和组合任务上取得一致性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过动作专家预训练构建视觉-动作先验，再以门控方式注入语言信息，能够有效解决VLA数据中的语言-动作结构性不平衡问题，提升模型对分布外语言指令的泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models that couple pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) with continuous action experts have achieved strong manipulation performance, yet generalization to out-of-distribution (OOD) language instructions remains poor. A known challenge is the structural imbalance in VLA data, where language is far less diverse than visual and action content, making policies prone to visual shortcuts. While discrete-action methods mitigate this through vision-language co-training, continuous action experts lack such protection: they start from random initialization and learn entirely from imbalanced data, producing noisy gradients that corrupt the VLM and fail to exploit its language capability. We address this from a Bayesian perspective, factorizing the policy into a language-agnostic Vision-Action (VA) prior and a language-conditioned VLA likelihood, and propose APT, a two-stage training method emphasizing Action expert PreTraining. In Stage 1, the action expert is pretrained as a VA prior on vision-action pairs from a frozen VLM, bypassing the language imbalance. In Stage 2, language tokens are injected through a gated fusion mechanism that integrates VLM features while preserving the learned visuomotor prior. APT applies to mainstream VLA architectures, including the $π$ and GR00T-style architectures. Comprehensive experiments validate that APT achieves consistent gains on unseen instructions and compositional tasks. Project Page: https://xukechun.github.io/papers/APT/

</details>


### [58] [UniIntervene: Agentic Intervention for Efficient Real-World Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12372)
*Haoyuan Deng,Yitong Gao,Yudong Lin,Haichao Liu,Zhenyu Wu,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniIntervene 是一种智能体干预模型，通过未来条件动作价值估计和时序价值风险评判器自动检测低效探索并自主恢复策略，在真实世界机器人操作任务中将平均成功率提升8.6%，同时减少57%的人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人在回路强化学习（HiL-RL）框架需要频繁的人类纠正来引导策略脱离低效探索，人工成本高，限制了真实世界的可扩展性。

Method: 1) 未来条件动作价值估计：预测当前动作的潜在后果并评估其诱导价值，提供更稳定的进度信号；2) 时序价值风险评判器：聚合近期价值动态，在价值持续停滞或下降时触发干预；3) 从过去干预情节的记忆中检索高价值恢复目标，通过目标条件恢复策略生成可执行的纠正动作。

Result: 在多种真实世界操作任务中，UniIntervene 相比最先进的 HiL-RL 基线，平均成功率提升8.6%，人工干预减少57%。

Conclusion: UniIntervene 将干预从被动的人类纠正转变为一种价值感知的恢复过程，实现了更高效的真实世界强化学习，显著降低了人工成本。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning (HiL-RL) has emerged as an effective paradigm for real-world robotic manipulation, enabling online policy improvement with human guidance. However, current HiL-RL frameworks remain intervention-intensive, relying on frequent human corrections to redirect the policy out of unproductive exploration, which incurs high labor cost and limits real-world scalability. To address this, we propose UniIntervene, an agentic intervention model that detects unproductive exploration and autonomously recovers the policy toward high-value states, taking over the bulk of interventions from human operators. Specifically, UniIntervene first performs future-conditioned action-value estimation, predicting the latent consequence of the current action and evaluating its induced value, which provides a more stable progress signal. Building on this, a temporal value-risk critic aggregates recent value dynamics and triggers intervention when the estimated value exhibits sustained stagnation or degradation. When intervention is required, UniIntervene retrieves a high-value recovery target from a memory of past intervention episodes and produces executable corrective actions through a goal-conditioned recovery policy. In this way, UniIntervene turns intervention from passive human correction into a value-aware recovery process for efficient real-world RL. Extensive experiments on diverse real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that UniIntervene improves the average success rate by 8.6% while reducing human interventions by 57% relative to state-of-the-art HiL-RL baselines.

</details>


### [59] [Semantically-Aware Diver Activity Recognition Framework for Effective Underwater Multi-Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12374)
*Sadman Sakib Enan,Junaed Sattar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了DAR-Net，一个基于Transformer的框架，通过语义引导的学习策略将全局活动识别与局部像素级场景理解相结合，用于水下多机器人-人类协作中的潜水员活动识别。同时发布了首个水下潜水员活动（UDA）数据集，包含2600+张带像素级标注的图像，在六种潜水员活动识别任务上取得了优于现有模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境中实现有效的人机协作至关重要，但AUV需要理解周围环境并识别潜水员活动以确保安全协作。现有方法在水下低能见度条件下难以准确进行活动识别，且该领域缺乏标注数据资源。

Method: 提出DAR-Net框架，采用Transformer进行时序推理，并结合语义引导的多损失训练策略，将全局活动识别与像素级场景监督（局部人机交互语义）对齐。

Result: 在受控环境下的实验中，DAR-Net在识别六种不同潜水员活动时取得了有前景的准确率，优于现有最先进模型。同时贡献了首个UDA数据集（2600+张像素级标注图像）。

Conclusion: 该工作为水下人机协作中的活动识别提供了基线方法和数据集，为未来更智能的协作式水下机器人系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Effective multi-human-robot collaboration is essential for expanding human-led operations in the challenging and high-risk underwater environment. For autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to become true teammates, they must be able to comprehend their surroundings and recognize a diver's activities to offer assistance and ensure safety. Towards this goal, we introduce DAR-Net, a novel transformer-based framework that analyzes complex underwater scenes to classify diver activities. Our contribution lies in a semantically guided learning formulation that couples transformer-based temporal reasoning with pixel-level scene supervision. This multi-loss training strategy explicitly aligns global activity recognition with local human-robot interaction semantics, which is particularly critical in low-visibility underwater conditions. To address the significant challenge of data scarcity in this domain, we present the first-ever Underwater Diver Activity (UDA) dataset, a foundational resource containing over 2,600 annotated images with pixel-level masks. Through rigorous experimental evaluations in a controlled environment, we demonstrate that DAR-Net achieves promising accuracy in recognizing six distinct diver activities, outperforming state-of-the-art models. While this dataset provides a crucial baseline, our work serves as a pioneering step, laying the groundwork for future research and facilitating the development of more intelligent, collaborative underwater robotic systems.

</details>


### [60] [DIRECT: When and Where Should You Allocate Test-Time Compute in Embodied Planners?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12402)
*Jadelynn Dao,Milan Ganai,Yasmina Abukhadra,Ajay Sridhar,Mozhgan Nasr Azadani,Katie Luo,Clark Barrett,Jiajun Wu,Chelsea Finn,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DIRECT框架，通过多模态场景上下文动态分配测试时计算资源，在机器人具身规划中实现成功-成本帕累托前沿的优化，相比固定模型选择可降低高达65%的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLMs作为具身智能体高层规划器时，增加测试时计算会导致延迟、token消耗和FLOPs上升，而下游任务成功率提升有限且不均匀，限制了实际部署。关键在于选择何时何地投入计算资源。

Method: 提出DIRECT路由框架，利用多模态场景上下文按提示动态分配计算资源，沿链式思考深度、模型规模和记忆历史三个扩展轴进行优化，在VLABench和RoboMME基准上评估。

Result: 不同扩展轴带来定性不同的能力增益；在物理Franka机械臂DROID设置中，路由器在零样本操作和长程链式任务上以最高65%更低平均延迟匹配或超越更强模型的成功率。

Conclusion: 朴素扩展测试时计算是浪费的，DIRECT能以更低成本在机器人系统中实现前沿级别的具身规划。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as high-level planners for embodied agents, with an emerging strategy of scaling test-time compute to improve capability. However, we observe that doing so increases latency, token usage, and FLOPs while yielding uneven, often diminishing gains in downstream success, limiting where embodied agents can be deployed. We argue that choosing when and where to spend test-time compute is central to bringing frontier performance to the real world. We introduce DIRECT, a routing framework that uses multimodal scene context to allocate compute per prompt, improving the success--cost Pareto frontier over fixed model selection. Across three dominant scaling axes, namely chain-of-thought depth, model size, and memory history, our experiments on VLABench and RoboMME show that test-time compute is not a uniform lever: different axes yield qualitatively distinct capability gains. We validate these insights on a physical Franka arm in a DROID setup spanning zero-shot manipulation and long-horizon chaining, where our router matches or exceeds a stronger model's success rate at up to 65% lower average latency. Ultimately, our results show that naively scaling test-time compute is wasteful, and that DIRECT can provide frontier-level embodied planning in robotic systems at a fraction of the cost. Project page can be found at jadee-dao.github.io/direct/.

</details>


### [61] [World Pilot: Steering Vision-Language-Action Models with World-Action Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12403)
*Zefu Lin,Rongxu Cui,Junjia Xu,Xiaojuan Jin,Wenling Li,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: World Pilot是一个VLA框架，通过世界-动作模型（WAM）提供的潜在引导和动作引导两条路径，将场景演化先验和轨迹级运动先验注入决策链，在LIBERO-Plus零样本OOD基准上达到84.7%的总成功率，并在多种真实机器人操作任务中取得最高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型基于静态图文对预训练获得语义理解，但操作任务本质上是连续的、富含接触动力学的过程，静态预训练无法捕捉场景的动态演化，导致模型在分布外场景（如视角、几何、变形状态、位姿变化）下性能下降。

Method: 提出World Pilot框架，在VLA中集成World-Action Model (WAM)的两条互补路径：1) 潜在引导（Latent Steering）——用场景演化潜码条件化感知层；2) 动作引导（Action Steering）——提供预测轨迹作为动作生成器的运动先验。即使WAM仅用视频预训练而未经过动作后训练，场景演化先验仍然有效。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus零样本OOD基准上达到84.7%的总成功率，为当前最优；在四项真实机器人操作任务中均取得最高成功率，在视角、几何、变形状态和位姿变化下的性能优势最为显著。

Conclusion: 通过引入世界模型的动态先验（场景演化+运动轨迹），可以显著提升VLA模型在分布外操作场景下的鲁棒性和泛化能力，且视频预训练的世界模型在没有动作后训练的情况下仍能提供有效的场景演化先验。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models inherit semantic grounding from large-scale pretraining and perform competently across in-distribution manipulation tasks. This grounding, however, is built on static image-text pairs, whereas manipulation is a continuous, contact-rich process whose dynamics this pretraining cannot capture. We present World Pilot, a VLA framework that augments the policy with priors from a World-Action Model (WAM), routed into the decision chain through two complementary pathways. Latent Steering conditions the perception layer on a scene-evolution latent, and Action Steering supplies an anticipated trajectory as a motion prior to the action generator. Together the two priors equip the VLA with an anticipated view of the scene and a trajectory-level motion hint alongside its semantic conditioning, and the scene-evolution prior remains effective even when supplied by a video-pretrained world model that has not been action-post-trained. World Pilot attains a state-of-the-art Total success rate of 84.7% on the LIBERO-Plus zero-shot OOD benchmark and the highest success rate on every real-robot setting across four manipulation tasks, with the largest margins under shifts in viewpoint, geometry, deformable state, and pose. Project Website: https://world-pilot.github.io/

</details>


### [62] [FACTR 2: Learning External Force Sensing for Commodity Robot Arms Improves Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12406)
*Steven Oh,Jason Jingzhou Liu,Tony Tao,Philip Han,Kenneth Shaw,Satoshi Funabashi,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,Deepak Pathak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NEXT方法，无需专用力传感器即可从自由运动数据中估计外部关节扭矩，并结合FIRST采样策略在行为克隆中提升力感知策略学习，在远程操作和策略学习中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务需要力感知能力，但专用力传感器成本高昂，许多机器人手臂缺乏此类硬件支持。

Method: 提出NEXT（神经外部扭矩估计）数据驱动方法，仅需10分钟自由运动数据、1分钟训练即可估计外部关节扭矩；并设计FIRST（力信息重采样训练）策略，在行为克隆中上采样预接触和接触段数据。

Result: NEXT的估计精度可与专用关节扭矩传感器媲美；在五个长时程任务中，FIRST在任务进度上比先前的力感知策略提升超过17%。

Conclusion: NEXT和FIRST无需额外传感硬件即可为低成本机器人带来力感知远程操作和策略学习能力。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires force sensitivity, but many robot arms lack dedicated force sensors due to their high cost. We present Neural External Torque Estimation (NEXT), a data-driven method that estimates external joint torques without needing any dedicated force sensors. NEXT trains in 1 minute from only 10 minutes of free-motion data, yet achieves estimates comparable to dedicated joint-torque sensors. NEXT enables force-feedback teleoperation on low-cost arms and improves policy learning through Force-Informed Re-Sampling Training (FIRST), which up-samples pre-contact and contact segments during behavior cloning. Across five long-horizon tasks, FIRST outperforms prior force-aware policies by over 17% in task progress. Together, NEXT and FIRST bring force-aware teleoperation and policy learning to off-the-shelf robots without additional sensing hardware. Video results and code are available at https://jasonjzliu.com/factr2

</details>

*Éric Cancès,David Gontier,Solal Perrin-Roussel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究二维材料置于平行电极间（三维环境）的Kohn-Sham DFT模型，证明在狄利克雷边界条件下（库仑相互作用被屏蔽为短程Yukawa型），周期性和准周期性材料的非线性Kohn-Sham DFT模型是适定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为二维材料（如石墨烯、扭曲双层石墨烯等莫尔材料）在平行电极封装环境下的电子结构计算提供严格的数学理论基础，确保模型在物理设定下的适定性。

Method: 将平行电极的狄利克雷边界条件引入Kohn-Sham DFT模型，使库仑相互作用变为短程Yukawa型，在周期性和准周期性（不可公度转角）两种情形下分析非线性模型的适定性。

Result: 证明了在平行电极封装环境下，对于周期性材料（如石墨烯）和准周期性材料（如不可公度转角的扭曲双层石墨烯及其他莫尔材料），非线性Kohn-Sham DFT模型是适定的。

Conclusion: 该工作为二维材料在电极封装环境下的第一性原理计算提供了严格的数学保证，尤其适用于对转角相关莫尔材料电子结构的研究。

Abstract: We study Kohn-Sham Density Functional Theory (DFT) models describing the electronic structure of two-dimensional materials placed in a three-dimensional environment, encapsulated between two parallel conducting electrodes. In this geometry, the Dirichlet boundary conditions at the electrodes screen the Coulomb interaction, which becomes effectively short-ranged, of Yukawa type. We prove that some nonlinear Kohn-Sham DFT models are well-posed in this setting, both for periodic materials (such as graphene) and for quasi-periodic materials (such as twisted bilayer graphene and other moiré materials for generic incommensurate twist angles).

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we give a direct proof of determinantal formulas for connected $k$-point functions for hermitian matrix models. We also give a new proof of KP integrability for them. From the viewpoint of KP hierarchy, we further give a new proof of the explicit formula for the corresponding affine coordinates. Furthermore, duality for some hermitian matrix models is proved.

*P. Zhelnin,J. Dacpano,C. Argüelles*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: TAMBO是一种基于山谷几何结构的中微子望远镜，在1-1000 PeV能量范围内通过前所未有的信噪比区分能力，实现对天体物理中微子点源的精确探测和定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天体物理中微子点源的探测因大气本底信号干扰和look-elsewhere效应带来的统计惩罚而面临巨大挑战，现有手段难以有效区分信号与背景。

Method: TAMBO利用其独特的深谷几何结构（Deep Valley geometry），实现1-1000 PeV能量范围内前所未有的信号-背景区分能力，生成极高纯度的中微子样本。

Result: 初步灵敏度研究表明，TAMBO能够绘制弥散和点源中微子发射图，在高能中微子天文学领域取得显著进展。

Conclusion: TAMBO凭借其独特的山谷布局和优越的信噪比性能，为高能中微子天文学提供了新的探测能力，有望推动中微子源的精确定位与研究。

Abstract: The detection of astrophysical neutrino point sources remains challenging due to atmospheric backgrounds obscuring signal and statistical penalties from the look-elsewhere effect. The Tau Air-shower Mountain-Based Observatory (TAMBO) is a neutrino telescope that achieves unprecedented signal-to-background discrimination in the 1-1000 PeV energy range. Leveraging its unique deep valley geometry, TAMBO will generate an exceptionally pure neutrino sample, enabling precise investigations of neutrino sources. Preliminary sensitivity studies demonstrate TAMBO's potential to map diffuse and point-source neutrino emissions, representing a significant advancement in high-energy neutrino astronomy.

</details>


### [66] [A synchro-curvature treatment of gamma-ray luminosity trends in pulsars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11234)
*A. Pathania,K. K. Singh,K. K. Yadav*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文采用同步-曲率辐射框架解释费米卫星探测到的300多颗高能脉冲星的伽马射线辐射，通过比较观测截断能量与理论预测，确定发射粒子的平衡洛伦兹因子和俯仰角，从而量化曲率辐射和同步辐射在辐射损失中的相对作用，并解释脉冲星群的光度趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高能脉冲星研究通常将伽马辐射单纯归因于曲率辐射或同步辐射机制，缺乏统一框架。本文旨在采用同步-曲率辐射模型，从物理上解释脉冲星伽马射线光度与自转能损之间的标度关系（L_γ ∝ Ė^s, s~0.68），并理解辐射机制的相对贡献。

Method: 采用同步-曲率辐射理论框架，将观测到的伽马射线谱截断能量与理论预测进行比较，并在辐射反应近似下，确定发射带电粒子的平衡洛伦兹因子和俯仰角。

Result: 确定了辐射粒子的平衡洛伦兹因子和俯仰角，量化了曲率辐射与同步辐射在辐射损失中的相对贡献，并给出了脉冲星群L_γ-Ė光度趋势的物理解释。

Conclusion: 同步-曲率辐射框架能够为高能脉冲星的伽马射线辐射提供统一的、物理自洽的解释，揭示了曲率辐射与同步辐射在脉冲星辐射机制中的相对作用，并为脉冲星群的光度标度关系提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In recent years, the Large Area Telescope (LAT) onboard the \emph{Fermi} satellite has detected more than 300 pulsars in the high energy range. The population studies of high energy pulsars show that the gamma ray luminosity of a pulsar ($L_γ$) can be expressed in terms of the spin down luminosity ($\dot{E}$) as $L_γ\propto \dot {E}^s$ having exponent $s\sim 0.68$. This high energy emission, assumed to originate far from the stellar surface and near the light cylinder, is usually studied in either purely curvature or purely synchrotron regime. In this work, we adopt a synchro-curvature radiation framework to understand the origin of gamma ray emission from the pulsar and its implications at the population-level. By comparing the observed cutoff energies of the differential gamma-ray spectra with the theoretical synchro-curvature predictions and enforcing radiation reaction approximation, we determine the equilibrium Lorentz factor and pitch angle of the emitting charged particles. This approach allows to quantify the relative roles of curvature and synchrotron radiation to the radiative losses, thereby providing a physically grounded interpretation of the luminosity trend across the pulsar population.

</details>


### [67] [A phase-coherent timing solution for the X-ray dim isolated neutron star eRASSU J131716.9-402647](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11291)
*J. Kurpas,A. M. Pires,A. D. Schwope,F. Haberl,S. Sheth*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过 NICER 等 X 射线观测，确认 eRASSU J131716.9-402647 为 20 多年来首颗新确认的 X 射线暗淡孤立中子星（XDINS），获得了自旋周期 ~12.8 s、周期导数 ~9×10⁻¹⁴ s s⁻¹、偶极磁场 ~3×10¹³ G 等关键参数，并发现其热光度超出自旋能量损失率，表明历史再加热过程的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eRASSU J131716.9-402647 基于其热 X 射线辐射和长自旋周期，被认为是最有希望的 XDINS 候选体之一，但需要更详细的时变和能谱特征来确认其分类。

Method: 利用新的 NICER 观测数据结合先前的 X 射线后随观测，对源进行相干计时分析以获得自旋周期及其导数，同时进行能谱建模监测谱态变化，并分析脉冲轮廓的能量依赖性。

Result: 获得相干计时解：P~12.8 s，Ṗ~9×10⁻¹⁴ s s⁻¹，对应偶极磁场 3×10¹³ G，自旋光度 ~10³⁰ erg s⁻¹；15 个月监测期间谱态无显著变化；热光度超过旋转能量损失；双峰脉冲轮廓的能量依赖性与 XDINS RX J1308.6+2127 高度相似，脉冲分数随能量增加。

Conclusion: 结果明确确认 eRASSU J131716.9-402647 为 XDINS，这是 20 多年来首次新确认的该类天体，其热演化受历史再加热过程显著影响。

Abstract: Based on its predominantly thermal X-ray emission and long spin period, the isolated neutron star eRASSU J131716.9-402647 is one of the most promising candidates for membership in the still small class of X-ray dim isolated neutron stars (XDINSs). Confirmation of this classification, however, requires a more detailed characterisation of the source's timing and spectral properties. In this work, we present new NICER observations which, together with previous X-ray follow-up, allow us to constrain the timing properties and long-term evolution of eRASSU J131716.9-402647. We obtain a coherent timing solution with a spin period of $P\sim12.8$ s and a period derivative of $\dot{P}\sim9\times 10^{-14}$ s s$^{-1}$, which best-describes the spin evolution of the source. These parameters imply a dipolar magnetic field strength of $3\times10^{13}$ G and a spin-down luminosity of order $10^{30}$ erg s$^{-1}$. Spectral modelling reveals no significant change in the spectral state over the 15 months of observational monitoring and indicates a thermal luminosity that likely exceeds the rotational energy loss. This suggests a thermal evolution that has been significantly influenced by past reheating. The energy dependence of the double-humped pulse profile closely resembles that observed in the XDINS RX J1308.6+2127, with the pulsed fraction increasing towards higher energies. Taken together, these results unambiguously confirm the XDINS nature of eRASSU J131716.9-402647, making it the first newly confirmed XDINS in more than two decades.

</details>


### [68] [Intermediate States in Chaotic Triple Evolution and Applications to Black Hole Merger Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11294)
*Dina Meylakh,Nicholas C. Stone,Nathan W. C. Leigh*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于统计力学重新推导了三体相互作用的椭圆结果分布，将其与双曲分布结合，并利用蒙特卡洛算法模拟稠密星团中重复的二元-单体散射，量化了可观测偏心引力波合并事件的比例。研究发现椭圆OEM占总椭圆合并的约32-63%，OEM占总合并比例为2.6-4.4%（当前LIGO/Virgo灵敏度下为1.6-3.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三体相互作用中混沌态与层级运动交替出现，层级运动产生的双星可能引发引力波并合，但混沌态的结果对最终引力波事件有决定性影响。现有椭圆结果分布模型使用了两个自由参数，且对偏心引力波合并（OEM）的量化研究不足，因此需要更精确的模型来预测星团中可观测偏心引力波事件。

Method: 利用平衡态统计力学重新推导椭圆结果分布（将自由参数从两个减少为一个），结合双曲分布，并与N体模拟对比验证；基于两种结果分布设计蒙特卡洛算法，模拟稠密星团（质量范围10^5-10^7 M⊙）中重复的二元-单体散射过程，量化OEM事件。

Result: 新模型将椭圆结果分布的自由参数从两个减为一个，除极低角动量三体系统外，预测的解体概率与N体模拟一致。椭圆OEM占椭圆合并的32-63%，OEM占总合并比例为2.6-4.4%（f_min=10 Hz, e_min=0.1）；在当前LIGO/Virgo灵敏度（f_min≈34.4 Hz）下OEM比例为1.6-3.1%。星团总质量对弹出双星比例影响显著。

Conclusion: 简化后的椭圆结果分布模型有效，蒙特卡洛算法可用于预测星团中偏心引力波事件。星团质量越大，OEM比例越高，但当前探测器灵敏度限制了可观测OEM事件的数量。

Abstract: Three-body interactions exhibit phases of strong chaotic evolution as well as hierarchical motion where one body separates from a binary and follows a hyperbolic or elliptic trajectory around it. The binaries produced during phases of hierarchical motion may lead to gravitational wave (GW) inspirals, but this depends on the outcomes of the chaotic states. In this paper we re-derive the elliptic outcome distribution using equilibrium statistical mechanics and explore it together with the hyperbolic distribution. When comparing to N-body simulations, we find that we can reduce the elliptic outcome model to one free parameter instead of the previously used two and that the predicted disintegration probabilities agree except for very low angular momentum triples. We then use both outcome distributions along with a star cluster model to design a Monte Carlo algorithm for repeated binary-single scatterings within dense star systems. We explore star cluster masses of $[10^5 - 10^7] M_{\odot}$, with the goal of quantifying observably eccentric merger (OEM) GWs, visible to instruments such as LIGO and Virgo. Assuming an OEM detection sensitivity of $f_{\rm min}=10 {\rm Hz}, e_{\rm min} = 0.1$, we find the elliptic OEMs are about $\sim (32 - 63)\%$ of the total elliptic mergers and that the total cluster mass greatly impacts the fraction of ejected binaries. The OEM to total merger fraction (OEM fraction) is found to be $(2.6 - 4.4)\%$. Considering the detection sensitivity that GW interferometers have today $(f_{\rm min} \simeq 34.4 {\rm Hz})$ we obtain the OEM fraction in the $(1.6 - 3.1)\%$ range.

</details>


### [69] [A magnetar formation in binary neutron star merger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11299)
*Kenta Kiuchi,Alexis Reboul-Salze,Yuichiro Sekiguchi,Masaru Shibata*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 使用日本超算富岳，以6.25米分辨率对1.35-1.35太阳质量双中子星进行了全局相对论中微子辐射输运磁流体动力学模拟，消耗约5.3亿核时。初始磁场3.16×10^12 G，发现并合后3 ms内K-H不稳定性将磁场放大至电磁饱和能量~10^50 erg，磁能谱符合Kazantsev谱，动能谱符合Kolmogorov谱，星体尺度磁场增强至少316倍，表明中子星并合后可在数毫秒内暂时形成磁星。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究双中子星并合后磁场的快速放大机制及其是否能在短时间内形成磁星，填补高分辨率模拟在这一问题上的空白。

Method: 在富岳超算上开展全局广义相对论中微子辐射输运磁流体动力学（MHD）模拟，空间分辨率达6.25米，初始最大磁场强度设为3.16×10^12 G，与观测到的双脉冲星上限一致。

Result: 并合后3 ms内K-H不稳定性将磁场放大至电磁饱和能量~10^50 erg；磁功率谱密度呈现Kazantsev谱，动能谱呈现Kolmogorov谱；星体尺度磁场增强至少316倍。

Conclusion: 中子星并合后可在数毫秒内暂时形成磁星。

Abstract: We conduct a global general relativistic neutrino-radiation-transfer magnetohydrodynamics simulation of a $1.35$-$1.35M_\odot$ binary neutron star with the unprecedented spatial resolution of $6.25$\,m on the Japanese supercomputer FUGAKU. The total consumed CPU time is $\approx 530$ million core hours. We initialize the binary neutron star's magnetic field to be $3.16\times 10^{12}$~G at maximum, which is compatible with the upper end of the observed binary pulsars. We demonstrate that the Kelvin-Helmholtz instability that emerges when the two neutron stars touch amplifies the magnetic field to an expected electromagnetic saturation energy of $\sim 10^{50}$~erg within $3$~ms after the merger. However, many observations of SSA show that the spectral index at frequencies below the peak is less than the expected $+2.5$ (non-thermal) or $+2$ (thermal). We argue that an inhomogeneous emitting region is the most likely explanation in many cases. Power law inhomogeneous cylindrical slab and broken power law inhomogeneous sphere models are used to investigate how the presence of inhomogeneity affects parameter estimates using traditional SSA methods. We find that in some cases inhomogeneity can lead to traditional SSA methods underestimating the minimum energy and the size of the emitting region by over an order of magnitude. Quantitative correction factors are found which can be applied to traditional estimates to correct for inhomogeneity, depending on the value of the observed flattened spectral index and the range in frequency over which this value is observed. Furthermore, we derive simple correction factors for non-spherical homogeneous emitting regions. Finally, we explore the effects of inhomogeneity on measurements of polarisation around the spectral peak, and on lightcurves for expanding emitting regions.

Abstract: Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) images of black holes appear as rings with a brightness asymmetry. Here, we expand on our previous study of the asymmetry magnitude $a_1$ to study the position angle of the peak brightness asymmetry $\mathrm{PA}_1$ in general relativistic magnetohydrodynamic (GRMHD) models. For larger spin magnitudes ($a_{*}>0$ and $a_{*}\lesssim-0.5$), the mean $\mathrm{PA}_1$ falls within $1σ$ of the approaching limb of the black hole, regardless of viewing inclination, disk magnetization, or source. By comparing the $(a_1, \mathrm{PA}_1)$ distribution in M87* observations with models, we demonstrate that we can mildly disfavor low-magnitude spins and strongly disfavor all spin vectors that point toward Earth. The alignment of $\mathrm{PA}_1$ relative to the large-scale jet axis may suggest that M87*'s disk does not have a large tilt. By combining $\mathrm{PA}_1$ with the pattern speed measured in optimistic 2026 M87* video conditions, the EHT can constrain whether M87* is prograde or retrograde with $\sim 84\%$ accuracy. In Sgr A*, we show that a detection of $(a_1, \mathrm{PA}_1)$ could constrain the magnitude and direction of the galactic center spin vector. Finally, if future EHT expansions increase the sample of horizon-scale sources, a simple set of observables (ring diameter, asymmetry magnitude, and asymmetry angle) could enable robust constraints on black hole mass, spin, and inclination.

Abstract: The source of the optical/UV emission in tidal disruption events (TDEs) remains an enduring question in the field. To explore this connection, we ran time-dependent 1D radiation hydrodynamic simulations of TDE emission with the Sedona monte carlo radiative transfer code, focusing on the reprocessing paradigm. Our simulations follow a compact, evolving X-ray and EUV bright source and surrounding reprocessing outflow over multiple months, using luminosities and mass flow rates consistent with hydro simulations of tidal disruptions. We determine the efficiency of reprocessing as a function of time in this dramatically changing environment and reproduce key observables including timescales, luminosities, and color evolution. Notably, we see a strong wavelength-dependence in the emission timescale due to reprocessing effects. Early on there is an X-ray flare which quickly fades as material builds up and obscures the hot source. At the same time, the optical/UV luminosity begins to rise. Though the optical/UV light curve has a similar shape to the bolometric light curve, the optical peak is offset by $\sim$3 weeks from the bolometric peak due to the time required to build up the reprocessing layer. This implies that early time, high energy emission may be missed for TDEs discovered in optical surveys, and the initial disruption and mass return time to the black hole may occur earlier than optical light curves suggest.

To validate the Newtonian version of this criterion, we performed axisymmetric numerical simulations of non-relativistic transonic rotating flows which are stable according to the original Rayleigh criterion but can be either stable or unstable according to the new one. The results of computer simulations are found to be in perfect agreement with the theory. The hydrodynamic stability of accretion disks is often explained by referring to the original Rayleigh criterion, even if their rotation is highly supersonic. To clarify the matter, we analysed the hydrodynamic stability of flows rotating about central compact object and derived an instability criterion that retains the explicit dependence on the flow Mach number. Abstract: Most galaxies host supermassive black holes (SMBHs) that remain weakly accreting or dormant for much of their lifetimes. At the lowest accretion rates, these systems may represent the transition between active nuclei and dormant black holes, but whether they can still launch collimated jets remains unclear. The nuclei in our Galaxy (\sgra) and M31 are key examples of this regime, although no clear jet structure has yet been detected in either source. Here we report multi-frequency very long baseline interferometric observations of \Msixty\ (NGC~4649), a nearby elliptical galaxy hosting a nearly dormant SMBH with an Eddington ratio of $\sim10^{-8}$. We detect a compact two-sided jet with an unusually steep synchrotron spectrum, demonstrating that collimated outflows can persist even under nearly dormant accretion conditions. The apparent radio core exhibits an unprecedentedly steep frequency-dependent position shift toward the SMBH, locating the central engine only $\sim57\,μ$as, corresponding to a projected distance of $\sim10$ Schwarzschild radii, upstream of the 8.37-GHz core. The observed jet morphology and steep core-shift behaviour are reproduced by general relativistic magnetohydrodynamic and radiative-transfer simulations, indicating a magnetically dominated, non-equipartition jet-launching region that departs from the standard conical equipartition picture. These results provide direct observational evidence that jet production can survive near the dormant SMBHs and establish \Msixty\ as a unique laboratory for probing jet formation on event-horizon scales in the lowest-accretion SMBH regime.

Abstract: With the aim to study the spectral properties of some X-ray sources from recently observed {\it Chandra} data, 9 galaxies which have been observed by {\it Chandra} ACIS-S during the year 2018 to 2022 have been considered for the present work. 27 sources with net source counts $ \ge$ 100 have been considered. The spectra of all the sources were fitted using two empirical models -- an absorbed powerlaw and an absorbed disk blackbody. From their estimated bolometric luminosities, the 27 X-ray sources are categorized as 6 X-ray binaries (XRBs) and 21 Ultraluminous X-ray sources (ULXs). All the six XRBs are found to be in the spectrally hard state ($Γ\sim$ 1.52-2.29) which indeed may be due to thermal comptonization. Only one ULX, CXOUJ032251.2-370950 (X-5), was found to be spectrally soft while the remaining 20 ULXs were spectrally hard. The spectral parameters of X-5 with an inner disk temperature (kT$_{in}$) $\sim $ 0.5 keV and an estimated bolometric luminosity, L$_X \sim$ 3.26 $\times$ 10$^{39}$ erg s$^{-1} $ requires a black hole of mass, M$_{BH} \sim$ 137.86$^{+66.62}_{-47.41}$ M$_\odot $ accreting at $ \sim$ 0.19 times its Eddington limit. 8 ULXs, X-4, X-8, X-9, X-10, X-11, X-12, X-20 and X-21, were found to be in the Extremely luminous X-ray sources (ELXs) regime with even their lower limit of luminosity $>$ 10$^{40}$ erg s$^{-1}$. Softening/Hardening of spectra with or without changes in the luminosity were also observed in some ULXs/ELXs. In the hard ELX, X-8, spectral softening with almost consistent luminosity was observed. While in the ULXs X-20 and X-25 spectral softening with increasing luminosity was observed. However spectral hardening with increase in luminosity were observed in the ULXs X-21 and X-26.

TL;DR: 本研究利用H.E.S.S.和Fermi-LAT观测的大量耀变体伽马射线谱，对质量约10 eV的轴子类粒子（ALPs）作为暗物质候选者进行约束。通过分析ALPs衰变对河外星系背景光（EBL）的贡献及其对伽马射线光深的影响，在ALPs占全部暗物质密度的假设下，在质量~15 eV处将光子-ALP耦合强度限制到$g_{aγ} \sim 7 \times 10^{-12}$ GeV$^{-1}$（95%置信度），填补了此前$m_a \sim 2.5-20$ eV参数空间的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极高能伽马射线在传播过程中会与河外星系背景光（EBL）发生相互作用产生正负电子对，导致其流强被衰减。轴子类粒子（ALPs）作为新物理假说，若质量为~10 eV量级并构成暗物质，其衰变产生的光子会增强EBL强度，从而进一步增加伽马射线的光学深度。该研究旨在利用现有伽马射线观测数据检验这一新物理场景，并对ALPs参数进行约束。

Method: 利用H.E.S.S.和Fermi-LAT观测的大量耀变体伽马射线谱样本，通过建模衰变ALPs对EBL的贡献，计算其对不同红移处耀变体谱的影响，并与标准EBL模型进行比较，从而对ALPs的质量和光子耦合强度进行统计分析。

Result: 在ALPs占全部暗物质密度的假设下，获得95%置信度的排斥限：在质量$m_a\sim 15$ eV处，光子-ALP耦合强度下限达$g_{aγ} \sim 7 \times 10^{-12}$ GeV$^{-1}$，覆盖了$m_a \sim 2.5-20$ eV参数空间此前未被约束的区域。

Conclusion: 该约束与现有天体物理界限具有竞争力，为其他探测技术提供了互补灵敏度，填补了重ALPs参数空间中的空白区域。

Abstract: The propagation of very-high-energy (VHE; $E_γ \geq 100$ GeV) gamma rays from extragalactic sources is affected by interactions with photons of the extragalactic background light (EBL), resulting in pair production that attenuates the intrinsic gamma-ray flux. This interaction renders the Universe increasingly opaque to VHE photons at high energies and redshifts. New physics scenarios involving axion-like particles (ALPs) could modify this expected optical depth. In particular, ALPs with masses $m_a \sim 10$ eV can decay into two photons over cosmological timescales, thereby contributing to the diffuse EBL. If such ALPs constitute a significant fraction of the dark matter density, their decay would enhance the EBL intensity and consequently increase the gamma-ray optical depth. In this study, we investigate this scenario using a large sample of gamma-ray spectra observed with the High Energy Stereoscopic System (H.E.S.S.) and the Fermi Large Area Telescope. We model the contribution of decaying ALPs to the EBL and assess their impact on the spectra of blazars across redshifts. By comparing these observations with standard EBL models, we place constraints on the properties of heavy ALPs, specifically their mass and photon coupling, and evaluate their viability as a dark matter candidate capable of modifying the gamma-ray transparency of the Universe. From the combined analysis, and under the assumption that ALPs constitute the entire dark matter density, we derive 95% confidence exclusion limits on the photon-ALP coupling down to $g_{aγ} \sim 7 \times 10^{-12}$ GeV$^{-1}$ for masses $m_a\sim 15$ eV. These constraints are competitive with existing astrophysical bounds and provide complementary sensitivity to other techniques, closing a previously unconstrained region of parameter space in the $m_a \sim 2.5$-$20$ eV range.

### [79] [Detection of a parsec-scale, compact, and fading ejecta from an accreting massive black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11939)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矮星系是搜寻中等质量黑洞（IMBH）的理想目标，特别是当它们表现出强烈的吸积和抛射活动时。矮星系SDSS J101747.09+393207.7显示出极高的X射线光度和X形光学结构（可能由矮-矮并合引起），为此需要探索其中潜在的IMBH抛射活动。

Method: 使用甚长基线干涉测量（VLBI）在4.9 GHz频率上对该矮星系进行观测。

Result: 在光学质心附近探测到一个毫角秒尺度、致密、亚微央斯基的射电成分。结合现有射电巡天数据，该射电成分在2015年之前未被探测到；在2019-2025年间呈光学薄陡谱且流量密度递减。

Conclusion: 该射电成分被鉴定为不稳定的吸积过程产生的短寿命、罕见喷流抛射物，可能在未来几十年内消失。这表明矮星系中吸积IMBH可能存在短寿命、爆发性的喷流活动。

Abstract: Dwarf galaxies, characterized by their low luminosities and masses, are excellent candidates for searches for intermediate-mass black holes (IMBHs), particularly when they show strong accretion and ejection activity. The dwarf galaxy SDSS J101747.09+393207.7 has recently been found to display a very high X-ray luminosity and an X-shaped optical structure, possibly caused by a dwarf--dwarf merger. To explore its potential IMBH ejection activity, we performed very long baseline interferometry (VLBI) observations at 4.9 GHz. In this work, we present the detection of a milliarcsecond-scale, compact, sub-microjansky radio component near the optical centroid. According to some existing radio sky survey data, the radio component was not detected until 2015; it displayed an optically thin steep radio spectrum and declining flux densities across 0.8--5 GHz from 2019 to 2025. Therefore, we identify it as a short-lived and rarely seen ejecta that was produced by unstable accretion onto a massive black hole and likely faded away in a few decades. These results indicate that short-lived, episodic jet activity from accreting IMBHs in dwarf galaxies might exist.

</details>


### [80] [Identifiability of $g$ mode Resonances in Eccentric Binary Neutron Stars with Multidetector Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11959)
*Mengfei Sun,Jie Wu,Qianning Hu,Jin Li,Nan Yang,Xianghe Ma,Borui Wang,Minghui Zhang,Yuanhong Zhong*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了在偏心双中子星系统中，利用第三代引力波探测器（ET和CE）通过深度学习识别微弱g模式共振相位信号的能力。实验表明，深度学习模型在ET+CE联合观测下准确率达0.897，优于匹配滤波方法（0.689），证明了神经网络在捕捉弱相位形态特征方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏心双中子星系统中的g模式共振是探测内部层结、超流性、成分梯度和物态方程的潜在探针。虽然当前探测器灵敏度难以分辨这类弱动力学潮汐信号，但第三代观测可能使其可及，因此需要开发能够识别微弱共振相位偏移的方法，以获取超越整体绝热潮汐形变能力的信息。

Method: 构建了包含点粒子、绝热潮汐、共振g模式和纯噪声四类数据集的偏心谐波框架。使用爱因斯坦望远镜（ET）和宇宙探索者（CE）探测器数据，训练深度学习模型对含噪时域应变进行分类，并与匹配滤波方法进行对比。

Result: 深度学习模型在ET、CE和ET+CE上分别达到0.655、0.815和0.897的准确率；匹配滤波方法仅达到0.514、0.677和0.689的准确率。深度学习显著优于匹配滤波，且联合观测提升了识别性能。

Conclusion: 共振修正表现为叠加在绝热潮汐背景上的弱相位形态差异，而匹配滤波只对整体相似性敏感。深度学习中的神经分类器能够从完整时域应变片段中更好地学习这些局部相位和形态特征，从而在弱相位差异情况下表现更优。联合第三代观测可提高微弱内模相位信息的可识别性。

Abstract: $g$ mode resonances in eccentric binary neutron star systems are potential probes of internal stratification, superfluidity, composition gradients, and the equation of state. Although such weak dynamical tidal signatures are unlikely to be resolved with current detector sensitivities, third generation observations may make them accessible, in which case identifying the weak resonant phase shift would provide information beyond the bulk adiabatic tidal deformability. We build a four class dataset in an eccentric harmonic framework, containing point particle, adiabatic tide, resonant $g$ mode, and pure noise samples, and use Einstein Telescope (ET) and Cosmic Explorer (CE) detector data to test whether this weak resonant phase signature can be identified from noisy time domain strain. The ET, CE, and ET+CE deep learning models reach accuracies of $0.655$, $0.815$, and $0.897$, respectively. On the same simulated samples, the matched filtering method reaches lower accuracies of $0.514$, $0.677$, and $0.689$. This result arises from the fact that the resonant correction manifests as a weak phase morphology difference superimposed on the adiabatic tidal background, whereas matched filtering is sensitive only to the overall similarity. Hence, in the presence of weak phase differences, the neural classifier employed in deep learning is better able to learn these local phase and morphology features from the complete time domain strain segment. The results indicate that joint third generation observations improve the identifiability of weak internal mode phase information.

</details>


### [81] [Emergent gravity from Michel flow with position dependent adiabatic index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11964)
*Apashanka Das,Souvik Ghose,Tapas K. Das*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义相对论框架下球对称Bondi吸积（Michel流）的多组分稳态解、相空间拓扑、线性径向扰动稳定性及对应的声学时空中事件视界的Carter-Penrose因果结构，从天体物理、动力系统与经典模拟引力三个视角全面分析了吸积黑洞系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Michel流推广到多方指数随径向距离变化的多组分吸积情形，并从动力系统与模拟引力等多个视角统一研究吸积黑洞系统。

Method: 构建变绝热指数的状态方程下的广义相对论稳态跨声速积分解；利用动力系统理论分类跨声速点性质；施加线性径向扰动分析稳定性；提取声学时空中嵌入的度规并构造Carter-Penrose图识别声学视界。

Result: 获得了多组分Michel流的相空间流形，分类了跨声速点的动力学类型；证明了稳态解在线性径向扰动下稳定；构建了相应的声学时空度规并通过Carter-Penrose图标识了声学视界位置。

Conclusion: 该工作从天体物理、动力系统与经典模拟引力三个互补视角统一刻画了吸积黑洞系统，展示了Michel流作为模拟引力平台的潜力。

Abstract: Spherically symmetric, general relativistic Bondi accretion is known as the Michel flow. The stationary integral transonic solutions for the Michel flow has been constructed for multi-component accretion described by an equation of state where the adiabatic index varies with the radial distance along which the streamlines are studied, and the corresponding phase portrait spanned by such radial distance and the flow Mach number has been obtained. Borrowing the techniques used in the dynamical systems theory, the nature of the transonic points of the aforementioned flow has been classified. The steady state flow has been perturbed to study the stability of the stationary solutions, and it has been found that such flows are stable under the (linear) radial perturbation. As a consequence of the stability analysis, the corresponding acoustic space time embedded within the accreting matter has been obtained, and the horizon of the metric of such sonic space time has been identified by constructing the causal structure with the help of the Carter-Penrose diagrams. In this way, the accreting black hole systems in the general relativistic set up has been investigated from various different perspectives - from its astrophysical aspects, from the dynamical systems point of view, as well as within the realm of the classical analogue gravity phenomena.

</details>


### [82] [Searching for cosmic vortices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12049)
*Marek Nikołajuk,Tomasz Karpiuk,Mirosław Brewczyk*

TL;DR: 本文研究冷氦白矮星被黑洞强烈潮汐瓦解的过程。将白矮星建模为玻色-费米液滴，利用量子流体动力学方程模拟双星系统演化。白矮星经过近星点时大量质量损失并落入黑洞形成吸积盘，盘中出现量子化涡旋产生特征性闪烁电磁辐射信号（时标约几秒）。同时白矮星远离黑洞，其表面涡旋使其几何拉长、旋转并发射引力波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究冷氦白矮星被黑洞强烈潮汐瓦解的动力学过程及其可观测信号。

Method: 将白矮星建模为玻色-费米液滴，使用量子流体动力学方程进行数值模拟。

Result: 白矮星经过近星点大量质量损失落入黑洞形成吸积盘；盘中产生量子化涡旋，表现为时标约几秒的特征性闪烁电磁辐射信号；白矮星表面涡旋使其拉长旋转并发射引力波。

Conclusion: 白矮星的潮汐瓦解产生多信使信号：吸积盘量子涡旋导致的电磁闪烁（秒时标）和白矮星形变旋转产生的引力波辐射。

Abstract: Our study focuses on the strong tidal disruption of a cold helium white dwarf passing a black hole. We model the white dwarf as a Bose-Fermi droplet and use quantum hydrodynamic equations to simulate the binary system's evolution. As the white dwarf passes through periastron, it loses a significant amount of mass. This mass falls onto the black hole and forms an accretion disc. Quantized vortices appear in the accretion disc, manifesting as strong electromagnetic radiation signals that exhibit characteristic flickering patterns changing on a timescale of a few seconds. Meanwhile, the white dwarf moves away from the black hole. As the white dwarf moves through space, vortices run along its surface. This elongates its geometry, causing it to rotate and emit gravitational waves.

</details>


### [83] [Broadened Lensing Rings of Compact Boson Stars: Enhanced Imprint of Accretion Flow in Images and Visibilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12092)

Abstract: In this work, we systematically study the gravitational lensing properties and observational signatures of compact boson stars. Unlike black holes, the photon effective potential of a compact boson star develops a nearly flat region, whose width increases with the compactness of the star. This flat structure significantly broadens the range of impact parameters that can produce large-angle deflections, leading to noticeably wider lensing rings of all orders. Photons constituting these rings traverse more complex paths, rendering the resulting images more sensitive to the spatial distribution of the accretion flow. Ray tracing results show that, compared to black hole models, the image topology and visibility amplitudes of compact boson stars exhibit a stronger dependence on the accretion flow structure. These results highlight qualitative differences in the observational properties of compact boson stars and black holes.

</details>
