Abstract: SN 1006 is a historical Type Ia supernova remnant that exhibits non-thermal emission ranging from radio to multi-TeV $γ$-rays. Most of this emission (particularly X-rays and $γ$-rays) is concentrated in polar caps aligned with the ambient magnetic field, which makes it an ideal laboratory for studying cosmic ray (CR) acceleration at different shock obliquities and the hadronic/leptonic nature of the $γ$-ray emission. We model SN 1006's morphology, multi-wavelength spectrum, and radial profile using a self-consistent multi-zone kinetic model of particle acceleration that accounts for: CR-driven shock modification, magnetic field amplification, drift in magnetic fluctuations, and temporal dynamics including adiabatic and synchrotron losses. Our model can reproduce both the observed spectral and spatial properties, with the exception of the radio profile that we argue requires 3D hydrodynamic effects to replicate. We find that quasi-parallel regions (where the shock normal aligns with the ambient magnetic field) exhibit very prominent CR acceleration ($\sim$20% efficiency), while quasi-perpendicular regions exhibit efficiencies below 1%, consistent with the results of kinetic simulations. We also find that electrons are responsible for the majority of the $γ$-ray emission from SN 1006 (i.e., it is a leptonic source), with the exception of the northwest region due to an encounter with a dense cloud.

</details>

Result: 先前报告的χ_eff偏斜实际上是高斯主体（中心在小χ_eff附近）之外存在扩展到更大|χ_eff|的额外结构。大多数质量区间存在正χ_eff过剩，仅在m1∈[46,65]M⊙区间需要负χ_eff成分（23:1概率比）。

Abstract: The distribution of the effective inspiral spin ($χ_\mathrm{eff}$) of the binary black holes detected by LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA can shed light on their formation pathways. We analyze the GWTC-5.0 dataset with two models-one flexible, one fully parametric-that jointly describe $χ_\mathrm{eff}$ and primary mass. We clarify that the previously-reported skewness in the $χ_\mathrm{eff}$ distribution is better understood as additional structure beyond a non-skewed Gaussian bulk centered at small $χ_\mathrm{eff}$. This additional structure extends to larger $|χ_\mathrm{eff}|$, a result previously reported using GWTC-4.0 data. We measure the asymmetry of the distribution of $χ_\mathrm{eff}$ outside the Gaussian bulk from the data. With both the parametric and the flexible analyses, we find tentative evidence for a mass-dependent excess of positive $χ_\mathrm{eff}$ over negative ones outside the Gaussian bulk. Only at $m_1 \in [46,65]\,M_\odot$ do the data require a negative $χ_\mathrm{eff}$ component outside the Gaussian bulk, with $23\text{:}1$ odds. If $χ_\mathrm{eff}$ outside the Gaussian bulk are produced by hierarchical mergers-as it has been suggested-then a fraction of those mergers may be produced in environments that can generate a surplus of binaries with positive $χ_\mathrm{eff}$, such as the disks of active galactic nuclei.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文通过相对论磁流体动力学模拟和辐射传输计算，发现AGN喷流的射电-光学中心偏移主要出现在喷流功率$L_{\rm j} \sim 10^{42.5} - 10^{44}\,\rm{erg}\,\rm{s}^{-1}$范围内，是由恒星风质量加载引起喷流减速所致，偏移量约0.1-4毫角秒，并可解释观测到的红移演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLBI和Gaia天体测量发现AGN射电与光学中心存在系统性毫角秒级偏移，但传统上仅用不透明度解释射电核心位移，无法解释光学中心相对于射电核心的下游偏移。作者旨在揭示这种偏移的物理起源及其对喷流能量耗散机制的约束。

Result: 射电-光学偏移仅在喷流功率$10^{42.5}-10^{44}\,\rm{erg}\,\rm{s}^{-1}$范围内出现；恒星风在数百至数千秒差距处触发喷流失速，使光学中心下移；偏移量0.1-4 mas（红移1处对应数十秒差距）；偏移取决于恒星分布、视角和光学喷流主导程度；成功再现了偏移发生率随红移演化的观测特征。

Conclusion: 射电-光学偏移可约束AGN喷流功率及喷流与宿主星系的耦合，提供了独立于传统射电瓣方法的诊断手段。恒星质量加载虽非唯一耗散机制，但作为星系核区不可避免的过程，是能量耗散的自然基线。

Abstract: Recent Very Long Baseline Interferometry (VLBI) and Gaia astrometry reveal systematic milliarcsecond-scale offsets between the radio and optical centroids of active galactic nuclei (AGN). These "radio-optical offsets" do not alter the standard opacity-driven interpretation of radio core shifts. Instead, they indicate that the optical emission centroid is frequently displaced downstream of the radio synchrotron optical depth $τ= 1$ surface, implying that additional dissipation and particle reacceleration occur beyond the opacity radio core within relativistic jets. We perform steady-state, axisymmetric relativistic magnetohydrodynamic (RMHD) simulations of AGN jets, including baryonic mass-load from stellar winds, varying jet kinetic power, and stellar core radius. Synthetic synchrotron emission maps in radio and optical bands are generated via a radiative transfer code, and centroid offsets are extracted for comparison with observations. Parsec-scale radio-optical offsets arise only for jet powers $L_{\rm j} \sim 10^{42.5} - 10^{44}\,\rm{erg}\,\rm{s}^{-1}$. In this regime, stellar winds trigger jet deceleration at intrinsic distances of a few $10^2-10^3\,\rm{pc}$, shifting the optical centroid downstream and producing offsets of $\sim 0.1 - 4\,\rm{mas}$ (a few tens of parsecs at $z=1$). Offsets depend on stellar distribution, viewing angle, and optical jet dominance, and vanish outside this power range. We reproduce the observed redshift evolution of offset incidence, linking it to the cosmic evolution of thermally pulsing asymptotic giant branch (TP-AGB) mass loss. Although stellar mass loading is unlikely to be the sole dissipation mechanism, its unavoidable presence in galactic nuclei makes it a natural baseline for energy dissipation. Radio-optical offsets therefore offer a constraint on AGN jet power and jet-host coupling, independent of traditional lobe-based methods.

</details>


### [87] [Time-dependent cosmic-ray escape from wind bubbles: hard spectra formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12390)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星风气泡作为动态系统能够加速宇宙线，但传统研究多采用稳态近似。本文旨在探究时变粒子输运描述如何影响逃逸宇宙线通量，以更真实地刻画这一动态过程。

Method: 采用球对称模型，宇宙线在终止激波处连续注入，通过对流和扩散传播至时变的前向激波自由逃逸边界。将一维球对称时变输运方程转化为随机微分方程组，利用修改版CRPropa框架求解。

Result: 星风驱动阶段，下游逃逸谱可比传统DSA预期的E^{-2}更硬；低能端谱形显著依赖于湍流模型，不同湍流实现可产生可区分的低能抑制特征；低能粒子可能被有效束缚，影响多信使辐射和宇宙线累积grammage。

Conclusion: 时变效应导致星风气泡的宇宙线逃逸谱与传统稳态预期不同，低能谱形可作为区分湍流模型的观测特征，并对多信使辐射和宇宙线累积grammage有可观测含义。

Abstract: Overview: Wind-driven bubbles are dynamic systems that can accelerate cosmic rays, depending on their physical properties, up to very high energies. We investigate how a time-dependent description of the particle transport may impact the escaping cosmic-ray flux. Model: The wind bubble system is modeled as spherically symmetric. Cosmic rays are continuously injected at the position of the termination shock and propagate through advection and diffusion until the escape at the time-dependent position of the forward shock, which is treated as a free escape boundary. Methods: The one-dimensional spherical time-dependent transport equation is solved by transforming it into the corresponding set of stochastic differential equations, and integrated with a modified version of the open source cosmic-ray propagation framework CRPropa. Results: We find that, during the wind driven phase, the downstream escaping spectra from wind bubbles can be harder than $\sim E^{-2}$, the conventional expectation from diffusive shock acceleration. Depending on the turbulence model the initial energy spectrum can be significantly suppressed at lowest energies, which could be an observable feature to distinguish between different turbulence realizations. This effect could lead to an efficient confinement of low energy particles, potentially leading to observable implication in terms of multi-messenger radiation and cosmic-ray accumulated grammage within the bubble.

</details>

### [88] [Tensor Product $K$-theory is Rational Algebraic $K$-theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11412)
*Amartya Shekhar Dubey,Mattie Ji*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于含幺交换环R，在线性张量积（而非直和）下对有限生成自由R-模的对称幺半范畴进行群完备化，所得结果是代数K-理论空间K(R)的有理化的π₀提升。进一步推广到任意非平凡乘法闭子集S⊆ℤ>₀的局部化，以及p-完备化情形，并给出了无需乘法无穷循环空间理论的加构造证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典代数K-理论K(R)是通过直和下的群完备化构造的，那么若改为张量积结构进行群完备化会得到什么？这是一个自然且尚未被正式证明的"民间定理"（folklore theorem），需要给出直接证明。

Method: 使用加构造（plus-construction）方法，避免了乘法无穷循环空间理论的复杂工具。通过分析张量积群完备化与有理化、p-完备化以及一般局部化之间的关系，给出直接证明。

Result: 张量积群完备化得到K(R)的有理化（up to π₀）；类似地可得到p-完备化；对任意非平凡乘法闭子集S⊆ℤ>₀的局部化也可实现。这些结论也可从May的局部化定理导出。

Conclusion: 张量积群完备化与K-理论的有理化、局部化等操作存在深刻联系，为理解K-理论的不同完备化结构提供了新的视角和简化证明。

Abstract: For a commutative ring $R$ with unity, its algebraic $K$-theory space $K(R)$ may be obtained by group-completing the symmetric monoidal category of finitely generated free $R$-modules under direct sum. A natural question is what happens when one group-completes with respect to the tensor product structure instead. In this note, we give a direct proof of the folklore theorem that the resulting group-completion is the rationalization of $K(R)$, up to $π_0$. We also discuss how a similar group-completion would give the $p$-perfection and, more generally, the localization of $K(R)$ at any non-trivial multiplicatively closed subset $S \subseteq \mathbb{Z}_{> 0}$. The localization statement can be recovered from a localization theorem of May. We give a plus-construction proof without using the full machinery of multiplicative infinite loop space theory.

</details>


### [89] [Relative dendroidal Rezk nerve and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11895)
*Kensuke Arakawa,Victor Carmona,Francesca Pratali*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文将树状Rezk神经扩展到相对∞-operads设置，通过将其与∞-operads的局部化联系起来，推广了Mazel-Gee的定理，并以此获得了一个在operadic背景下证明局部化结果的高效工具。应用包括推广Willwacher关于循环operads和operadic模的结果，以及用离散几何描述球面上的局部常值分解代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将树状Rezk神经推广到相对∞-operads，并建立其与∞-operads局部化的联系，从而提供一个证明operadic局部化结果的通用有效工具。

Method: 通过扩展树状Rezk神经到相对∞-operads设置，利用该扩展与∞-operads局部化的关系，推广了Mazel-Gee的定理。

Result: 获得了证明operadic局部化结果的高效工具，推广了Willwacher关于循环operads和operadic模的结果，并用离散几何描述了球面上的局部常值分解代数。

Conclusion: 该工作为operadic局部化提供了统一且有效的工具，并产生了多个新的应用结果。

Abstract: We extend the dendroidal Rezk nerve to the setting of relative $\infty$-operads. Our main theorem relates it to localization of $\infty$-operads, generalizing a theorem of Mazel-Gee. By exploiting the relation, we obtain a surprisingly effective tool to prove localization results in operadic contexts. As applications, we obtain a number of new results on operadic localizations, including a generalization of Willwacher's recent result on cyclic operads and operadic modules, and a description of locally constant factorization algebras on spheres in terms of discrete geometry.

</details>


### [90] [On the metalinear algebraic cobordism spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12001)
*Ahina Nandy,Egor Zolotarev*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了金属线性代数配边谱 MML（也称为 MSL^c），建立了它与 MSL 之间的插值关系，证明了 MSL→MML 具有收缩映射，并给出了 MML ≅ MSL ⊕ Σ^{2,1}MGL 的等价分解。在此基础上确定了 MML 的若干不变量，包括 Milnor-Witt 茎、几何对偶以及切片，并描述了 2-可逆 MML 模范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由定向向量丛结构群构造的金属线性代数配边谱 MML，建立其与 MSL 之间的关系，并系统计算其不变量。

Method: 通过建立 MSL 与 MML 之间的插值关系，证明存在收缩映射；在 MSL-模范畴中参数化所有收缩映射，并固定其一得到 MML 的分解等价；利用该分解结合有效代数 K-理论与 Hermitian K-理论谱确定 Milnor-Witt 茎，利用 Stong 的复自旋配边环计算几何对偶，并通过切片描述 2-可逆模范畴。

Result: 得到 MML ≅ MSL ⊕ Σ^{2,1}MGL 的等价分解；确定了 MML 的前几个 Milnor-Witt 茎；计算了几何对偶；给出了切片及其对 2-可逆 MML 模范畴的描述。

Conclusion: MML 谱具有丰富的结构，可分解为 MSL 与 MGL 的平移直和，其不变量可通过已知的代数 K-理论、Hermitian K-理论谱及 Stong 环加以描述。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the metalinear algebraic cobordism spectrum $\mathrm{MML}$ (also sometimes denoted $\mathrm{MSL}^c$), which is built from the structure groups of oriented vector bundles. We establish an interpolation between $\mathrm{MSL}$ and $\mathrm{MML}$ and deduce that the canonical morphism $\mathrm{MSL}\to \mathrm{MML}$ admits a retraction. We parametrize all such retractions in the category of $\mathrm{MSL}$-modules and, after fixing one of them, obtain an equivalence $\mathrm{MML}\cong\mathrm{MSL}\oplus Σ^{2,1}\mathrm{MGL}$. As an application of these results, we determine various invariants of the metalinear algebraic cobordism spectrum over a field (after inverting the exponential characteristic). More precisely, we determine the first few Milnor-Witt stems of $\mathrm{MML}$ in terms of the very effective algebraic and hermitian K-theory spectra, and the geometric diagonal of $\mathrm{MML}$ in terms of Stong's complex-spin cobordism ring. We also compute the slices and use them to describe the category of 2-inverted modules over the $\mathbb{E}_\infty$-ring spectrum $\mathrm{MML}$.

</details>


### [91] [Relations in the 24-th homotopy groups of spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12046)
*Toshiyuki Miyauchi,Juno Mukai*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Toda括号⟨ν̄, σ, ν̄⟩和⟨ν, η, σ̄⟩非平凡，解决了Mahowald猜想，并确定了π^6_30中的ν̄₆ω₁₄与π^7_31中的ν̄₇ω₁₅之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明Mahowald关于Toda括号非平凡性的猜想，并确定特定同伦群中元素的关系。

Method: 通过建立Toda括号与J-同态以及广义P-同态之间的联系，利用这些关系推导所需结论。

Result: 成功证明了Toda括号⟨ν̄, σ, ν̄⟩和⟨ν, η, σ̄⟩非平凡，并给出了π^6_30和π^7_31中相关元素的关系。

Conclusion: 本文给出了Mahowald猜想的肯定回答，并为相关同伦群的计算提供了新的关系工具。

Abstract: The main purpose of this note is to give a proof of the fact that the Toda brackets \ $\langle\barν,σ,\barν\rangle$ and $\langleν,η, \barσ\rangle$ are not trivial. This is an affirmative answer of M.~Mahowald's Conjecture (J. Mukai, Determination of the $P$-image by Toda brackets, Geometry and Topology Monographs \textbf{13}(2008), 355--383). The second purpose is to determine the relations including $\barν_6ω_{14}$ in $π^6_{30}$ and $\barν_7ω_{15}$ in $π^7_{31}$. To this end, we provide relations between the Toda bracket and the $J$-homomorphism, and between the Toda bracket and the generalized $P$-homomorphism.

</details>


### [92] [Stable homology of complex braid groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12206)
*Andrea Bianchi,Filippo Callegaro,Luigi Caputi,Paolo Salvatore*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文计算了固定e≥2、n递增时B(e,e,n)和B(2e,e,n)两类复辫群的稳定同调，从而给出了所有无限族复辫群的稳定同调。通过显式计算其稳定分类空间的Quillen化实现，特别地，证明了Fuchs在70年代声称的D型Artin群稳定同调的等同性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复辫群是重要的代数对象，其稳定同调是代数拓扑与表示论中的核心问题。本文旨在补全所有无限族复辫群稳定同调的计算，并解决Fuchs在1970年代提出的关于D型Artin群稳定同调等同性的未证明断言。

Method: 通过显式计算复辫群稳定分类空间的Quillen化（Quillenization）这一同伦论技术，对B(e,e,n)和B(2e,e,n)两类群进行系统计算。

Result: 成功计算了B(e,e,n)和B(2e,e,n)的稳定同调，从而完整给出了所有无限族复辫群的稳定同调。同时给出了Fuchs关于D型Artin群稳定同调等同性断言的严格证明。

Conclusion: 复辫群所有无限族的稳定同调已被完全确定。Quillen化方法对这类问题有效，并澄清了Artin群稳定同调中长期悬而未决的问题。

Abstract: We compute the stable homology of complex braid groups of types $B(e,e,n)$ and $B(2e,e,n)$ for fixed $e\ge2$ and increasing $n$. This accounts for the stable homology of all infinite families of complex braid groups. We achieve this by explicitly computing a quillenization of their stable classifying spaces. In particular, we provide a proof of an identification of the stable homology of Artin groups of type $D$ claimed by Fuchs in the '70s.

</details>

### [93] [The Statistical Compass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11282)
*Eliuvish Han Cui*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本书将概率论和随机过程作为统计学的一种“翻译语言”，系统阐述如何从实验设计、数据对象到统计推断、稳定性评估和实际应用建立统一的理论框架。内容涵盖随机化、概率测度、核方法、似然、弱收敛、经验场、函数型数据、M-和Z-估计、检验、局部逼近、事件时间过程及预测，并通过历史和生物医学实例将抽象概念与记录、机制和决策相联系，旨在为经典概率、现代数据结构和统计实践提供共同语法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统计学提供一种统一的概率与随机过程语言，将经典概率理论、现代数据结构（如函数型数据、经验场）和统计实践（如M/Z估计、检验、预测）整合为可相互翻译的通用语法体系，解决抽象理论与实际应用之间的脱节问题。

Method: 采用从动机示例和随机化出发，逐步引入概率测度、核、似然、数据对象、弱收敛、经验场、函数型数据、M-和Z-估计、检验、局部逼近、事件时间过程及预测等核心概念，并通过历史和生物医学案例将抽象对象与具体记录、机制和决策相绑定的教学方法。

Result: 形成了涵盖经典概率、现代数据结构和统计实践的完整理论框架，为读者提供了一套可互译的通用“语法”，使抽象的概率-随机过程概念能够直接服务于统计推断、稳定性分析和实际决策。

Conclusion: 概率和随机过程可以作为统计学各分支间的通用翻译语言，通过统一的概念体系和丰富的实例支撑，能够有效连接理论概率、现代数据分析方法和统计实务。

Abstract: This monograph develops probability and stochastic-process ideas as a translation language for statistics: from designed observations and data objects to targets, stability statements, inference, and use. The chapters move from motivating examples and randomization through probability measures, kernels, likelihoods, data objects, weak convergence, empirical fields, functional data, M- and Z-estimation, testing, local approximations, event-time processes, and prediction. Historical and biomedical examples are used to keep abstract objects tied to records, mechanisms, and decisions. The aim is to give readers a common grammar for classical probability, modern data structures, and statistical practice.

</details>


### [94] [ChargeBD: Character-Aware Heterogeneous Agent Reasoning for Guided Engineering in Battery Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12057)
*Rui Huang,Zekun Jiang,Xingyu Niu,Yuqiang Li,Xinying Gu,Tianhang Zhou*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出了ChargeBD框架，通过MBTI启发的角色代理（以DeepSeek-V3-Plus为基础模型）和500题的ESS-LLM BenchMark，实现液流电池研发中多尺度、多目标约束下的引导式工程推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液流电池研发涉及分子设计、电解质优化、电极/膜材料、堆栈运行、系统管理和安全分析等多尺度、多目标约束，而通用LLM在创新探索、规则执行、机理建模和系统权衡等任务上适应性不足。

Method: 从50题RFB任务集扩展为500题ESS-LLM BenchMark；定义16个MBTI启发的角色代理作为结构化认知偏置模板；以DeepSeek-V3-Plus为基础模型，构建角色能力矩阵和认知优势矩阵。

Result: 构建了500题的ESS-LLM BenchMark，建立了16个MBTI人格启发的角色代理，形成了角色能力矩阵和认知优势矩阵。

Conclusion: ChargeBD框架通过异构角色代理的协同推理，有望提升LLM在液流电池研发中跨任务类型的适应性和引导式工程能力。

Abstract: Redox-flow battery (RFB) research spans molecular design, electrolyte optimization, electrode and membrane materials, stack operation, system management, and safety analysis, making it a constrained, multi-scale, and multi-objective energy-storage R&D problem. Although large language models (LLMs) can support scientific knowledge integration and proposal generation, generic LLM reasoning remains insufficiently adaptive across innovation-oriented exploration, rule-based execution, mechanistic modeling, and system-level trade-offs. Here we introduce ChargeBD, a character-aware heterogeneous-agent reasoning framework for guided engineering in battery development. Starting from a 50-question RFB-specific task set, we construct a 500-question ESS-LLM Benchmark and define MBTI-inspired persona agents as structured cognitive-bias templates rather than psychometric instruments or representations of real personalities. DeepSeek-V3-Plus is selected as the shared base model, and 16 MBTI-inspired persona agents are evaluated to construct a persona capability matrix and a cognitive advantage matrix.

</details>


### [95] [Weibull-Stationary Stochastic Differential Equations for Conditional Long-Horizon Wind Power Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12097)
*Luca Di Persio,Mehrdad Ghadiri*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件概率框架的月前风速-功率预测方法，结合威布尔分布、异方差卡尔曼滤波和三种随机微分方程模型，并利用XGBoost功率曲线将风速模拟转换为功率预测，在Kelmarsh风电场数据上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风功率预测方法在月前时间尺度（一个月提前量、十分钟分辨率）上缺乏系统的概率框架，且需要兼顾计算效率与预测精度，以满足下游运行决策（如储能、备用、市场优化）对概率性输入的需求。

Method: 从SCADA风速数据中估计序列依赖的月度威布尔形状和尺度参数，通过Godambe协方差校正，利用异方差卡尔曼滤波在双变量VAR(1)状态空间模型上进行预测。基于MMSE预测的威布尔不变律，构建并比较三种正风速SDE模型：OU-威布尔变换、Fokker-Planck漂移优先扩散、Fokker-Planck扩散优先模型。模拟的风速集合通过校准的XGBoost功率曲线映射到功率域。

Result: 三种SDE模型在概率精度上统计无显著差异（平均CRPS约1.57 m/s），扩散优先模型计算效率最优（速度提升约7倍）。功率域的Wasserstein距离为26.1-27.6 kW（低于额定容量1.4%），月度能量偏差约-7.3%，超越概率误差在0-1500 kW范围内低于1.6个百分点，额定功率附近约2.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 扩散优先SDE模型因计算优势被推荐；该方法为下游运营问题提供了决策相关的概率性输入；完全边缘化卡尔曼预测律下的威布尔参数分布留作自然扩展方向。

Abstract: We present a one-month-ahead conditional probabilistic framework for wind-power forecasting at ten-minute resolution. Monthly Weibull shape and scale parameters are estimated from serially dependent SCADA wind-speed data, corrected through a Godambe covariance, and forecast by a heteroskedastic Kalman filter on a bivariate VAR(1) state-space model. Conditional on the MMSE forecasted Weibull invariant law, we construct and compare three positive wind-speed SDE models: an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck-Weibull transform, a Fokker-Planck drift-first diffusion, and a Fokker-Planck diffusion-first model. The simulated wind-speed ensembles are mapped to power through a calibrated XGBoost power curve. Applied to January 2021 data from a Senvion MM92 turbine at Kelmarsh Wind Farm, the three SDE formulations are statistically indistinguishable in probabilistic accuracy, with mean CRPS values between 1.569 and 1.575 m/s. The diffusion-first model is therefore preferred on computational grounds, reducing runtime by about a factor of seven relative to the OU-Weibull model. In the power domain, the Wasserstein distance between simulated and observed distributions is 26.1-27.6 kW, below $1.4\%$ of rated capacity, while the monthly energy-yield bias is about $-7.3\%$ for the examined month. Exceedance-probability errors remain below 1.6 percentage points over the 0-1500 kW range and about 2.2 percentage points near rated power. These quantities provide decision-relevant probabilistic inputs for downstream operational problems, rather than completed reserve, storage, market, or fatigue-optimization decisions. Full marginalisation over the Kalman predictive law of the Weibull parameters is left as a natural extension.

</details>


### [96] [The data-driven extreme value distribution: non-parametric tail estimation with a derived stability criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12174)
*Michael Sandbichler,Tobias Hell*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出数据驱动极值分布（DDEVD），一种非参数极值估计方法，通过核密度估计聚合所有观测数据并去除参数化尾部假设。理论推导了最优带宽与稳定性条件，在亚小时级高山降水和金相显微图像两个实际场景中，DDEVD以远低于传统GEV拟合的误差率恢复稳定极值估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典极值理论的渐近假设在数据稀疏和非平稳场景下失效，而实际应用中这类场景日益增多，亟需一种不依赖参数化尾部假设、能在有限数据下稳定估计极端事件概率的非参数方法。

Method: DDEVD采用非参数估计，通过核函数对所有观测数据进行元统计聚合以重构基分布，推导了最优带宽的解析形式，并证明了稳定性条件 m < C·n^(1+γ/2)，建立了可靠外推与极值指数γ之间的定量关系。

Result: 在亚小时级高山降水数据中，DDEVD从单个十年数据中恢复稳定的100年重现水平（校准比0.96），偏离全记录参考值超过50%的概率低于1/50，而GEV拟合为1/5。在金相显微图像中，DDEVD在安全相关的晶粒尺寸尾部匹配广义极值拟合，标准对数正态分布在该处高估58%。

Conclusion: DDEVD作为一种无参数尾部假设的非参数极值估计方法，在数据稀疏场景下显著优于传统GEV拟合，为实际应用中面临有限数据和非平稳性的极值风险评估提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Quantifying the likelihood of extreme events underpins risk assessment, yet classical Extreme Value Theory relies on asymptotic assumptions that fail in the data-sparse, non-stationary regimes practitioners increasingly face. We introduce the Data-Driven Extreme Value Distribution (DDEVD), a non-parametric estimator that aggregates all observations metastatistically and reconstructs the base distribution with a kernel, removing parametric tail assumptions. We derive its optimal bandwidth and prove a stability law $m < C\,n^{1+γ/2}$ relating reliable extrapolation to the extreme value index $γ$. In sub-hourly Alpine precipitation, DDEVD recovers stable 100-year return levels from single decades (calibration ratio $0.96$), departing from the full-record reference by over $50\,\%$ in fewer than one window in fifty -- versus one in five for a GEV fit. In metallurgical micrographs, it matches a generalised extreme-value fit on the safety-relevant grain-size tail, where the standard log-normal over-predicts by $58\,\%$ at $1\,\mathrm{cm}^{2}$.

</details>

### [97] [Automated Responsive Thematic Mapping with Layout Guides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12008)
*Arjen Simons,Sarah Schöttler,Wouter Meulemans,Kevin Verbeek,Bettina Speckmann*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个用于计算响应式专题地图的算法框架，通过布局指南（layout guide）这一组合结构，能够平滑地适应不同显示尺寸，并保证稳定性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前响应式专题地图的方法存在局限性：人工制作劳动密集，且常依赖拼接不同视觉编码来适配不同设备类型，缺乏自动化的、平滑适应的解决方案。

Method: 提出一个算法框架，核心是布局指南（编码统计元素的视觉需求及其相对位置关系）和地图排列器（map arranger，根据视觉容器输入返回稳定一致的布局指南）。框架需要三个输入：参考布局、地图元素的全局垂直和水平顺序、以及能从布局指南构建专题地图的映射算法。

Result: 该框架在矩形和Demers两种专题地图类型上进行了验证，能够高效生成平滑适应不同显示尺寸的响应式地图。

Conclusion: 提出的算法框架首次实现了响应式专题地图的自动化和平滑适应，布局指南结构有效平衡了统计元素可读性与制图上下文，且保证布局随容器尺寸变化的稳定性和一致性。

Abstract: Thematic maps visually communicate statistical information about spatial units such as countries or states. They must balance the individual readability of those map elements that carry the statistical information and the overall cartographic context. Nowadays, most maps are not static images, but must flexibly respond to a range of device types and display sizes. Current approaches to responsive thematic mapping are limited: they are labor-intensive for practitioners and often rely on combining disjointed visual encodings to cover different device types. In this paper we introduce the first algorithmic framework to efficiently compute responsive thematic maps that smoothly adapt to different display sizes. A key component of our framework is the layout guide: a combinatorial structure which encodes the two essential aspects of a thematic map. The first aspect are the visual requirements of each statistical map element (at least their desired width and height), the second aspect is the cartographic context in the form of relative positions of map elements. Our main algorithmic contribution is the map arranger which takes a visual container as input and returns a suitable layout guide. The map arranger does so in a stable and consistent manner: if the container changes only a little, then so does the layout guide, and the same input container always results in the same layout guide. To use our framework, one needs three ingredients: $(1)$ a reference layout, which corresponds to the ``ideal'' thematic map, $(2)$ a total vertical and horizontal order for all map elements (the desired layouts for containers with extreme aspect ratios), and $(3)$ a thematic mapping algorithm that can construct a thematic map from a layout guide. We demonstrate our framework on two types of thematic maps, namely rectangular and Demers cartograms.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [A Scalable Approach for Transient Thermal Modeling of Automotive Power Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11226)
*Neelakantan Padmanabhan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于集总参数线性叠加（LPLSP）的瞬态热建模方法，用于汽车电力电子系统的快速热仿真。以六MOSFET逆变器模块为案例，与CFD仿真和LTI降阶模型对比，LPLSP模型误差小于5%，适用于早期设计迭代和长时间任务剖面仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车电力电子系统（如逆变器模块）的热管理对可靠性和性能至关重要，但传统CFD仿真计算成本高，难以满足早期设计阶段快速迭代和长时任务剖面仿真的需求。

Method: 采用Lumped Parameter Linear Superposition (LPLSP)方法，即集总参数建模与线性叠加原理相结合的混合方法。在Ansys Icepak中进行CFD仿真（含自然对流、定速强制对流、变速强制对流），并将LPLSP模型结果与CFD及LTI降阶模型进行对比验证。

Result: LPLSP模型能够在广泛工况下准确预测瞬态热行为，误差小于5%。相比CFD仿真，该方法大幅降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: LPLSP方法能够快速评估具有快速功率耗散瞬变和环境条件变化的电力电子系统热性能，特别适合早期设计迭代和长时间任务剖面仿真，有助于缩短汽车电力电子设计开发周期。

Abstract: Efficient thermal management is critical for the reliability and performance of power electronics systems in automotive applications. This work presents a computationally efficient modeling approach for transient thermal simulation of power electronic systems, with a focus on inverter modules using multiple MOSFETs mounted on a printed circuit board assembly (PCBA). A case study of an inverter module comprising six MOSFETs arranged as high-side and low-side pairs for a three phases system mounted on a PCBA, attached to a heat sink is considered. Computational fluid dynamic (CFD) simulations in Ansys Icepak are performed considering different heat transfer mechanisms, including natural convection, forced convection at constant velocity, and forced convection with varying flow velocity. A transient thermal model is developed using the Lumped Parameter Linear Superposition (LPLSP) method, a hybrid approach that combines lumped parameter modeling with the principle of linear superposition to capture transient thermal behavior efficiently. Temperatures of the components from the simulations are compared with temperatures from the LPLSP model and temperatures from a Linear Time Invariant (LTI) based reduced order model (ROM) developed for this system. It is observed that the LPLSP model is able to model a wide range of use cases very accurately with error of less than 5 %. This method enables rapid thermal performance evaluation of power electronics systems that have very fast transients in component level power dissipation and variations in ambient conditions, making it particularly well-suited for early-stage design iterations and long-duration mission profile simulations. The approach offers a practical path to reducing development cycles for automotive power electronics design.

</details>


### [99] [Preconditioning for near-contacts in large 2D Stokes flows: a locally compressed method of fundamental solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11273)
*Anna Broms,Anna-Karin Tornberg,Alex H. Barnett*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对稠密刚性颗粒粘性流体动力学模拟中的两大难点（迭代求解收敛慢及润滑驱动流离散未知量过多），提出了两体预处理策略并结合基本解法实现。通过为每个近接触粒子对预计算局部精细网格解，在迭代过程中对粗网格表示进行修正，并压缩为等效粗源。数值实验表明，该方法在密集悬浮体系中GMRES迭代快速收敛，例如在面积分数0.65、10000个单分散圆盘、最小间距10⁻³的随机密堆积问题中，仅用47次迭代、每体72个向量未知量即可达到五位数精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密刚性颗粒在粘性流体中的水动力学模拟面临两个关键困难：(i) 粒子间隙变小时迭代求解离散线性系统的收敛速率急剧下降；(ii) 精确离散润滑驱动流需要大量未知数。现有方法难以同时有效解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出两体预处理策略，结合基本解法实现。对每个近接触粒子对，通过求解局部边界值问题（精细网格）预计算难解析的相互作用表示。在迭代求解中，该流场修正粗网格表示所有粒子得到的解。局部精细网格修正可被压缩，使得除粒子对自身外所有粒子受到等效粗源的影响。

Result: 数值实验展示了在具有挑战性的多粒子场景中GMRES快速收敛。例如，面积分数φ=0.65、10000个单分散圆盘、最小分离10⁻³的随机密堆积，仅用47次GMRES迭代，每体72个向量未知量即可达到五位精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的两体预处理策略能有效解决稠密颗粒流体动力学模拟的两大困难，在极端密堆积条件下仍保持高效收敛和低计算成本。

Abstract: We tackle two key difficulties in the simulation of the viscous hydrodynamics of a large dense collection of rigid particles: (i) the poor convergence rate of an iterative solution of the discretized linear system as particle gaps shrink, and (ii) the large number of unknowns needed to accurately discretize the resulting lubrication-driven flows. Our focus is the 2D Stokes resistance and mobility boundary value problems for nearly-touching disks. To address both challenges, we introduce a general two-body preconditioning strategy, and implement it with the method of fundamental solutions. For each close particle pair, the hard-to-resolve interaction is represented in a basis precomputed by solving a local boundary value problem on a fine grid. In an iterative solve, the resulting flow field corrects that obtained from a coarse representation of all particles. The local fine-grid correction can even be compressed so that all particles except the pair itself are affected by an equivalent set of coarse sources. Numerical experiments demonstrate rapid GMRES convergence in challenging multi-particle settings, with iteration counts remaining low even in densely packed suspensions. For example, the mobility problem is solved for a random close packing with area fraction $φ= 0.65$, $P = 10000$ monodisperse disks, and minimum separation $10^{-3}$, in just 47 GMRES iterations, achieving five digits of accuracy with 72 vector unknowns per body.

</details>

Method: 定义了两类与Gauss-Legendre多项式相关联的对偶多项式族，通过理论推导建立其数学性质，并展示如何利用这些结果进行Gauss-Legendre多项式的表示、Lagrange基的对偶基构造以及特定逼近问题的求解。

Result: 成功定义了两类对偶多项式族，并证明了它们可用于：(1) 推导Gauss-Legendre多项式的多种表示形式；(2) 构造Lagrange基函数的对偶基；(3) 解决CAGD中的某些逼近问题。

Conclusion: 本文建立的对偶多项式理论为Gauss-Legendre多项式在计算机图形学和CAGD中的应用提供了新的数学工具，特别是在基函数构造和逼近问题求解方面具有实用价值。

Abstract: We define and investigate two families of dual polynomials associated with the Gauss--Legendre polynomials, which have recently found interesting applications in computer graphics. Using the presented results, one can derive representations of the Gauss--Legendre polynomials, construct the dual bases for Lagrange bases and solve certain approximation problems arising, for example, in CAGD.

### [101] [A Two-Sided Sketching Algorithm for Low-rank Tensor Train Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11603)
*Gaohang Yu,Yihao Pan,Ailun Jian,Xiaohao Cai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于一次性素描算法和子空间迭代的随机化张量列车分解方法，有效解决了大规模SVD计算瓶颈，并提供了完整的误差界和鲁棒性分析，实验验证了其高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量列车（TT）分解在计算过程中常受大规模矩阵奇异值分解（SVD）的阻碍，计算效率低下，需要更高效的分解方法。

Method: 提出了一种随机化算法，结合一次性素描算法（one-pass sketching algorithm）和子空间迭代（subspace iteration），用于获取张量的TT近似。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上的数值实验验证了所提算法的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的随机化素描算法能够高效地完成张量列车分解，并提供严格的误差界和鲁棒性保证。

Abstract: Tensor train (TT) decomposition is a powerful method to acquire low-rank tensors. However, the computational process is frequently obstructed by the large-scale matrix singular value decomposition (SVD). The sketching algorithm serves as an efficient data compression technique that can quickly derive low-rank matrix approximations. In this paper, we propose a randomized algorithm to obtain the TT approximation of tensors using a one-pass sketching algorithm and subspace iteration, and offer thorough error-bound and robustness analysis. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [102] [High-order multi-structures-preserving exponential integrators for the derivative nonlinear Schrödinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11734)
*Liping Wu,Li Yang,Chaolong Jiang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新型高阶保质量、保能量、保动量的指数积分器，用于求解导数非线性薛定谔方程。该方法基于指数辅助变量方法重构原系统，采用Fourier伪谱法进行空间离散，结合高阶预测-校正Lawson Runge-Kutta方法进行时间离散，在离散层面同时保持质量、能量和动量守恒。数值实验验证了方法的精度和守恒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值方法难以同时在离散层面保持导数非线性薛定谔方程的质量、能量和动量守恒性质，且兼顾高阶时间精度与计算效率。

Method: (1) 利用指数辅助变量方法(ESV)将原系统重构为指数辅助变量系统；(2) 空间上采用标准Fourier伪谱法离散；(3) 时间上采用高阶预测-校正Lawson Runge-Kutta方法离散。

Result: 所提出的方法在离散层面同时保持质量、能量和动量守恒，具有时间高阶精度和高计算效率，数值实验验证了精度和保能量性质。

Conclusion: 本文成功构造了一类同时保持质量、能量和动量守恒的高阶指数积分器，为导数非线性薛定谔方程的数值求解提供了高效且结构保持的离散方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel class of high-order mass-, energy- and momentum-preserving exponential integrators for solving the derivative nonlinear Schrödinger equation. Firstly, we reformulate the original system into an exponential supplementary variable system based on the idea of the exponential supplementary variable approach, and then the reformulated system is discretized by using the standard Fourier pseudo-spectral method in space and the high-order prediction and correction Lawson Runge-Kutta method in time, respectively. The proposed method is highly efficient, temporally high-order accurate, and simultaneously preserves the mass, energy and momentum in the discrete setting. Finally, numerical experiments validate the accuracy and energy-preserving properties.

</details>


### [103] [Curvature-Induced Force Fields in Hyperelasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11772)
*Victor Dods*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了在二维黎曼流形（如漏斗面或抛物面）中，扁平超弹性体嵌入弯曲曲面时的静力平衡问题。通过引入重力势，数值模拟了物体在弹性恢复力与重力平衡时产生的“悬浮”现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者最初受创建黎曼流形中第一人称计算机可视化启发，发现一般黎曼流形缺乏非平凡等距群，无法使用刚体力学，因此转向可变形体力学。本文旨在研究超弹性体在弯曲曲面上的静平衡及其数值实现。

Method: 将扁平超弹性体B嵌入二维旋转曲面S（高斯曲率K(r)随r增大而减小的非平坦曲面，如漏斗面z=-r^{-1}或抛物面z=½r²）上的区域Ω中。通过定义存储能量密度函数，并添加重力势U(r)=z(r)，采用变分法建立问题，进行数值模拟求解静力平衡。

Result: 当物体具有足够刚度且保持在区域Ω内时，存在平衡构型，其中物体的变形响应力与重力完全抵消，在曲面内产生一种“悬浮”现象。

Conclusion: 本文详细介绍了该问题的数值实现方法，并讨论了数值解及其各种推论，展示了超弹性体在弯曲黎曼流形中的静平衡行为。

Abstract: Originally motivated by creating first-person computer visualizations within Riemannian manifolds -- the author was led to study deformable-body mechanics, as rigid-body mechanics is not available in a generic Riemannian manifold due to its lack of nontrivial isometry group. Hyperelasticity is a particularly nice sub-category of continuum mechanics in which a deformable, elastic body's behavior is determined by a stored energy density function. This allows problems to be posed variationally, and powerful tools brought to bear on studying and solving them.
  This article presents numerical simulations of static solutions to a particular class of problems in hyperelastic mechanics in 2-dimensional Riemannian manifolds in which a flat hyperelastic body $B$ is embedded into a region $Ω$ in a nowhere-flat surface $S$ of revolution $z=z\left(r\right)$ such that $\left|K\left(r\right)\right|$ decreases as $r\to\infty$, where $K$ denotes the Gaussian curvature of $S$. For example, the funnel $z=-r^{-1}$ or the paraboloid $z=\frac{1}{2}r^{2}$. Because $B$ is flat, the body can't achieve a zero-stored-energy configuration, and restorative forces arise in the body to move it toward a region of lower stored energy -- meaning, toward a flatter configuration.
  With the addition of a gravitational potential $U\left(r\right)=z\left(r\right)$ on $S$, forces act on the body to pull it toward $r=0$. If the body has sufficient stiffness and remains within the region $Ω$, then the body has an equilibrium configuration in which the body's deformation-response forces perfectly cancel the gravitational forces. Such a configuration represents a kind of "levitation" phenomenon within this surface.
  The numerical implementation of this problem will be detailed and the resulting numerical solutions and various consequences discussed.

</details>


### [104] [Splitting strategies for the fully-coupled nonlinear thermo-hydro-mechanical problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11801)
*Stefano Bonetti,Michele Botti,Paola F. Antonietti*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对完全耦合的非线性四场热-孔隙弹性模型，提出了一种在任意多边形网格上采用间断伽辽金方法进行空间离散的新型半解耦和全解耦迭代算法。该格式对模型系数的强异质性具有鲁棒性，并在适当条件下证明了两种方案的收敛性，通过广泛数值算例验证了方法的收敛性和鲁棒性，并在地球物理背景下进行了概念验证测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决完全耦合的非线性四场热-孔隙弹性模型的高效求解问题，特别是针对多孔介质中热-力学-孔隙压力多物理场耦合问题的数值模拟挑战。

Method: 采用任意阶加权对称内罚间断伽辽金方法在任意多边形网格上进行空间离散，提出半解耦和全解耦两种迭代求解策略，并在理论上证明两种方案在适当条件下的收敛性。

Result: 数值模拟验证了所提方法对模型系数强异质性的鲁棒性以及收敛性，并通过文献及物理上合理的测试案例完成了地球物理背景下的概念验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的两种解耦迭代算法能够高效求解完全耦合的非线性四场热-孔隙弹性问题，在任意多边形网格上具有良好的鲁棒性和收敛性，适用于地球物理领域的实际应用。

Abstract: We propose novel semi-decoupled and fully-decoupled iterative algorithms for efficiently solving the fully-coupled nonlinear four-field thermo-poroelastic model discretized in space by discontinuous Galerkin method on polytopal grids. We present the model problem, its four-field formulation, and the arbitrary-order weighted symmetric interior penalty scheme exploited for its spatial discretization. Such a scheme is robust with respect to strong heterogeneities in the model coefficients. Then, we present the two solution strategies and prove that under suitable conditions both schemes are convergent. A wide set of numerical simulations is presented to assess the convergence and robustness properties of the proposed method. Moreover, we test the scheme with literature and physically sound test cases for proof-of-concept applications in the geophysical context.

</details>


### [105] [Sparsity-Driven Source Localization in Tomographic Sensing Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11840)
*Marco Mattuschka,Noah An der Lan,Arne Ficks,Max von Danwitz,Alexander Popp*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对双站FPA FTIR高光谱成像系统，提出了一种基于稀疏正则化和水平集方法的数学框架，用于从层析测量数据中反演气态污染物释放源的位置与强度，并预测羽流演化，以支持危险品泄漏的早期预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用双台焦平面阵列傅里叶变换红外光谱仪（FPA FTIR）同时以适当张角运行时，能够实现对不可见气态污染物羽流的层析重建，提升时空精度。然而，从这些层析测量数据中反演释放源的位置和量化排放强度是一个严重病态且欠定的逆问题，现有方法难以有效处理。开发能够从遥测数据重建释放位置并预测未来羽流演化的工具，对于危险物质泄漏场景中的早期预警和态势感知至关重要。

Method: 采用平流-扩散方程描述污染物传输，将源识别问题构建为对应的逆问题。针对问题的严重病态性和欠定性，引入稀疏促进正则化方法并结合高性能优化算法求解。为将层析测量数据融入离散格式，采用阈值浓度的水平集描述，使测量表示与计算网格无关，避免昂贵的网格重划分过程。

Result: 提出了一套完整的数学建模与算法框架，能够从双站FPA FTIR光谱仪的层析测量数据中识别、定位并量化污染物释放源。

Conclusion: 该方法通过稀疏正则化应对逆问题的病态性，利用水平集技术实现测量数据的网格无关表示，为基于遥测数据的危险物质泄漏早期预警和态势感知提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Hyperspectral standoff detection systems such as Focal Plane Array (FPA) Fourier Transform Infrared (FTIR) spectrometers provide high spatial resolution in detecting airborne chemical contaminants that are invisible to the human eye but potentially hazardous. When two such systems are operated simultaneously with a suitable opening angle, they enable tomographic reconstruction of contaminant plumes with improved spatial and temporal accuracy. This work presents a mathematical model of these measurement capabilities and an algorithm to identify, localize, and quantify contaminant release sources. The objective is to develop a a tool that reconstructs release locations and predict the future plume evolution from standoff measurement data, thereby supporting early warning and situational awareness in hazardous material release scenarios. The transport of contaminants is modeled by an advection-diffusion equation, and the corresponding inverse problem for source identification is formulated accordingly. Owing to the severe ill-posedness and underdetermination of the problem, a sparsity-promoting regularization approach is employed together with a high-performance optimization algorithm. To incorporate the tomographic measurement data into the discrete formulation, a level-set description of a threshold concentration is used, allowing the measurements to be represented independently of the computational mesh and avoiding costly remeshing procedures.

</details>


### [106] [Polytopal Discontinuous Galerkin Discretizations of Coupled Non-Newtonian Stokes-Darcy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11935)
*Paola F. Antonietti,Michele Botti,Nicola Parolini,Valentina Pederzoli,Marco Verani*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对非牛顿流体-Stokes-Darcy耦合系统，提出并分析了一种多面体间断伽辽金方法，该方法具有几何灵活性和任意阶精度，完成了完整先验分析，并在广义inf-sup理论框架下建立了适定性、稳定性和误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非牛顿自由流与多孔介质中非牛顿流耦合系统的数值逼近问题，传统的有限元方法在复杂几何构型下存在局限性，需要一种几何灵活且高精度的数值格式。

Method: 提出了多面体间断伽辽金（polytopal DG）离散格式，分别对自由流区域采用剪切依赖的非牛顿黏度模型、对多孔介质区域采用速度依赖的非牛顿黏度模型，在广义inf-sup理论框架下进行分析。

Result: 建立了方法的适定性、稳定性和误差界，并通过数值结果验证了误差估计的正确性。

Conclusion: 所提出的多面体DG方法能灵活处理复杂几何构型，具备任意阶精度，且理论上保证了耦合非牛顿Stokes-Darcy系统的数值逼近的可靠性和准确性。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a polytopal discontinuous Galerkin method for the numerical approximation of a coupled non-Newtonian Stokes-Darcy system modeling the interaction between a non-Newtonian free-flow fluid and a non-Newtonian flow through a porous medium. Due to its geometric flexibility and arbitrary-order accuracy, the proposed discretization scheme is well-suited to configurations with complex geometries. We provide a complete a-priori analysis that considers shear-dependent and velocity-dependent non-Newtonian viscosity models for the free-flow and porous media regions, respectively. The well-posedness, stability, and error bounds of the method are established in the framework of generalized inf-sup theory. Error estimates are confirmed by numerical results.

</details>


### [107] [Analysis of Power Iteration Algorithm with Partially Observed Matrix-vector Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11956)
*Soumyadip Ghosh,Lior Horesh,Vassilis Kalantzis,Yingdong Lu,Tomasz Nowicki,Shashanka Ubaru*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对云架构中矩阵-向量乘积计算受限（部分观测、落后节点拖累）的场景，提出了两种在幂迭代过程中处理缺失条目的算法，通过将缺失值置零或使用历史值进行替换，使期望近似值收敛到真实主特征向量，并通过数值实验验证了其性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在云架构的控制器-工作者模型中，矩阵-向量乘积分布在远程服务器上执行，慢速工作者（straggling）导致延迟。作者希望在不等待所有工作者返回结果的情况下继续进行幂迭代，同时保证收敛到正确的主特征向量。

Method: 提出两种算法：1) 基于概率切换的方法，在将缺失条目置零与使用历史记录值之间随机切换；2) 基于平均化的方法，通过对忽略迭代矩阵某些列后生成的局部幂迭代近似进行平均来处理缺失条目。

Result: 两种算法的期望近似值都收敛到真实主特征向量；数值实验表明所提方法优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 在矩阵-向量乘积部分观测的约束下，通过合理的缺失值处理策略（置零/历史值/平均化），幂迭代算法仍能收敛到正确的主特征向量，且具有实际性能优势。

Abstract: We consider the problem of computing the dominant eigenvector of a symmetric matrix via the power iteration algorithm subject to constraints in the computation of matrix-vector pr ucts. In particular, we focus on scenarios where the entries of matrix-vector products with the input matrix are only partially observed. Such constraints frequently arise on cloud architectures implemented via the controller-worker model where the matrix-vector products are distributed across workers on remote servers. Instead of a prolonged delay incurred by waiting for the slowest workers to return their output to the controller, a phenomenon known as straggling, a set of pre-determined values can replace the values of the delayed workers and allow the power iteration to proceed to the next iteration. In this paper, we develop two algorithms whose expected approximation converges to the true dominant eigenvector. The first algorithm relies on a probabilistic switch between two different approaches to set the omitted entries: either set them to zero or to their previous recorded value. The second algorithm relies on averaging previously generated partial power iteration approximations obtained by ignoring a set of columns of the iteration matrix. several theoretical details are discussed while numerical experiments verify the effectiveness of the two proposed schemes and demonstrate their comparative performance advantage over current state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [108] [Fully decoupled, linear and structure-preserving block-centered finite difference methods for the Keller-Segel chemotaxis system on staggered non-uniform grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12095)
*Jie Xu,Hongfei Fu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对经典Keller-Segel趋化系统，提出了两种完全解耦、线性且保结构的交错非均匀网格块中心有限差分格式，分别具有一阶和二阶时间精度，并证明了格式在离散层面保持正性、质量守恒和能量耗散等物理性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构造能够同时满足完全解耦、线性、保结构（正性、质量守恒、能量耗散）的高精度数值格式，以更准确高效地模拟趋化动力学，特别是快速爆破现象。

Method: 在交错非均匀空间网格上提出两种块中心有限差分方案：一种时间一阶精度（无条件保正、保质量守恒、保能量耗散），另一种时间二阶精度（在充分时间步条件下保正），两种格式均空间二阶精度且完全解耦、线性。

Result: 一阶格式无条件保持正性、质量守恒和能量耗散；二阶格式在充分（非必要）时间步条件下保持正性。数值实验验证了理论结果及格式的准确性和可靠性。

Conclusion: 所提出的两种格式在非均匀网格上实现了高精度、完全解耦、线性且保结构的数值模拟，特别适用于趋化系统中快速爆破现象的仿真。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose two fully decoupled, linear and structure-preserving block-centered finite difference schemes for the classical Keller-Segel chemotaxis system on staggered non-uniform spatial grids. Both novel schemes are second-order accurate in space; one is first-order accurate in time, while the other achieves second-order temporal accuracy. Moreover, we show that the schemes preserve several inherent physical laws at the discrete level: (i) the positivity of both the cell density and the chemoattractant concentration; (ii) the conservation of total cell mass; and (iii) a discrete energy dissipation property for the first-order scheme. In particular, the temporally first-order scheme unconditionally preserves positivity, mass conservation, and energy dissipation, whereas the second-order scheme ensures positivity under a sufficient (but not necessary) time-step condition. The proposed methods yield more accurate and efficient simulations of chemotactic dynamics, especially in the presence of rapid blow-up phenomena, on specified non-uniform spatial grids. Numerical experiments are conducted to validate the theoretical findings and to illustrate the accuracy and reliability of the proposed schemes.

</details>


### [109] [A Decoupled Low-Order Conforming Mixed Finite Element Method for a Three-Dimensional Fourth-Order Singularly Perturbed Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12176)
*Yuanchun Tang,Baiju Zhang,Zhimin Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对三维四阶椭圆奇异摄动问题，提出了一种解耦的低阶协调有限元方法。通过广义亥姆霍兹分解将问题简化为两个二阶椭圆问题和一个带无旋约束的广义奇异摄动Stokes型方程组，分别采用标准线性元和MINI元离散，并通过添加含拉格朗日乘子的L^2项实现了对摄动参数的鲁棒性，建立了关于摄动参数一致的O(h^{1/2})误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对三维四阶椭圆奇异摄动问题，现有数值方法在处理摄动参数的鲁棒性和计算效率方面存在不足，需要一种解耦且低阶协调的有限元方法。

Method: 利用广义亥姆霍兹分解将原四阶问题解耦为两个二阶椭圆问题和一个带无旋约束的广义奇异摄动Stokes型方程组；二阶问题用标准线性有限元离散；Stokes型系统采用MINI元，并添加含拉格朗日乘子的L^2项以确保对摄动参数的鲁棒性。

Result: 建立了关于摄动参数一致的O(h^{1/2})误差估计，数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的解耦低阶协调有限元方法能有效求解三维四阶椭圆奇异摄动问题，且对摄动参数具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper develops a decoupled low-order conforming finite element method for a fourth-order elliptic singular perturbation problem in three dimensions. By means of a generalized Helmholtz decomposition, the problem is reduced to two second-order elliptic problems and a system of generalized singularly perturbed Stokes-type equations subject to a curl-free constraint. The former are discretized by standard linear finite elements. For the latter, we employ the MINI element and show that, after adding an $L^2$ term involving a Lagrange multiplier, the resulting discretization becomes robust with respect to the perturbation parameter. We further establish an error estimate of order $h^{1/2}$ uniform with respect to the perturbation parameter. Numerical experiments are included to support the theory.

</details>


### [110] [Symmetric structure-preserving discretization of N-phase incompressible fluid mixtures with arbitrary density ratios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12256)
*M. F. P. ten Eikelder,A. Brunk*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对任意密度比下N相不可压缩Navier-Stokes-Cahn-Hilliard混合物模型，提出了一种对称的全离散数值方法，该方法能同时保持相体积守恒、相质量守恒、总体积守恒、总质量守恒以及离散能量耗散律，并数值验证了其结构保持特性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构保持方法主要针对两相流或需区分参考相的公式，缺乏真正对称的N相离散格式；而多相流计算需要数值方法保持连续模型的平衡结构、饱和度约束、能量耗散以及各相对称性，尤其在任意密度比情况下更困难。

Method: 提出一种对称的全离散方法，用于任意密度比下N相不可压缩Navier-Stokes-Cahn-Hilliard混合物模型，通过精心设计的离散格式确保所有解满足精确的相体积守恒、相质量守恒、总体积守恒、总质量守恒以及离散能量耗散律，并在初始数据满足体积饱和度约束时保持该约束在每个时间步成立。

Result: 数值验证表明该方法的解满足所有结构保持性质（相体积守恒、相质量守恒、总体积守恒、总质量守恒、离散能量耗散律），并在代表性多相流问题中展现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方案为具有复杂界面动力学和任意密度对比的不可压缩N相混合物流动提供了一个计算框架。

Abstract: Diffuse-interface models are a widely used framework for interfacial dynamics in complex fluids, in which interfaces are represented through smooth transition layers and capillary effects are encoded by a free-energy functional. For incompressible mixtures with more than two phases, however, robust computation is substantially more difficult because the numerical method should preserve the balance structure of the continuum model, maintain the saturation constraint, dissipate energy, and treat all phases symmetrically even when density ratios are arbitrary. Existing structure-preserving methods are largely developed for binary flows or for formulations that distinguish a reference phase, so a genuinely symmetric N-phase discretization remains lacking. The practical problem is therefore to construct a fully-discrete method for N-phase incompressible Navier--Stokes--Cahn--Hilliard mixture models that retains the key thermodynamic and conservation properties of the continuum equations for arbitrary density ratios.
  Here we propose a symmetric fully-discrete method for the N-phase incompressible Navier--Stokes--Cahn--Hilliard mixture model with arbitrary density ratios. The method yields a fully-discrete problem in which every solution satisfies exact phase volume conservation, phase mass conservation, total volume conservation, total mass conservation, and a discrete energy-dissipation law. In addition, if the volume-saturation constraint holds for the initial data, then it is preserved at every time step. We numerically verify these structure-preserving properties and demonstrate the robustness of the method in representative multiphase flow problems. The resulting scheme provides a computational framework for incompressible N-phase mixture flows with complex interfacial dynamics and arbitrary density contrasts.

</details>


### [111] [An improvement on B-spline basis condition number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12270)
*Yimin Zhong*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文改进了单变量B样条基条件数的上界，从$k 2^k$降至$O(\sqrt{k}\log k\,2^k)$，适用于所有$k\ge 2$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低B样条基条件数的已知上界，以更精确地刻画其数值稳定性。

Method: 通过理论分析，推导出更紧的上界$O(\sqrt{k}\log k\,2^k)$，替代原有的$k 2^k$。

Result: 条件数上界从$k 2^k$改进到$O(\sqrt{k}\log k\,2^k)$。

Conclusion: 新的上界更紧，对B样条基的数值性质提供了更好的理论保证。

Abstract: In this short note, we improve the upper bound on the condition number of the univariate B-spline basis of order $k$ from $k 2^k$ to $O(\sqrt{k}\log k\,2^k)$ for all $k\ge 2$.

</details>


### [112] [Adjoint Method versus Physics-Informed Neural Networks in PDE-Constrained Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12337)
*Zhen Zhang,Alessandro Alla,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对伴随优化和物理信息神经网络(PINNs)在PDE约束逆问题中的性能进行了公平比较。在统一域、控制方程、观测模型、正则化、优化器、参数化和精度设置下，通过四个基准测试（非稳态Burgers、含噪Darcy渗透率反演、3D Allen-Cahn反应识别、非稳态Navier-Stokes粘度识别）发现：未知量的表示形式在很大程度上决定优选方法——网格场更适合离散伴随，神经表示对PINNs更自然；时间依赖问题中PINNs能以更低成本提供满意的重构，而PINN预热启动的伴随策略能以大幅降低的成本恢复伴随级的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伴随优化和PINNs在PDE逆问题中的性能难以公正评估，因为现有比较往往采用不同的公式、参数化、优化器和正则化选择。需要建立一个统一的公平比较框架来厘清两种方法的相对优劣。

Method: 从共同的抽象公式出发，在完全相同的域、控制方程、观测模型和正则化项上实例化两种方法，并匹配优化器、未知参数化和算术精度。采用四个代表性基准问题（非稳态Burgers、含噪Darcy、3D Allen-Cahn、非稳态Navier-Stokes）进行比较。

Result: 1) 网格场表示下离散伴随方法更优；2) 神经表示是PINNs的原生形式，对闭合和本构建模更相关；3) 时间依赖问题中，伴随反演受轨迹存储和微分的限制，PINNs能以更低成本提供满意重构；4) PINN预热启动的伴随策略能以大幅降低的成本恢复伴随级精度。

Conclusion: 未知量的表示形式是决定方法选择的关键因素。PINN预热启动的伴随策略能够在降低计算成本的同时保持伴随级精度，为实际问题提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Inverse problems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) are central to computational mechanics and are commonly solved by adjoint-based optimization, while physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a flexible alternative. Their relative performance remains difficult to assess because the two approaches are often compared under different formulations, parameterizations, optimizers, and regularization choices. We present a fair comparison of adjoint optimization and PINNs for PDE-constrained inverse problems. From a common abstract formulation, we instantiate both methods on identical domains, governing equations, observation models, and regularization terms, while matching the optimizer, unknown parameterization, and arithmetic precision wherever applicable. The benchmarks include unsteady Burgers, noisy Darcy permeability inversion, three-dimensional Allen--Cahn reaction identification, and unsteady Navier--Stokes viscosity identification. The results show that the representation of the unknown largely determines the preferred method: grid-based fields favor the discrete adjoint, whereas neural representations are native to PINNs and relevant for closure and constitutive modeling. For time-dependent problems, adjoint inversion can be dominated by trajectory storage and differentiation, while PINNs provide satisfactory reconstructions at lower cost. A PINN-warm-started adjoint strategy then recovers adjoint-level accuracy at substantially reduced cost.

</details>


### [113] [MATLAB-Based Layerwise Self-Adaptive Physics-Informed Neural Network in Applications to Multidimensional Coupled Burgers' Equations with High Reynolds Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12348)
*Harish P. Bhatt,Xi Chen,Jingsai Liang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的物理信息神经网络（PINN），通过层级自适应权重策略和双阶段优化方法，高效模拟高雷诺数下多维耦合Burgers方程的时空解，能够捕捉尖锐激波前沿，相比标准PINN具有更高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高雷诺数下多维耦合Burgers方程的解随时间演化会产生尖锐激波前沿，传统基于网格的数值方法（如有限差分、有限元）在求解陡峭梯度时存在稳定性差和强网格依赖性问题，亟需一种更鲁棒的数值模拟方法。

Method: 提出改进PINN框架：（1）采用层级自适应权重策略，在训练过程中动态调整物理残差、初始条件和边界条件的惩罚权重；（2）采用双阶段优化策略，实现更稳定的收敛。

Result: 数值实验表明，相比于标准PINN（带或不带L-BFGS优化），所提框架在相对L2误差范数上取得了更高的精度，并能有效捕捉解随时间的尖锐激波前沿演化。

Conclusion: 改进的PINN框架通过层级自适应权重和双阶段优化，有效解决了高雷诺数下尖锐激波模拟的难题，在精度和稳定性上优于标准PINN，为非线性偏微分方程的数值模拟提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper presents an improved physics-informed neural network for simulating the spatio-temporal solution profile of the multidimensional coupled Burgers' equations with high Reynolds numbers. As time evolves, the sharp shock fronts emerge in the solution, creating significant computational challenges for the conventional mesh-based numerical methods. In particular, numerical methods such as finite differences and finite elements suffer from poor stability and strong mesh dependency when resolving the steep solution gradients. To address these challenges, the proposed framework employs a layerwise self-adaptive weighting strategy that dynamically adjusts the penalty weights for the physics residual, initial conditions, and boundary conditions throughout training. Moreover, the framework uses a dual-phase optimization strategy to achieve more stable convergence.
  To check the effectiveness and accuracy of the proposed framework, a set of numerical experiments is conducted to compare it with the standard Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) with and without Limited-memory Broyden-Fletcher-Goldfarb-Shanno (L-BFGS) optimization. Numerical results exhibit that the proposed framework achieves higher accuracy in terms of relative $L_2-$ error norm than the standard PINN and is able to capture the development of sharp shock fronts as time evolves in the solution.

</details>

### [114] [A High-Precision Clock Synchronization System for the CEPC Accelerator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11590)
*Jun Hu,Xin Zhou,Xiaoshan Jiang,Dapeng Jin*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种增强型白兔（WR）时钟同步系统，通过DSPLL替代DAC+VCXO、GTX相位对齐、级联全局控制架构及TD3强化学习PID自整定等技术，实现了3.38 ps（点对点1米光纤）和6.66 ps（12级级联末端）的同步精度，满足了CEPC 30 ps的预算要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CEPC百公里地下隧道需将参考时钟分发至192个控制节点，要求同步精度30 ps（标准差），现有标准WR方案因模拟驱动链（DAC+VCXO）和重启时序不确定性无法满足该需求。

Method: 1) 用Si5345A DSPLL时钟发生器（DCO相位控制）替代DAC+VCXO+倍频PLL的模拟调谐链；2) GTX收发器相位对齐与手动字节对齐修复降低重启不确定性；3) 引入级联全局控制架构，PC端PID由TD3强化学习自动整定，片上温度前馈校准达-0.76 ps/°C。

Result: 点对点同步精度：1米光纤3.38 ps，50 km光纤3.92 ps；12级级联末端：恒温6.66 ps，13°C温度波动下7.30 ps；TIE抖动<1 ps；重启不确定性2.82 ps（标准差）；4级级联稳定运行25小时；所有指标均优于CEPC 30 ps预算。

Conclusion: 所提出的增强型WR时钟同步系统在精度、稳定性和温度适应性上均显著优于标准方案，完全满足CEPC 30 ps同步精度要求，为百公里级大型科学设施的高精度时钟分发提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The Circular Electron Positron Collider (CEPC) distributes a reference clock distributed to 192 control nodes along its 100~km underground tunnel. The required synchronization precision is 30~ps (standard deviation). We present an enhanced White Rabbit (WR)-based clock synchronization system designed to meet this requirement. A noise-budget analysis of the standard WR slave loop identifies the analog actuation chain (DAC + VCXO + multiplier PLL) and restart-induced timing uncertainty as the dominant limitations. In our redesigned node, the DAC+VCXO chain is replaced by a Si5345A DSPLL clock generator with DCO-based phase control, removing the board-level analog tuning stage. GTX transceiver phase alignment and manual byte-alignment fixing reduce restart uncertainty from 88.8~ps to 12~ps peak-to-peak. For multi-node operation, we introduce a cascaded global-control architecture with PC-side PID auto-tuned by TD3 reinforcement learning, on-chip-temperature feed-forward calibrated to $-0.76\,\mathrm{ps}/^\circ\mathrm{C}$. The measured point-to-point synchronization precision is 3.38~ps over 1~m fiber and 3.92~ps over 50~km. In a 12-level cascade, the end-node precision reaches 6.66~ps at constant temperature and 7.30~ps under a 13$\,^\circ$C temperature swing. Synchronized-clock TIE jitter stays below 1~ps regardless of cascade depth. Restart uncertainty is 2.82~ps (std.\ dev.). A 4-level cascade operated stably for 25 hours of continuous monitoring. All measured metrics fall well within the CEPC 30~ps budget.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器收集的7.33 fb⁻¹数据，通过四种τ⁺衰变模式测量了Dₛ⁺→τ⁺ν_τ的分支比为(5.37±0.08_stat±0.06_syst)%，并给出了目前最精确的f_{Dₛ⁺}|V_{cs}|测量值及|V_{cs}|或f_{Dₛ⁺}的导出结果，同时验证了轻子普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量Dₛ⁺→τ⁺ν_τ分支比，进而提取CKM矩阵元|V_{cs}|和Dₛ⁺衰变常数f_{Dₛ⁺}，检验轻子普适性及标准模型预言。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在质心能量4.128-4.226 GeV收集的7.33 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻对撞数据，通过τ⁺的四种衰变模式(e⁺ν_eν̄_τ, μ⁺ν_μν̄_τ, π⁺ν̄_τ, π⁺π⁰ν̄_τ)进行联合测量，采用最大似然拟合提取信号。

Result: 分支比B(Dₛ⁺→τ⁺ν_τ)=(5.37±0.08±0.06)%；f_{Dₛ⁺}|V_{cs}|=(248.2±1.9±1.4±0.6±0.8) MeV；结合格点QCD的f_{Dₛ⁺}得|V_{cs}|=0.993±0.008±0.006±0.003±0.003；结合CKMfitter的|V_{cs}|得f_{Dₛ⁺}=(255.0±1.9±1.4±0.6±0.8) MeV；分支比之比B(τ⁺)/B(μ⁺)=10.04±0.29，与标准模型轻子普适性预言一致。

Conclusion: 获得了目前最精确的Dₛ⁺→τ⁺ν_τ分支比和f_{Dₛ⁺}|V_{cs}|测量值，结果与标准模型预期一致，进一步验证了轻子普适性。

Abstract: Using a dataset of 7.33 fb$^{-1}$ of $e^+ e^-$ annihilation data collected with the BESIII detector at center-of-mass energies from 4.128 to 4.226 GeV, we report an updated measurement of the branching fraction of $D_s^+ \to τ^+ ν_τ$ via four $τ^+$ decay modes: $τ^+ \to e^+ ν_e \barν_τ$, $μ^+ ν_μ \barν_τ$, $π^+\barν_τ$, and $π^+ π^{0} \barν_τ$. The branching fraction is determined to be $\mathcal{B}({D_s^+ \to τ^+ ν_τ}) = (5.37 \pm 0.08_{\rm stat} \pm 0.06_{\rm syst})$ %. The product of the modulus of the Cabibbo-Kobayashi-Maskawa matrix element $|V_{cs}|$ and the $D_s^+$ decay constant $f_{D_s^+}$ is measured to be $f_{D_s^+} |V_{cs}| = (248.2 \pm 1.9_{\rm stat} \pm 1.4_{\rm syst} \pm 0.6_{\rm input} \pm 0.8_{\rm EM})$ MeV. Both the branching fraction and the product $f_{D_s^+} |V_{cs}|$ are the most precise results yet obtained. Then, taking $f_{D_s^+}$ from lattice quantum chromodynamics calculations results in $|V_{cs}| = 0.993 \pm 0.008_{\rm stat} \pm 0.006_{\rm syst} \pm 0.003_{\rm input} \pm 0.003_{\rm EM}$. Conversely, one finds $f_{D_s^+} = (255.0 \pm 1.9_{\rm stat} \pm 1.4_{\rm syst} \pm 0.6_{\rm input} \pm 0.8_{\rm EM})$ MeV when taking $|V_{cs}|$ from the CKMfitter group as an input. Combining with the world average value of $D_s^+ \to μ^+ ν_μ$, the ratio of the branching fractions between $D_s^+ \to τ^+ ν_τ$ and $D_s^+ \to μ^+ ν_μ$ is estimated to be $10.04 \pm 0.29$, which is consistent with the Standard Model prediction of lepton flavor universality.

</details>
